# Killer560's Mod - Full Feature Descriptions

The detailed version of the feature list in the README. Keep this file up to date whenever a feature is added or changed - the public features Doc is generated from it.

> **Menu:** the mod menu (`/killer560`) has a header bar (the mod's version and build, a search box, and buttons for the Discord, the GitHub page and this document), a sidebar of categories - Home, Favorites, Dungeons, Mining, HUD, Chat, Items, Social, Cosmetics, Misc and Profiles - and a grid of feature cards. Every feature is its own card: its name, one line about it, and its on/off switch (a feature that is off has a dimmed card); only Secrets and Puzzle Solvers are grouped, as titled runs of cards inside Dungeons. Inside a category the cards are in alphabetical order by name (ignoring case), with the cheat build's red Cheat block after them in the same order; Secrets and Puzzle Solvers are placed by their own names and list their sections alphabetically too (Puzzle Solvers lists its solvers alphabetically). Inside a drawer the feature's on/off switch is the first row. A feature that is really several features side by side (Dungeon Queue, Dungeon Alerts, Dungeon ESP, Wither Dragons, Leap Menu, Croesus, Experiments, Inventory Theme, Hide Potion Effects, F7 Spots, P3 Nav, Maxor's Crystals, Thorn and, in the cheat build, Auto Blood Camp, Auto Terminals, Interactive Map and Sharp Shooter) has a switch for each, and its card and drawer switch are ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are; with everything off, pressing the card turns on only the first one, and with something on it turns every one off. Clicking a category also lists its sections under it in the sidebar (Legit, each group such as Secrets or Puzzle Solvers, and the red Cheat block); clicking one scrolls the grid to it and lights it, and otherwise the section in view is lit (scrolled to the end, the last one, however short); a cheat section is lit in red, with a red bar and a faint red band, and the other cheat rows are a dimmer red. Every scrollbar (the grid's, a drawer's, a page's) can be pressed and dragged. Every feature with a switch has a Toggle Keybind row at the very bottom of its drawer: none bound by default; click it, then press a key or mouse button (Escape cancels, Backspace or Delete clears it; every key row in the mod works this way and takes any mouse button, Mouse 4 and 5 included), and that key turns the feature on and off in game with no screen open, exactly as its card switch does, showing the new state above the hotbar. **Untested** Clicking a card opens that feature's settings in a drawer on the right, with its own switch, a star and a close button (the close button closes the drawer; Escape closes the menu, and the menu opens again on the same drawer, scrolled where you left it); every setting is a row with its label on the left and its switch, value or slider on the right, and Health and Mana Bars gets Basic and Advanced tabs for its Advanced section. Clicking a card opens that feature's settings in a drawer on the right, with its own switch, a star and a close button (the close button closes the drawer; Escape closes the menu, and the menu opens again on the same drawer, scrolled where you left it); every setting is a row with its label on the left and its switch, value or slider on the right, and Health and Mana Bars gets Basic and Advanced tabs for its Advanced section. The star on a card or in a drawer adds the feature to Favorites, which lists them as cards and, on Home, as a column of live switches; favorites are kept across restarts and in profiles. Ctrl F (or clicking the search box) searches every feature and setting, short names included ("etable" finds the Experimentation Table features): every word you type is looked for, in any order, in each card's name and description, every row of its drawer (closed sections and the rows a switched-off feature hides included) and each row's hover text; the start of a word is enough ("star" finds Starred), plurals and -ed/-ing endings match, and common other words count too ("esp" finds a highlight or hitbox, "effects" finds potion, "mob" finds monster, "keybind" finds hotkey, "hud" finds overlay, "colour" finds color). A feature's chat command finds its card too, with or without the "/" ("invsort" finds Inventory Sorter, "/pv" Profile Viewer). A card whose name or command is what you typed comes first, then cards whose name holds your words, then descriptions; settings follow, best first. The arrow keys pick a result, Enter opens its drawer scrolled to the setting, and a feature result's switch turns it on or off in place. Home shows the version, the update channel and the last update check, buttons for Edit HUD, Edit Bars, Check Updates, Discord, GitHub and this document, the Menu Theme row, and cards for its own settings (Profiles is in the sidebar, and Favorites has its own page). Menu Theme (on Home as a row of buttons whose Customize... opens its settings, and as the round button at the right of the header: left click the next, right click the previous) picks the menu's colors: Amber (the orange look, the default), Dark (neutral grays), Light (light panels and dark text), Ocean (teal on navy), Viridian (leaf green on a deep forest green, with a gradient panel background that fades from forest green to near black under a soft glow from the top center; its name is a placeholder and will be renamed) or Custom, where the accent, panel background, card background, text and on-switch colors are each set in the color picker; changing a color starts Custom from the preset you were on, and Reset puts that preset's colors back. Save as New Theme keeps the colors on screen as a saved theme (Custom 1, Custom 2...); each saved theme has Use, a name box to rename it and Delete (a second click confirms), and while one is in use, changing a color changes that theme. Saved themes are kept across restarts and in profiles. Everything in the menu follows the theme - header, sidebar, cards, drawer, switches, value pills, sliders, the search and the tooltips - and it is kept across restarts and in profiles. Animations (in Menu Theme's settings): Smooth (the default) eases the whole menu - it fades in and grows slightly as it opens and fades out as it closes, a card lights up and lifts a little under the mouse, switches slide, a pressed row or card flashes, the drawer slides in from the right and back out, a new category's cards rise into place, cards glide to their new places when the drawer narrows the grid, scrolling glides, the selected category's highlight slides down the sidebar, tooltips and search results fade in, a slider's knob glides to a clicked spot and a stepped value fades in from the side it was stepped to. Every animation lasts 100-200 ms and runs on time, not frames, so it looks the same at 30 FPS as at 300; a click always acts at once, on where things end up. Reduced keeps only the short color fades (nothing slides, lifts or grows) and Off changes everything at once. Kept across restarts and in profiles. Animation Speed (the slider under it) picks how fast they run: Slow, Normal, Fast (the default, the timing above) or Extra Fast. Every slider in the menu that stops on values (whole numbers, steps of 5 or 50, tenths, a list of choices) only ever rests on one: while dragging, the knob jumps from value to value (gliding between them under Smooth) and always sits exactly where the number it shows belongs; the arrow keys move a slider one value at a time. Click Sounds (Home) picks the sound every button click makes: Default (Minecraft's own click), one of six short original presets (Soft Tap, Tick, Pop, Wood Knock, Chime, Bleep; synthesized for the mod, CC0, listed in docs/SOUND-LICENSES.md) or a sound of your own (.ogg, .wav or .mp3, or anything ffmpeg can read, a video included). Left click on Click Sound steps to the next and plays it, right click goes back; Click Volume sets how loud, from 100% to 500% (above 100% the click plays in layers, so 300% is three copies together; it is also scaled by Minecraft's master volume), and Preview plays it once. The click plays on every screen, Minecraft's own included, and also when you click an item in a chest-style Skyblock menu (Default stays silent there, as in the game). To import, press Open Sound Folder and drop a short Ogg Vorbis file in (under 1 MB; it is listed the next time you step Click Sound), or copy a sound file's path and press Import From Clipboard: an .ogg, .wav, .mp3, .aiff or .au of at most ten seconds and 1 MB is copied into the folder as it is and chosen. Anything else (m4a, flac, a longer sound, or a video such as mp4, where only the audio is kept) is cut to its first three seconds and saved as an .ogg by ffmpeg, which must be installed (without it the import says so and nothing is copied); a file with no sound, or not a sound at all, is refused with a message saying which. Imported sounds ride in profiles like the GIFs do. Sound Effects (Misc since 2026-10-20, out of Home's mod settings; added 2026-10-18) replaces five game sounds, each its own row with a switch and a Sound choice (Default, a preset or an imported file, the same list as the click; all off by default): Arrow Hit (an arrow hitting a mob or a player), Attack Hit (every left click that hits a mob), Block Hit (every left click on a block, the sound while you mine it), Mob Death (a mob dying, not a player) and Block Break (a block breaking). The chosen sound plays where the game's one would, from the same place with the same pitch and volume. Its switch is on the card and drawer as the tab's master switch, and Open Sound Folder and Import From Clipboard work as they do for the click (the imported file is then picked in a row's Sound). Descriptions (Home) has its own switch first (Descriptions, on by default, also the card's switch: off hides every card description and every HUD editor hint, and the two rows below keep their values for when it is back on), then two switches, both on by default: Show Descriptions hides the one-line description under every feature card's name (hover a card for its longer text), and Show Editor Descriptions hides the hint line at the top of the HUD editor (the line saying why the editor is empty stays). Every card description is short enough for its card at the narrowest the grid lays one out, with no "..."; the hint line in the HUD editor is swapped for a shorter wording on a narrow window rather than cut. The mod's own screens follow it too, with the same beveled panels, wells and buttons: /profit and its trackers, Dungeon Profit (/croesus), Storage Search, the Run Log, the Inventory Sorter layouts, Custom Items, and the Bazaar and Auction House (their browsers and their reskins of Hypixel's menus). So do the Dungeon Sim's menu, Design a Map, its Filters, Sim Items and loading screen, Leap Order, the Interactive Map's frame and legend, the HUD editor, the scoreboard editor, Termism, and in the cheat build the AP3 and Breaker Aura config pickers and the AP3 and Auto Routes node editors. Each of those has a Theme setting in its feature's settings: Theme Default (follow the menu theme, the default) or Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or Custom for that screen alone; the Bazaar and Auction House also offer Inventory Theme, the flat look they had before, which follows the Inventory Theme setting. Every Theme setting lists the presets, Custom and every theme you have saved (read when you press it, so one saved a second ago is already there, under the name you gave it; deleting a saved theme puts the screens using it back on Default). The overlays that draw over the game with a look of their own have a Menu Theme setting too, which starts on Default - the overlay exactly as it always looked - and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian, Custom or a saved theme: the Storage Overlay (and the Profile Viewer panels that copy it), the Inventory Theme, the Inventory HUD, the Croesus chest profit panel and the Croesus and Experiments Start buttons, the custom Leap Menu (Croesus, Experiments and the Leap Menu each have ONE Theme setting in their tab that their screens and overlays all follow), the Item Browser and its recipe panels, and the Terminal Solver panels (which on a menu theme use its panel and accent instead of the Panel Background / Border colors). The Party Finder overlay, the Custom Scoreboard, the Health and Mana Bars, the class chips and the Themed Main Menu keep their own colors. In the cheat build every cheat-only feature sits in one red Cheat block at the bottom of its category, with cheat-only settings at the bottom of their drawer under a red header. The old New staging tab was removed on 2026-10-07. A setting that steps through several values (a mode, style, order, color preset) shows its value between a ‹ and a › arrow: a click on the right half of the value goes forward, on the left half back, and a right click on it goes back, wrapping round at both ends; a click on the setting's name does nothing. Every color setting opens the same color picker (a saturation/brightness square, a hue bar and an alpha slider), which also has a hex box showing the color that you can type into, Copy (puts the color on the clipboard as #RRGGBB, or #AARRGGBB when it is see-through, and says "Copied"), Paste (takes #RRGGBB, RRGGBB, #RGB, 0xRRGGBB or #AARRGGBB from the clipboard, spaces ignored; anything else gets a red "not a color" line and changes nothing; a color without alpha keeps the setting's own see-through amount), Ctrl+C and Ctrl+V for the same while the hex box is not being typed in, and a Recent row of the last eight colors picked, copied or pasted in any picker, newest first: click one to use it, so a color moves from one menu to another without the clipboard. Recent is kept across restarts and in profiles. The DVD text color uses this picker too. Every text box in the mod that holds a value (a setting, a name, a message, a hex color) saves it when Enter is pressed in it, exactly as its Set, Save or Add button does, and the box flashes green and lets go of the cursor; a box that had no such button now has a Set button beside it. In the color picker Enter applies the typed hex and closes, like Done; in a Party Finder listing form Enter keeps what is typed without listing the party. Search boxes, chat inputs and sign screens are unchanged.

**Chat**
- Translate — auto-translates your chat messages into another language before sending
- Auto Correct — fixes common typos before you send
- Command Auto Correct — optional: fixes typos in command names you type ("/wardorbe" → "/wardrobe") against the server's real command list, never touching arguments
- Chat Emotes — shortcuts that turn into fun chat emotes
- Click Translate — click a chat message to see it translated
- Copy Chat — with chat open, hold Shift or Ctrl and Left-Click a message to copy the whole message (every wrapped line of it), or Right-Click to copy only the line you clicked. Text is copied without color codes, a message stacked by Chat Hider is copied without its (xN), and a chat line confirms the copy. A plain click does exactly what it does without the mod
- Peek Chat — a key (or mouse button) that shows the whole chat history, as tall as the open chat, with no chat box and no menu: you keep walking, attacking, using items, switching hotbar slots and turning the camera while it is up. While it shows, the mouse wheel scrolls the chat instead of the hotbar, Page Up/Page Down scroll a page and Home goes back to the newest line. Peek Mode: Toggle Peek shows it on a press and hides it on the next press (or Escape, which then does not open the pause menu); Peek Mode: Hold To Peek shows it only while you hold the key. Opening any menu hides it. Links and hover text need a mouse cursor, so click them in the real chat. Only shows with no menu open. Off and unbound until you set it in Chat > Peek Chat
- Chat Tabs — (to-do Doc, 2026-11-01; off by default) a row of tabs just above the chat input while chat is open: All, Party, Guild, Officer, Co-op and Messages. Click one and the chat shows only that channel's messages (by Hypixel's prefixes: "Party > ", "Guild > ", "Officer > ", "Co-op > ", and "From"/"To" private messages); All shows everything. With Switch Channel (on) the click also puts you in that chat: /chat all, party, guild, officer or coop. Private messages have no channel of their own, so with Messages selected a plain line you type goes as /r (to whoever last messaged you); Mod Chat, when you are in it, keeps your lines. Typing /chat p (or any of Hypixel's channel names) yourself selects the matching tab. The filter applies while the chat is open; Filter Closed Chat (off) keeps it after you close it. It uses Minecraft's own chat filter and Fabric's screen events, so other chat mods (Lunar's chat mod, chat history mods) keep working beside it. In Chat
- Image Preview — with chat open, hover a link to a picture and the picture appears beside the cursor, scaled to fit **Max Preview Size** (64-400, default 160). Works on imgur in every form (i.imgur.com files, imgur.com/&lt;id&gt; pages, imgur.com/a/ albums and /gallery/ posts, with or without https://), Discord's cdn.discordapp.com and media.discordapp.net, and any direct .png/.jpg/.jpeg/.gif/.webp/.bmp link; a link wrapped over two chat rows counts, and so does one the server did not make clickable. An imgur album shows "1/N" and the mouse wheel steps through it (if imgur's API stops answering, an album or gallery link still shows its cover picture, read from the page itself); animated GIFs play (**Play GIFs**). A middle click opens a bigger viewer (arrow keys or the wheel step an album; Escape or a click goes back to chat). A left click is untouched: vanilla still asks before opening the link. **Hosts: Trusted Hosts Only** (default) fetches only imgur and Discord; **Any Host** fetches from any site and also reads a page's og:image, which tells that site your IP address when you hover. Never fetched: local network addresses, anything but http/https, files over 10 MB, pictures over 8192 px a side or 40 million pixels. No cookies or account data are sent. Pictures are kept on disk up to **Cache Size** (10-1000 MB, default 100; least recently shown deleted first), so hovering the same link again sends nothing. On by default. Chat > Image Preview
- Chat History — the **Chat History** switch (also on its card) keeps more chat scrollable than vanilla; OFF is vanilla's last 100 chat messages. While it is ON, **Length** picks **Limited** (a **History Limit** slider from 200 to 10000 messages in steps of 200, default 1000) or **Unlimited** (the whole session stays in chat and can be scrolled back to; more memory the longer you play), and the Length you chose comes back when you turn it off and on again. Off by default. It only ever raises the cap, so another chat-history mod's larger limit is kept (works alongside Skysoft, Unlimited Chat History and More Chat History). Chat > Chat History
- Command Prefixes — **/killer560**, **/k**, **/k5** and **/k560** each have a switch and each opens the mod menu and takes every `/killer560` subcommand (`/k bugreport`, `/k leaporder`); only /killer560 is on at first, and a name another mod already uses is skipped (a WARN in the log). Switching a name takes effect at once; its tab completion for a name switched on later is added at the next join. **Alternate Prefix** (off by default) lets a chat line start with a character you choose instead of the slash: pick one of `.` `,` `!` `-` `#` or type any one character that is not a space or a slash, and `.pv Bob` runs `/pv Bob`. Only a first word that is a client-side command counts: `.hello`, `. lol` and `...` go out as normal chat, and nothing run this way is sent to the server. Chat > Command Prefixes
- Chat Hider — one switch over every chat tidier and hider (it replaced Hide Chat Messages and Chat Tidy on 2026-10-08; settings carry over). **Stack Duplicate Messages**: when a line arrives that repeats one of the last 50 messages in chat from the last 60 seconds (same text, same colors and formatting, same click action), the earlier copy is removed and one line ending in a gray (x2), then (x3) and so on, is added at the bottom, keeping the first copy's click and hover. Other messages in between do not stop it; the window keeps an old line from being pulled down from far up the chat. Blank lines and separator lines (----- or ▬▬▬) never stack, so back-to-back message boxes keep their spacing; signed player chat is left alone. **Hide Damage Messages**: hides Hypixel's combat spam, both families with one switch: "Your <ability> hit N enemy/enemies for N damage." (Implosion, Guided Sheep, Spirit Sceptre, Witherborn, Explosive Shot, Spirit Pet, Thunderstorm, Molten Wave and any other ability with that shape), and the hits you take: "A Crypt Wither Skull / Spirit Sheep / Chicken Mine exploded, hitting you for N damage.", "<boss, trap or mob> hit you for N (true) damage" (Maxor's Frenzy, Storm's Giga Lightning, The Arrow Trap, Wither TNT, Bonzo's Balloon...), "... struck you for N damage" (Stormy mobs, Spirit Chicken lightning), "The Lost/Frozen Adventurer used <ability> on you!" and "Your bone plating reduced the damage you took by N!". Only whole server lines are hidden: the same words typed by a player in any chat, or with anything else on the line, always show. Hiding is display-only: every other feature still reads the line, and the game log still records it. Heal lines (Wish, tether, fairy) are not touched. **Hide Useless Messages** ("There are blocks in the way!", "Profile ID: ..."), **Hide Discord Warnings**, **Hide Microsoft Warnings**, **Hide Empty Chat Messages**, **Hide Actionbar** and **Hide Non-Rank Invites** (party invites from players without a rank), **Hide Ban Announcements** (the Watchdog broadcast: "Watchdog has banned N players in the last 7 days." and "Staff have banned an additional N"). **Hide Mining Reminders** and **Hide Sack Messages** (both on by default, chattidy-1104; display time, so mining features still read the lines): the "You need a tool with a Breaking Power of N to mine X! Speak to Fragilis ..." reminder (one line or two) and "You have unspent Tokens of the Mountain! Use /hotm to assign them!", "SERENDIPITOUS! Your Crystal Serendipity attribute doubled your rewards!", "COMPACT! You found an Enchanted Hard Stone!" (any item name; hidden before the stacking, so no "(x8)"), and the "[Sacks] +N items. (Last Ns.)" / "-N items" / "+N items, -N items" lines; a player typing the same text is never hidden. **Hide Already Looted Chests** (on by default): the server line "This chest has already been looted." (display time, whole line only). The **Dungeons** section (2026-10-25) hides dungeon chat, each family its own switch, only while you are in a dungeon: **Hide Boss Dialogue** (every "[BOSS] ..." line of the dungeon bosses: The Watcher, Bonzo, Scarf, The Professor, Thorn, Livid and F5's fake Livids, Sadan, Maxor, Storm, Goldor, Necron, the Wither King; the Watcher's "You have proven yourself. You may pass." stays), **Hide Mort** ("[NPC] Mort: ..."), **Hide Blessings** ("DUNGEON BUFF! ... found a Blessing of ...", "A Blessing of ... was found/picked up!", "... has obtained Blessing of ...!" and the indented "Granted you ..." stat lines under them), **Hide Others' Pickups** (another player's "... has obtained Wither Key!", Blood Key, Superboom TNT, Revive Stone, Premium Flesh and any other "has obtained" line, and "A Wither/Blood Key was picked up!"; your own pickups stay), **Hide Ability Spam** ("This ability is on cooldown for Ns.", "<ability> is now available!", "... is ready to use! Press DROP to activate it!", "Used <ability>!", the ULTIMATE-available line, Creeper Veil on/off, orb pickups and tethers formed; heals stay) and **Hide Dungeon Breaker Spam** ("A mystical force prevents you from digging that block!", "... digging there!", "... digging in this room!" and "You don't have enough charges to break this block right now!"). Every line must be the whole server line, so a player typing the same words in any chat is never hidden Dungeon pack additions (featF-1108, both builds, off by default, dungeons only): **Compact Melody Messages** hides the in-progress party Melody callouts ("Melody 1/3 - 33%", "Melody 67%") and keeps the finished one; **Mute Melody Messages** hides every one. The Team Melody HUD still reads them (they are hidden at display time).
- Auto Meow — replies with a random cat noise ("purr~", "*slow blink*", ...) when another player says meow, purr, nya or mew, and replies in the same chat the message came from: party chat with /pc, guild with /gc, officer with /oc, co-op with /cc, a private message with /msg back to that player, all chat with /ac, and Mod Chat in Mod Chat (never through Hypixel). The channel is read from the whole chat line, so someone typing "Party > ..." in all chat is answered in all chat. Your own messages never trigger it, and it waits 3 seconds after each reply. Optionally plays a cat sound with its own volume
- Cringe — random one-liner messages for fun; `/cringe <chat>` picks the chat (pc, gc, oc, cc, ac, r, or kc for Mod Chat). Its first switch turns the whole feature on or off (on by default; it is also the switch on its menu card): off, `/cringe` and Leap Message's cringe line send nothing
- Spotify Lyrics — posts the lyrics of whatever you're playing on Spotify into chat, synced to the song. **No setup and nothing to configure**: open the Spotify desktop app and play something. It reads the app on your own PC directly — no account, no API key, no website, no login, and no source to pick. Only the artist and title ever leave the machine, to lrclib.net for the lyrics. There is no timing offset either: the app reports a track change instantly and the lyric clock stops while you are paused. Scrubbing back or forward, pausing, and starting the game part-way through a song are followed too: the mod reads Spotify's real position, length, play/pause state and cover from Windows' media controls (a small hidden PowerShell reader that runs only while one of the two switches is on and closes with the game), and snaps the lyric clock to Spotify's position whenever they differ by more than a second. Without that reader the clock is a stopwatch from the track change and a scrub is not seen. Lyrics are matched to the recording Spotify is playing: every lrclib listing is scored, a listing is the same song when its title matches Spotify's once both are normalized ("Song - X Remix" and "Song (X Remix)" alike; "(feat. ...)", "Remastered 2011", Explicit/Clean dropped; "Sped Up", "Live" and remix names kept apart) and its artists include Spotify's, and among those within two seconds of Spotify's length the one whose line times agree with the other listings wins (a lone hand sync of the exact length can be a second off), the closest length breaking a tie; when none is that close lrclib's /api/get is asked with the length (and the album when Windows gives one, and the " (X)" spelling of a " - X" title), and failing that the closest same-song listing is shown with "other version" beside the time (its timing may be off); with no listing of the song at all the HUD says the lyrics found are for another version. Every candidate and why one won is logged ("Lyrics versions for ..."), and lrclib's answer is kept per track for the session. While an ad plays it says you are listening to an ad rather than leaving the previous song's last line up. **Music Source** (2026-11-01, default Music Players) picks what is followed through Windows' media controls: Music Players, any player that tells Windows what it plays (Apple Music, iTunes, Tidal, Deezer...) and a browser tab that names an album, which music sites do (Spotify's web player, YouTube Music, Apple Music on the web) and plain videos do not; or Anything Playing. A playing Spotify app always comes first, then whatever is playing; the lyrics, HUD, F5 lines, sharing and the transport controls all follow the chosen player (volume stays the Spotify app's). **Display Censor** (All / Minimal / None, default Minimal) is what the HUD, your F5 lines and other players' shared lines star out: All stars every swear word, Minimal only slurs, None nothing; chat lyrics keep their own Profanity Filter with the same three levels (Minimal is slurs only since 2026-11-01). The tab has two switches at the top, one per feature, and the card is on while either is (or while a Song Tag switch below is). **Send Lyrics To Chat** (off by default; it was the "Mod Enabled" row, and keeps its saved value) turns chat posting on or off; under the Chat Lyrics header pick the chat it posts to (party, all, guild, plain, or Mod Chat), full lyrics or just the song title, and a profanity filter. A line sent to chat never starts with a slash, carries no section signs or control characters, and is cut to fit the 256-character chat limit with its prefix. Windows only. **Spotify HUD** (the second switch, off by default; it works with Send Lyrics To Chat off): a panel in the mod menu's own beveled frame and theme colors, moved and scaled in Edit HUD, showing the cover, the song title and artist, elapsed and total time with a progress bar in the accent color, and the lyric line being sung with the current word in bright accent on a tinted chip, the words already sung in white and the rest dimmed. While paused the line is held and nothing moves. The cover and the total time are Spotify's own when the media reader runs (otherwise a note and lrclib's length). The highlighted word is an estimate unless the lyrics have per-word timing (rare on lrclib): the line's time is shared out by each word's syllables plus a little per letter, no word over 0.75 s, so a held note or a pause inside a line still puts it ahead or behind. **HUD Lyrics** (off by default), **Word Highlight**, **Hide When Paused** and **Smooth Lyric Scroll** (on by default: when a line ends the rows ease up over 200 ms and the finished line slides out and fades, instead of jumping) toggle the rest. Whatever the settings, the HUD and your F5 lines go away once a song has been paused for over a minute, and come back when it plays. The cover shows for every song (2026-11-01: it never had, because the reader could not open Windows' thumbnail stream from PowerShell; it is now a square from the middle of the picture, so a browser's wide thumbnail is not squashed). The lyric clock re-syncs to Spotify whenever Spotify takes a new reading (every few seconds while playing, and at once on a seek, pause or resume): the position is Windows' reading carried forward from the time Spotify took it. A track start, a seek, a pause and a resume are taken from Spotify's reading at once; slow drift is corrected only when the median of the last three readings is over 40 ms off, and eased in by at most 30 ms a poll, so the lit word never jumps back. A correction over 300 ms is written to the log, and once a minute, if Spotify's readings disagreed by over 40 ms, a "Spotify clock check" line says by how much. **Word-Timed Lyrics** (on by default): once Spotify's length is known, Kugou's public lyrics service (no account, no key; only the artist, title and length are sent, as to lrclib) is asked once per track for KRC lyrics, which carry a start for every word; a version within two seconds of Spotify's length, with the same normalized title and artist, is used instead of lrclib's, and every word lights at its own time. Kugou's lyrics keyword search lists only its better-known songs, so a song it misses is found by Kugou's song search and its lyrics by the song's hash. When lrclib also has the recording, Kugou's words are moved onto lrclib's agreed line times (recorded against the sound, those sat closer to the vocals). The lrclib search is asked again when lrclib is busy (HTTP 429), then by title alone and by a free-text search when the artist search misses; a YouTube or browser video is looked up by its cleaned names (no "(Official Video)", "- Topic" or "VEVO", no bare "ft. X", "Lyric Video" or "Official Audio" tail, an "Artist - Song" title split); a song every search answered with no synced lyrics is remembered for the session, so replaying it does not ask lrclib again (an unreachable lrclib is not remembered). A clock drift two readings agree is over 250 ms is set at once instead of eased. Measured 2026-10-27: Kugou had word-timed lyrics within 2 s for 40 of 50 popular songs; lrclib had word times for none of them. Songs Kugou does not have (his High Forever remix among them) use lrclib's line times, where the lit word inside a line is an estimate (the line's time shared out by syllables, at most 0.75 s a word), measured at a median 354 ms and a 90th percentile of 1.4 s from the real word start. The log says which source each song got ("Word times for ..."). Listening to Spotify's audio to place the words was measured and does not help (word starts line up with detectable onsets in a full mix no better than chance; speech recognition finds 2-4 % of sung words), so it is not in the mod. With chat open, the mouse over the Spotify HUD shows a row of buttons under it (Previous, Rewind, Play/Pause, Skip Ahead, Next, Volume Down, the volume in its own box, Volume Up) that call the same controls; Play/Pause is the wide one in the middle, filled with the accent color and drawn as a triangle or two bars, so it is not mistaken for a skip. **Always Show Controls** (off by default) draws the row all the time and puts it inside the HUD's Edit HUD box; clicking still needs chat open. A click on the HUD never reaches chat, a control that cannot work is dimmed with the reason under the row, and with chat closed the HUD is display-only. **Controls** (Windows, no login and no Premium): the transport buttons left the tab on 2026-11-01 (they are on the HUD and the keys); Rewind and Skip Ahead jump by the Rewind/Skip Step (5, 10, 15 or 30 s, default 10). The **Spotify Volume** slider moves Spotify's own in-app volume slider through Windows UI Automation when the Spotify window can be read, and otherwise Spotify's slider in the Windows volume mixer (the log says which); it needs Spotify to have played sound once. Only the newest value of a dragged slider is sent and the reader drops any older volume command still waiting, so the volume follows the slider at once in both directions (before, every 1 % step ran in turn and a long drag down lagged seconds behind). They go to Spotify through Windows' media controls, through the same hidden reader, which runs while a control key is bound and for two minutes after a control or the tab was used. A control Spotify does not allow at that moment (an ad, Spotify closed) is grayed out and its hover says why. Under **Control Keys** each control, plus Volume Up and Volume Down (by the Volume Key Step, 2, 5 or 10 %, default 5), can have a key: all unbound by default, Backspace unbinds; they work in game with no screen open, never while typing or in a menu.
- Song Tag — in the Spotify Lyrics tab. **Share My Song** shows the song you play on Spotify as its own gray line above your name, to the other mod users in your lobby. While Share My Song or Show Others' Songs is on the mod keeps its relay connected in your lobby's room (Mod Chat's room when Mod Chat is on, but the lobby's while Mod Chat is set to Party and you are in no party, where Mod Chat then sends nothing; your party's in a dungeon); until 2026-11-01 nothing connected the relay for Song Tag, so with Mod Chat off and no party nothing was ever shared. **Share What** (Song Name / Lyrics / Song Name + Lyrics, default Song Name) picks what you send; with lyrics, the line you are on is sent as it changes (at most once a second), and **Share Current Word** (on by default) adds when each word starts, so others see the word you are on lit on their own clock. Supporters only (killer560, 2026-10-26): for anyone else the row is grayed out with "Supporters only" as its hover, and nothing is ever sent from a non-supporter, even with an old config file that has it on; until the supporter list has loaded nothing is sent, and it goes out once it has. It is sent only while Show Me As A Mod User is on; it travels over the mod's relay, never through Hypixel. You see your own line above your name in F5 whenever it is on and a song plays, even when it is not reaching anyone (no relay room, Show Me As A Mod User off), and the row's hover then says why. Paused, an ad or nothing playing hides the line. **Show Others' Songs** draws other supporters' songs within 16 blocks, and the lyric line they share above it with the word they are on lit, in your F5 colors; your Display Censor stars their words and anything with a slur is hidden; anyone can turn it on. You see every mod user in your lobby who shares, partied or not (killer560, 2026-11-06): while Mod Chat's Party mode (its default) keeps the relay in your party room, a second link carries only the songs in your lobby room (never in a dungeon, whose instance is only your party); the row's hover says what is connected right now. Until 2026-11-05 a Hypixel warp made the game forget its party, so two players in one party could end up in different rooms and see nothing of each other's songs. **My Lyrics In F5** (everyone, not a supporter perk) shows the lyric line being sung above your own head in third person, above the song line when there is one. Only you see it and nothing is sent. **Highlight Word In F5** (on by default) lights the word being sung in that line exactly as the HUD's Word Highlight does: the same word at the same moment (one shared timing and clock), sung words bright, the word being sung in the bright accent and underlined, the rest dimmed, in the menu theme's colors; held without a highlight while paused. **Song In F5** (everyone, off by default) shows the song's title and artist above your own head in F5, nothing sent. **F5 Colors**: Menu Theme (the HUD's colors) or Custom, with an **F5 Text Color** for the whole line and an **F5 Highlight Color** for the word being sung (words still to come a shade darker). Your own name is shown under these lines in F5 even with My Name in F5 off, since they hang over it. All the F5 rows (with My Name in F5) are one section, Lyrics In F5, in the Spotify Lyrics tab, and the same rows are in Player Cosmetics under Spotify In F5: both places change the same settings. Share My Song is off by default; Show Others' Songs is on by default (killer560, 2026-11-06), like other players' names and skins, and a saved Off stays off.
- Mod Chat — a genuinely private chat channel between mod users. Messages travel over the mod's own relay server rather than Hypixel's party or guild chat, so players who don't run the mod never see them. `/kc <message>` sends one line (like `/pc`, and Translate, Auto Correct and Chat Emotes apply to it the same way), and `/chat k` switches your typed chat into Mod Chat (like `/chat p`) until you pick another channel with `/chat a`, `/chat p`, `/chat g` and so on, which still go to Hypixel unchanged; leaving the server switches it back too. Received messages show in your chat by default (Log To Chat). The tab shows who else from your party or lobby is connected. Falls back to nothing if the relay is unreachable — it will never quietly post your message in public chat
- Voice To Text — speak and the transcription is sent to chat, by Push To Talk (hold a key, release to send) or Open Mic (always listening; what you said is sent once you stop talking for the Silence Before Send time, 1.0 s by default, 0.3 to 3.0 s). Open Mic tells speech from room noise by how far it rises over the quietest moment of the last few seconds, so a fan or a hissing headset does not hold it open; a blip shorter than about a third of a second, an empty result, or one that is only "uh"/"um"/"the" is not sent, and it keeps listening while the last sentence is transcribed. Fully offline (Vosk); on first use it downloads the Vosk engine (~26MB, checksum-verified) and a small speech model (~40MB) into the config folder, so neither is in the mod jar. Pick which microphone it records from, and which chat it goes to: party, all chat, guild, plain, or Mod Chat. **Untested with a real microphone** While the speech engine or model downloads (first use only) the line above the hotbar shows the percent done, e.g. "[Voice] Downloading speech model: 37%". **Show Status On HUD** (on by default) turns off every status line above the hotbar - Listening, Transcribing, the download percent, the sent line; a failure (no microphone, set-up failed) still shows.

- Auto Inventory Sorter — save the exact slot each item should live in as a named layout, then put your inventory back into it with a key or `/invsort apply <name>`. `/invsort`, Open Layouts in the tab, or the Open Menu Key opens the layouts menu, which sizes itself to your window (bigger slots and rows on a normal screen, each item scaled up to fill and centered in its slot, with its Item Rarity box behind it when Item Rarity Backgrounds is on): it shows a copy of your inventory, you move items around in it (click to pick up and click to drop, or drag), type a name and Save. A loaded layout's grid shows only its own items, so under the grid a Your Inventory list holds every other item in your real inventory that the grid is not showing: click one (or drag it) and click a slot to put it there, drop a grid item on the list or press Backspace over a slot to empty it. This only draws: nothing is clicked in your real inventory. The bottom-right hotbar slot (the Skyblock Menu, map or arrows, which can never be removed) is locked: the sorter never moves, fetches into or puts away what is in it, a layout never holds it (older layouts' entry for it is ignored), and the editor shows it as Lock. Right-click a slot (or press Delete over it) to make it Free: it is left out of the layout, so whatever is in that slot when the layout is applied is never taken for another slot, never moved and never put away (right-click it again, or Reset, to put it back; layouts saved before Free existed have none). The saved layouts are listed on the right, taking the whole height of the menu: each row shows the layout's name, an Edit button at its right (it loads the layout into the editor on the left) and its hotbar as items (the very items from your inventory, or a red-tinted icon for one you do not have with you; hover one for its tooltip), and the rows and their items grow with the window; left click one to swap to it at once (the menu closes and it sorts), right click one, or press its Edit button, to load it into the grid to edit, Rename or Delete it, drag one up or down to reorder the list (kept across restarts), and click the key cell at the right of a layout to set the key (or mouse button) that applies it (Backspace clears it). Storage mode, per layout (right click it, then the two buttons under the list): Fetch From Storage (on for every new layout) takes the layout's missing items out of your Ender Chest pages and backpacks first, opening each page the Storage Overlay has seen them in and shift-clicking them out, and Put Others In opens your /storage so you can open the Ender Chest page or backpack to use (the menu comes back with it set; Escape changes nothing; right-click the button for none), and every item the layout does not use is shift-clicked into that page first (never an item Item Protect protects or Locks In Place, never the SkyBlock Menu; a full page leaves the rest in your inventory). Hypixel Loadout, per layout (a third button under the list, labeled "Loadout: 3" or "Loadout: None"; it opens a picker that is a copy of your /loadouts pages: whenever you have a page open (any way), the mod remembers its items per account, also across restarts, and the picker draws them as Hypixel does, with Hypixel's tooltips on hover, each loadout's number on its slot the layout's loadout outlined and the loadout you are wearing marked green (Hypixel's equipped one, the loadout without "Left-click to equip!"); the page's Next Page and Previous Page arrows turn the picker's page, and Hypixel's Close button is left out; a click on a loadout's slot sets the layout's loadout, saves it and returns to the layouts menu, and a None button clears it. A page you have not opened yet shows plain number buttons (1-12 on page 1, 13-24 on page 2, and so on) and says "Open /loadouts once to see your loadouts here", with < and > to turn the page. The picker is the mod's own and sends nothing to the server), makes applying the layout (menu click, its key or `/invsort apply <name>`) select that loadout first, the way Loadout Keybinds' Open, Swap, Close does (`/loadouts`, a click on that loadout, the menu closed), and sort afterwards, so the sort and any storage visit work from the inventory the loadout leaves; a loadout on a later page is reached through /loadouts' Next Page arrow ("Loadout 14 isn't there" if /loadouts has fewer pages), is stored in the layout's file and survives a rename, and if the menu does not open or the click does not go through the sort does not run. Escape or the Emergency Cancel Key stops it mid-swap. Applying moves each item with the fewest clicks there are: number-key swaps around a hotbar slot wherever one is in the move, the cursor carried round the rest, a displaced item put back where the moved one came from; the proof and the exhaustive check are in docs/INVSORT-OPTIMALITY.md. While it sorts, the inventory takes no clicks or keys from you at all (Escape or the inventory key closes it, which stops the sort), and your movement and sneak keys are held off; no click goes out until the server has you standing still. Ticks Between Moves sets the clicks' pace and Random Extra Ticks adds up to that many more at random. Applying opens your inventory, makes the clicks and closes it again, the same open, click, close a player makes by hand. Instant (0-tick), at the bottom of the tab under its own cheat header and off by default, sends every click of a sort in one tick without opening the inventory at all; it is untested on Hypixel, where clicks with no inventory open have drawn a limbo ("Bad client inventory move"). It stops on its own if your inventory changes under it, if the inventory closes, or if another menu is open, and it leaves items Item Protect has Locked In Place where they are. Layouts are one file each so you can hand one to a friend. Items are matched as the very item you saved: by its Skyblock uuid, or for an item without one by everything Hypixel stores on it (enchantments, runes, attributes), so with two runebooks or two enchanted books the layout's own one is the one it sorts, keeps and fetches from storage; a layout saved before 2026-11-03 matches any copy of the item's Skyblock id until you save it again; applying such a layout says in chat how many of its slots are saved that way, so you know to save it again. If you start a sort that uses storage (Fetch From Storage on, or a Put Others In page) within 4 seconds of a warp or server change, when Hypixel will not yet open backpacks or the Ender Chest, it shows "Waiting for the lobby: sorting in 2.6s" above the hotbar, counting down, and then runs by itself; pressing the key again does not queue a second one, and Escape, the Emergency Cancel Key or opening a menu cancels it (leaving the lobby again restarts the wait). With Put Others In set to None (hover the button for what it does) the items that are not in the layout stay in your inventory, and the sort says how many were left. With a Put Others In page set, everything the layout does not use, from the hotbar or the main inventory, is put into that page, so only the layout's items (and the locked last hotbar slot) remain; if the page fills up, the sort says exactly how many items could not be put away, and items Item Protect or Lock In Place protect stay and are counted too. `/invsort list` and `/invsort stop` are there too. **Dump Into Chest** (added 2026-11-04): with a chest, large chest, barrel, Ender Chest page or backpack open, a Dump Into Chest button above the screen's top right corner (with the Storage Overlay up it sits in the overlay's side buttons, directly below Scan All) (or the Dump Key, unset by default; Dump Button On Chests turns the button off) shift-clicks every item of your inventory into it, one click per paced tick at the sorter's Ticks Between Moves and Random Extra Ticks. It never touches the locked bottom-right hotbar slot, never the SkyBlock Menu, and dumps the hotbar too while Dump Hotbar is on (on by default; a saved OFF from before 2026-11-05 was turned on once). Each slot is clicked once; an item that fits nowhere is skipped and the run ends when everything was tried or the chest is full ("Dumped N stacks into the chest (the chest is full)"). Escape, the Emergency Cancel Key or closing the chest stops it. Other Hypixel menus (shops, the SkyBlock Menu...) never show the button, because a shift-click there would buy or sell. **Cheat build only**
- Price Tooltip — adds price lines to any Skyblock item's tooltip, in the style other Skyblock mods use (yellow labels, aqua values): NPC Sell Price, Bazaar Buy/Sell (Bazaar items), Lowest BIN Price, 1 or 3 Day Avg. Price, Est. Item Value, Obtained (the date the item was created, in red) and Museum (Combat): Donated / Not Donated. Each line has its own switch under the feature switch, which is off by default. A stack shows the whole stack's price and the unit price on every line, e.g. 108,832.0 Coins (64x 1,700.5 each). Est. Item Value is the item's own price plus what was applied to it - Recombobulator, Hot and Fuming Potato Books, enchantments (priced as Bazaar books), dungeon stars (their essence and items) and master stars, gemstones and the cost of each unlocked slot, Art of War and Peace, scrolls, enrichment, Polarvoid, Wood Singularity, Farming for Dummies, Jalapeno, Mana Disintegrator, Transmission Tuners, Wet Books, Etherwarp, drill and rod parts, dye, skin, rune, and a pet's item and skin; reforges are not counted. A pet is valued by its real level and XP: the lowest BIN of the same pet with no item or skin listed within 3 levels in the last Auction House scan, else the level 1 price moved toward the level 100 price by its share of the XP to level 100 (level 100 toward 200 for the dragons that go to 200), else whichever of those prices exists; the breakdown's Base line shows the level, and Networth takes off a candied pet's discount (35%, at most 5M at level 100 or 2.5M below, not for dragons or Scatha); the Profile Viewer's pet list values each pet the same way. Value Mode Full Price (the default) counts each part at its market price, Networth weighs applied upgrades the way networth calculators do (a Recombobulator counts 80%, an Art of War 60%); every part is priced at what buying it costs now, the Bazaar Buy (instant-buy) price, else the lowest BIN, and the Profile Viewer networth uses the same mode; Value Breakdown lists every part while Shift is held (or always, or never). Number Format Exact (5,900,000.0) or Short (5.9M). Museum status is read for your selected profile from the API (your Profile Viewer key if set, else the keyless backend), every 10 minutes. Prices come from Hypixel (item list and Bazaar) and Skyblocker's public price lists (lowest BIN and averages, with Coflnet as a fallback), refetched every 1-30 minutes (Refresh Every, default 5) only while you are using the tooltips, kept on disk so they show at once after a restart, and never fetched on the game's render thread. The Storage Overlay, Storage Search, Bazaar and Profile Viewer item tooltips carry these lines too (they are added where those screens draw the tooltip, once). Replaces the Item Browser's old Show Item Value line, whose setting it inherits once

**Cosmetics**

- Player Cosmetics (Cosmetics category) — one tab for how players look on your screen: Supporter Names and Supporter Sizes switches for other supporters' shared looks (each on its own; both off hides everything), Share if Supporter (one toggle that shares your display name, with its fade or letter colors exactly as you see it, and your own size once your account is linked as a supporter; players on an older version of the mod see the name in its first color), Share Ribbon Trail and Show Others' Trails (both on by default, also on the Ribbon Trail tab: share your Ribbon Trail with other mod users - supporters only, grayed out with the reason otherwise; the squares Trail is never shared - and draw the ribbons other supporters share), and three dropdowns. Custom Names is the in-game name changer: your display name with a color, a smooth per-letter fade between two colors, or a color of its own for each letter, Name Animation for a faded or letter-colored name (Off by default; Wave flows the colors along the name to the right in a smooth loop, Pulse dims and brightens the whole name; both are left click forward, right click back, with an Animation Speed from 0.25x to 3x - shared with the name, so other players on this version of the mod see it animate on your nametag, in chat and in the tab list, and older versions show it still), a Rank row for the tag in front of your name (Keep Original, None, VIP, VIP+, MVP, MVP+, MVP++ or Custom, left click forward, right click back; Rank Color steps the tag's color through the 16 chat colors, and Custom takes your own text such as KING++ with its own color for the + signs - it replaces a Hypixel rank tag directly in front of your name in chat, the tab list and everywhere else the name shows, and adds nothing where there was no tag), a live preview of the name as it will be painted (animated too), a reset back to your real name, renames for other players, and Per-Profile Names (off by default: your custom names are one set shared by every profile, kept outside the profiles, so loading or exporting a profile never changes them; on, they are saved inside each profile like any other setting, and the switch itself is the same in every profile). A name inside a command is never restyled: typing /killer560, a command with your name as its argument, and the chat box's command suggestions all show the name as it is; My Name in F5 (off by default) shows your own nametag above you in both third-person views, never in first person. Supporters' shared names show on nametags, in chat and in the tab list, Skyblock's text rows included, and every player's nametag follows their size so it stays just above the head from 0.05x to 2x. Player Size has My Size and Others' Size sliders from 0.05x to 2x, each with its own reset, plus your model's width/height/thickness. Play as Mob draws your player as any in-game mob: My Mob (Player by default; a button that opens a menu listing every living mob, Player first, with a search box that narrows the list as you type, and clicking a mob picks it; the bosses and giants - ender dragon, wither, giant, elder guardian, warden - are not on it) shows the mob in your own F5 view, walking, looking and swinging the way you do, with your nametag over its head, and My Size and Width/Height/Thickness apply to it as they do to your player; with Share if Supporter on and your account linked as a supporter, other players with the mod see you as that mob too (older versions see the player). Show Others' Mobs (on by default) draws other supporters as the mob they share, except inside a dungeon, where everyone always shows as a player so a teammate is never mistaken for an enemy. It is a picture only: your hitbox and everything the mod's features read stay a player. Held Item is the held-item transform: its first row is the switch with an **Edit** button beside it, which opens every slider in one column over the game, against the screen edge away from the hand holding the item (left for a right-handed player), with the world neither blurred nor paused, so the item can be watched while it is set (the wheel scrolls the column). Besides scale, offsets and rotations per hand and the No Swing / No Equip / No Hand Sway switches it has **Swing Speed** (0.05x-4x) and **Swing Range** (0x-3x, default 1x: how far the item travels forward on each hit). On Skyblock, and anywhere while Held Item is on, the held item never sinks from the attack cooldown while you click (Hypixel has none), so spamming a sword keeps it at full height; switching items still lowers and raises it. Cape (off by default) is a switch; while it is on, **Design** wears one of the built-in capes (each carries the mod logo in its colors) or Make Your Own, a pattern and two colors from its designer, and the design you chose comes back when you turn it off and on again. Only your own player shows it. The name changer never recolors or renames anything inside this mod's own menus. Everything here is local rendering only — it never touches your hitbox, collision or reach, and anything *sent* to Hypixel (party invites, friend adds) always uses your real IGN, so renaming yourself can't break being partied or friended. If a nickname collides with a real account's name, chat asks which one you meant instead of guessing. There's a reset at the bottom
- Custom Items (Cosmetics category; was Armor Recolor) — client-side only: open the menu with Open Custom Items or `/customitems`, click any item in your inventory, armor or hotbar, and the preview on the right shows it with its look. Customize Custom Item searches every Minecraft and Hypixel item: pick one and Apply to Selected draws your item as that one; reopening the page shows the item applied, and Clear takes it off. Customize Custom Color has one row per change, each with its clear on the right: Applies To (this exact item by its UUID, or every copy by its Skyblock id), Color with Reset Color (the color picker has a Hypixel Dyes button listing every Hypixel dye, with Clear Dye), Skin with Clear Skin (the Hypixel item skins that fit the item, found by its id so starred, recombed and fragged items find theirs - Wither Goggles' Celestial, Cyberpunk and Corrupt skins, for example; "Skin: N/A" when it has none), and for armor Trim Color and Trim Pattern, each with its clear (choices read as "emerald", not "minecraft:emerald"). On that page an armor piece is worn by an armor stand in the preview, so the trim pattern shows. Clear Changes and Copy Look (click it, then another item: that item takes every change) sit at the bottom. The tab has the switch and Reset All. Nothing is sent to the server and the real item never changes.
- Held Item Outline (Cosmetics category, off by default) — an outline, a fill, or a filled outline around the item you are holding, drawn on the first-person item model itself: not on the hotbar or the HUD (they draw on top of it), and nothing in third person, with F1, or with an empty hand. Style picks Outline, Fill or Filled Outline; Outline Color and Fill Color each have their own color and alpha; Outline Side picks which face of the item the outline follows: Near (the default) hugs the face nearest you, Far hugs the whole shape including the item's thin sides (the original look), and Both draws around the near and the far face with a line joining them at every corner. A flat item (sword, bow, most items) and a block get all three; a model with no flat front and back (a shield) always uses Far. Outline Width is 1 to 10 screen pixels in half-pixel steps; Main Hand (on) and Off Hand (off) pick which hands get it. It shows whenever it is on, on any server: Skyblock Only does not hide it. It follows the item exactly through swings, eating and the held-item transform, since it is the item drawn a second time into its own mask
- Name Changer — client-side only: change how your own name shows (one color, a fade, or a color per letter, which can Wave or Pulse) and rename specific players (chat, nametags, tab list, GUIs), each with its own color picker. Bold Name draws your own display name in bold, and with Share if Supporter other mod users see it bold too (shared as an &l, which the relay already accepts). Your name shows in exactly the color you pick (one of the 16 chat colors stays that code; any other color is painted as a true color, and shared as one), in the preview and in game. Text Shadow draws your display name with a drop shadow everywhere, name tags included, in a dark shade of its own color (only you see it). Lives in Player Cosmetics under Custom Names
- Ribbon Trail (Cosmetics category, off by default) — one smooth band streaming behind you like a Roblox trail: it stands upright behind you (never flat, whatever way the camera looks), starts just behind your back, and is colored from the head (at you) to the tail, fading out at the end. Set its height, how long it lasts, its opacity and its colors (two or three of your own, your Name Changer fade colors, or a flowing chroma); it shrinks back into you when you stand still and can be hidden in first person. **On Teleport** (Break, Connect or Arc; left click forward, right click back; Connect by default) says what the ribbon does when you etherwarp or otherwise teleport (a jump of more than 4 blocks in one frame): Break leaves a gap between where you left and where you landed, Connect draws one straight band between the two points, Arc draws a curved band that rises between them. It works alongside the squares Trail. A supporter can share it: with Share Ribbon Trail on (on this tab and in Player Cosmetics, on by default, grayed out with the reason for anyone who is not a linked supporter) and Share if Supporter on, your ribbon's colors, height, length and opacity go up with your shared name, and other players on this version of the mod see it behind you, drawn exactly as you see your own (Name Fade uses your shared name's fade colors). Without a display name of your own, only the ribbon goes up, and a name staff set for you is never replaced. Supporter status is checked once at startup, so your name, size and ribbon are shared without opening the menu. Show Others' Trails (on by default) hides everyone else's. At most 16 players' ribbons are drawn, the nearest within 96 blocks; older versions of the mod ignore it. Only ribbons are shared or drawn for others: a squares trail an older version shares is ignored Since 2026-10-18 it has no card of its own: its settings are the Ribbon Trail section of Player Cosmetics, the only trail that is shared.
- Trail (Cosmetics category, off by default) — a square dropped behind you each tick, green on the ground and blue in the air, with adjustable length, size, opacity and fade, and a Show In First Person switch. **On Teleport** (Break, Connect or Arc, Connect by default) says what the squares do when you etherwarp or otherwise teleport (a jump of more than 4 blocks in one tick): Break lays no squares between where you left and where you landed, Connect lays a line of squares between the two points, Arc lays them along a curve that rises between them. It is for configging and only you see it: it is never shared
- Nickhider — its own tab in the Cosmetics category: Randomize Others gives every other player a stable fake name for the session. Besides the players on the tab list and in your world, it hides every name chat shows: friends and guild members joining or leaving, party, guild, officer and co-op chat, private messages both ways, Skyblock all chat, party notices (joined, left, removed, disconnected, invites, transfers, Party Finder joins, the /p list lines), friend notices and /f list, guild notices and member lists, and trade requests. A name read from chat keeps the same fake name everywhere for the session. It works on its own, without the Name Changer toggle. Randomized Name (shown while Randomize Others is on) shows everyone as the base name you type plus a number instead - no two players share a number, each keeps theirs for the session, and changing the base name keeps the numbers; an empty base name falls back to made-up names
- Mod User Tag (Cosmetics category) — See Other Mod Users puts a tag before the name of every player who shows as a mod user (name tags and the tab list), and Show Me As A Mod User lets other mod users see yours; both off by default. The tag is a bold K: in the mod's orange for a legit user, red for a cheat user. In the cheat build, Show As picks whether you show as legit or cheat.

**Hud Elements**
- GIF Player — plays a GIF (with optional audio) as a HUD overlay. Ships with a built-in dancing cat girl GIF in every install, off until you switch it on; it is listed as "Dancing Cat Girl" and toggled like any other GIF, and DVD can bounce it too; your own GIFs in the folder are untouched and still show by default. The GIF / audio folder is one folder for every instance and Minecraft version on this PC (the shared store, gifs); GIFs and audio an instance had in its old folder move in on first use (the same bytes once, different bytes under both names), and profiles keep which GIFs are on but not the files. GIFs are hidden while an inventory, chest, storage or any other menu is open
- DVD — the classic bouncing DVD logo screensaver on your screen; hidden while an inventory, chest, storage or any other menu is open. Its first switch turns the whole feature on or off (off by default; it is also the switch on its menu card): off, no box is drawn whatever each box's own switch says, and your boxes are kept. Each box's **Corner Sound** plays on a perfect corner hit: a game sound (Level Up for a new box, Experience Orb, Note Pling, Harp, Bell or Chime, Amethyst Chime, Arrow Hit, Item Pickup, Villager Yes, Anvil Land, Blaze Hurt), one of your own audio files from the GIF folder, or None; **Corner Volume** sets it from 0 to 500% (above 100% it plays in layers, so 300% is three copies together). The **On Screen Corner Text** box under its heading is the text shown on screen on a corner hit
- Video Browser — watch YouTube Shorts in a small 9:16 window pinned over Minecraft (Windows only; uses your installed Edge or Chrome in its own profile, log into YouTube once). Keybinds for show/hide, next, previous, play/pause and mute work without leaving the game. Dark / Light / System / Amber theme option. Also plays normal YouTube, TikTok, Instagram Reels, Twitch or any URL; an edit mode to drag-move and resize the window; a zoom setting; a DVD-style bouncing placement; a YouTube comments guard so next/previous does not fire while comments are open, and **Auto Scroll** (off by default): when the playing video ends, or loops back to its start as a Short does, the feed goes to the next one as the Next key does. The menu search finds it from tiktok, yt, youtube, web, browser, reels, instagram, twitch or shorts. **Untested**
- Health and Mana Bars — reads Hypixel's action bar (health, mana, defense, overflow mana, vitality, and any other current/max stat it shows with its own icon) plus the game's own experience bar, and draws your own bars and text from them. Turn on Stat Bars and choose a **Layout**: Predefined (the default for a new setup) puts the bars and texts in areas round the hotbar, the way Skyblocker's fancy bars work - two rows above the hotbar where every readout in a row gets an equal share of the hotbar's width, a column left of the hotbar and one right of it, and Hidden - stacked clear of whichever vanilla hearts, hunger, armor, air and XP rows are still showing, and following the window, GUI scale and HUD scale; in Edit Bars you drag a readout onto another area (the target lights up and the others make room) or onto the Hidden tray. Custom puts each one exactly where you drag it in Edit Bars. Edit HUD shows every switched-on bar where it sits in game, so other HUDs can be placed around them, but they cannot be moved there; only Edit Bars moves them. Both arrangements are kept, so switching back finds everything where it was; a setup from before the setting that already shows a readout stays Custom. The page itself is short (simplified 2026-10-12): Stat Bars, Edit Bars, an on/off for each bar, Style and Text Size. Everything else is under **Advanced**, closed until you open it and left as you leave it (the menu search still finds the settings inside): the layout, the bars' look, every color in one grid, the text readouts and the vanilla hides. While Maniac Miner runs (from its "You used your Maniac Miner Pickaxe Ability!" line to its expiry line, 35 s at most), Hypixel's mana figure lags, so the Mana Bar takes 20 mana off for each block the server breaks under your crosshair while you hold attack, and the next different figure the action bar prints wins; always on. The bars: Health (absorption - health past max on the action bar, or the game's own absorption - is its own segment in the Absorption Color straight after your health, like golden hearts, and the health fill stays your health out of max), Mana, Defense (shown as damage reduction, defense / (defense + 100)), Vitality (the resource healing abilities, Wither Shield and Creeper Veil spend; Hypixel shows it on the action bar only while it is in use), Other Resource (anything else current/max with its own icon), XP (the level and progress the game has - on Hypixel that bar is sometimes a countdown instead, and then that is what it shows) and Drill Fuel (the held drill's fuel off the action bar's "1,650/3k Drill Fuel"; Hypixel shows it only while a drill is in your hand, so the bar shows only then; a prismarine shard icon). Advanced holds the shared bar length and thickness, the on/off number on the bar, and the colors: each bar that is on, Absorption, Overflow Mana and the background, in one grid. **Bar Style** sets their look: Bold (the default) has a black and orange double rim, a glossy two-band fill and notches at every quarter; Sleek has a thin dark frame, a sunken track with a soft inner shadow and a gradient fill with a light top edge. Both draw the stat's own item as an icon at the bar's left (a golden apple for health, an enchanted book for mana, an iron chestplate for defense, Hypixel's ♨ Vitality symbol, outlined in the Vitality Bar's color, for vitality, a bottle o' enchanting for XP, a clock for the other resource). You also choose Rounded (a two-step curve) or Square corners, Icons on or off, whether the number sits Inside the bar or Beside it (after its end, in the bar's color, the bar shortened to fit in the same box; bars stacked in one column keep room for the widest number among them, so they end at the same length), Text Size (Small, the default, or Normal, both with a dark outline all round the digits), Smooth Fill (the bar slides to a new value instead of jumping) and Damage Ghost (after a drop a pale strip shows where the bar was, holds for a moment and drains down). Health's absorption and the mana bar's overflow mana show as their own layer in their own color (Absorption Color, Overflow Mana Color, a deep blue by default) after the value, ending at the bar's end at most. The icon and a Beside number take their room inside the bar's own box, so placing and resizing in Edit Bars works the same in every style. A bar showing its number is always at least the number's height plus one unit above and below it (12 units, a little thinner than the 14 it was before 2026-10-13; a Bar Height above that is drawn as set), and the number keeps four units clear of each end - when the full "current/max" does not fit, only the current value shows, and on a very short bar none. In the Predefined layout every bar and text shares one size (scrolling any of them in Edit Bars resizes them all together; it is saved like any setting) and every bar is the Bar Height thick, so a row never mixes sizes; Custom keeps each one's own scale and size. The bars and texts stay drawn while an inventory, chest or any other screen is open, behind it the way vanilla's hearts and hotbar are **Text readouts** (in Advanced): the same stats as text in the same places, plus Overflow Mana, Intelligence (worked out from max mana, which is 100 + Intelligence) and Effective Health (health x (1 + defense / 100)), and a text shadow. **Hide** (in Advanced): the hearts/hunger/armor/air set that only acts while Stat Bars is on (with Unhide Hearts In Rift), and the always-on hides for the vanilla health, absorption, mount health, regeneration bounce, armor and hunger bars, the XP bar with its level, Hypixel's own stat text on the action bar (only the stat numbers go - "5/7 Secrets" or an ability name on the same line stays), and Drill Fuel Text (only the drill fuel segment goes, whether or not the stat text hides). Skill XP Text (off by default) takes Hypixel's skill XP gain segment ("+0.6 Mining (15.05%)", "+12.5 Combat (1,234/50,000)", the "XP" variant) off the action bar and leaves every other segment. Held Item Name sets vanilla's item name that pops up over the hotbar when you switch slot: Hidden Over Bars (the default) hides it only where it would land on one of these bars or texts, Hidden always hides it, Shown never does. The number on a bar is centered on it, to the pixel, in both layouts. Edit Bars has the tab's own settings in a panel beside the bars (Settings Panel: Right, the default, Left or Hidden; the wheel scrolls it, a hover shows each row's description), so every change shows on the bars at once while you drag and resize them. Every bar and text is its own HUD element, moved and scaled in Edit Bars, where in Custom a bar's edges also drag its length and thickness on their own (the Bar Width / Bar Height sliders set every bar at once). True Defense is not offered: Hypixel never puts it on the action bar, only in the /stats menu. The old one-line Classic Display is gone; a saved setup that used it and no other readout comes back as the matching Health/Mana bars and Health/Mana/Defense texts where it was

**Helpers**
- Kick Rejoin Timer — (Misc, both builds, off by default; 2026-11-03, SkyHanni's Kick Duration) for being kicked out of SkyBlock to a Hypixel lobby, where Hypixel's rejoin policy keeps you out of SkyBlock for 60 seconds. It starts on Hypixel's "You were kicked while joining that server!" or "A kick occurred in your connection, so you were put in the SkyBlock lobby!" ("An exception occurred..." too) and always counts 60 s from that line; a second kick line starts the 60 s again, and Hypixel's "Cannot join SkyBlock for a moment! (Queue join in cooldown)" or "You tried to rejoin too fast" replies change nothing. While you are off SkyBlock the Kick Rejoin Timer HUD (movable in Edit HUD) shows "Kicked from SkyBlock 12s ago - rejoin in 48s", then "- rejoin now!"; when the 60 s are over **Rejoin Title** (on) shows "Rejoin SkyBlock now!" and **Rejoin Sound** (on) pings. **Kick Party Message** (off by default) sends **Kick Party Message Text** (default "Kicked from SkyBlock, back in 1 minute!"; at most 100 characters, color codes and line breaks left out, empty puts the default back) to party chat once per kick: a second after the kick, once you are really off SkyBlock, it sends /pl and reads Hypixel's reply; if the reply lists party members it sends your text 0.75 s later, and if it says you are not in a party, or no reply comes within 3 s, it sends nothing. That reply to its own /pl is hidden from your chat (only those lines, only within the 3 s), and the mod's party tracking learns your party from it. A kick line that restarts the wait does not ask or send again. The timer clears when you are back on SkyBlock, after 5 minutes, or if you never left SkyBlock (a failed island warp: then no message either). A limbo ("You were spawned in Limbo.") does not start it. Only Hypixel's own lines count, matched whole, so a player typing them does nothing. /pl and the party line are the only things it ever sends. **Untested**
- Performance HUD — your real ping to the server (a measured round trip, once a second, the way Odin and Skyblocker do it; Hypixel's tab list always says 1 ms) and the server's TPS. Previously called Lag Display; the "server lag" readout was replaced with a real TPS figure and the text bar at the bottom removed **Colors**: Good, Okay, Bad and Awful colors for Color By Value (TPS, ping and FPS pick one by how good the number is; ping is Good under 100 ms, Okay under 150, Bad under 200 and Awful from 200) and a Text color for every line when Color By Value is off and for the CPS line; each opens the color picker. **Stutters**: the FPS line counts frames per second, so one long freeze barely moves it. Frame Time (on) shows the longest frame of the last five seconds and how many stutters there were in the last minute; 1% Low FPS (off) shows the FPS of the slowest 1% of frames. Stutter Threshold (50 ms; left click more, right click less) is how long a frame must be to count, and Log Stutters (on) writes each one to the game log and to `killer560smod-lagdisplay-stutters.log` (never carried in a profile) in plain words: how much of the frame was the game (ticks, packets, world, GUI/HUD, chunk work, the mod's own slowest callbacks), the GPU/driver swap, Java garbage collection, and time outside the game, with a verdict such as "likely your PC" when the game was not running at all (the render thread used little CPU, or a background thread that only sleeps stalled too). Frames are timed only while the Performance HUD is on with one of these three. The Performance HUD stays on screen over the Spirit Leap menu, at its usual spot, so TPS and ping can be read while leaping (leaphud-1108).
- Quiver Display — shows the real "Arrows Remaining" count from any real arrow/quiver item anywhere in your inventory or off hand, in a text color you pick, with an optional Low Arrow Alert (an on-screen title and a sound, text editable with & codes and {count}) when you drop below a threshold of 20 to 200 arrows
- Experimentation Table Solver — solves Chronomatron, Ultrasequencer, and Superpairs by highlighting the answer: you click, it just shows you where. **Superpairs Overview** (on by default, a HUD element you can move in Edit HUD Positions) shows a panel beside the Superpairs board like SkyHanni's: every reward found (green once claimed, white with both tiles seen, "1/2" with one), the powerups turned over, the pairs still to find (the half-found ones, plus the new pairs neither of whose tiles has been seen: exact, "up to N" or "N-M" as the board allows) and the powerups still covered, counted from the fact that a board holds at most 2 powerups: with the powerups already turned over taken off those 2, an odd number of unseen tiles that are no known partner means exactly 1 powerup, an even number means 0 when fewer than 2 are left, and "up to 2" only while none has been turned over and 2 or more such tiles remain. It needs the solver or Auto E-Table on, and never clicks anything. It is its own setting rather than a mode, so you can run it on its own. The **cheat** build adds a separate Auto E-Table section that clicks for you, independent of the solver - either, both or neither can be on; its auto-swap to your best Guardian pet before a run is on by default. In the cheat build the Superpairs highlights follow Auto E-Table's Auto-Solve Superpairs switch: with it off, Superpairs shows no highlights. Its defaults are killer560's own delays: Stop At Max Clicks, Click Delay 400 ms, First Click Delay 600 ms, Random Delay up to 50 ms, no auto-renews (an older settings file moves to these for every delay it never changed); the Superpairs skips and buying Titanic bottles are off until you turn them on. Click Delay is the least time between any two of its clicks, whatever the random delay draws, and neither delay goes below 150 ms.
