When a room flips before any of its mobs could be seen (mid-path, a fraction of a second after you land), it keeps watching for up to a minute: Hypixel leaves an insta-cleared room's mobs standing, so starred mobs seen there later make it an insta clear, standing in it with none makes it a room without starred mobs, and never seeing it counts for nothing. A starred mob that disappears counts as a kill only while you are in its room. The Entrance is never recorded. Entries made by an Interactive Map path, its final landing included, are recorded as etherwarps by the Interactive Map. Rooms already cleared, rooms with no starred mobs, and flips it could not see (no dungeon map, or you were too far away) are kept but count for nothing. A file written before these rules (version 1) is migrated on first load: its failures are kept but no longer counted (most were pass-throughs), its Entrance entries are dropped, and the original file is first copied aside unchanged as `insta-clear.v1-<time>.json`. An entry is known to insta clear only after at least 2 insta clears and not one failure (an entry has 15 seconds after it to flip the room). Auto Secret uses what it records through its own key; there is no readout command any more (`/autosecret` was removed on 2026-11-01). Evidence accumulates across runs in `config/killer560/dungeons/autosecret/insta-clear.json`. It sends nothing to the server.
- Scrollable Tooltips — the mouse wheel scrolls an item tooltip that is taller than the screen, with an optional hold-a-key modifier. Short tooltips are untouched and the wheel still works normally on them.
- Custom Enchant Colors — recolors enchantment names in item lore by SkyHanni-style tier (Poor/Good/Great/Perfect, plus a distinct Ultimate color), not per enchant — matches SkyHanni's own default colors and tier math. Perfect defaults to an animated chroma rainbow like SkyHanni's real default, which flows diagonally like a waterfall, top left to bottom right in gentle waves, across every enchant line of the tooltip at once, with Chroma Speed and Chroma Size sliders; a static color and Bold are options. True Max Detection (on by default) colors a level that only exists above an enchant's normal cap — Giant Killer VII, Sharpness VII, Growth VII, Ender Slayer VII, and others reached only through Experiments/the Dark Auction, not the enchanting table or ordinary book combining — as Perfect instead of an ordinary high level; turning it off falls back to never calling anything above Good "Perfect". Only acts on items Hypixel actually tags with enchantment data.
- Terminal Aura — **cheat build only.** Opens P3 terminals by itself when one comes within range (range, delay, ground-only, an after-leap delay so you don't steal the terminal the person you leapt to was walking into, and **Pause On Movement Keys**, which holds off while you hold WASD or jump — it reads the keys rather than your velocity, so walking into a wall counts and being knocked about does not; sneak is not one of them), and an **Aura FOV** slider (Any by default) that limits it to terminals in front of you and **Turn To Terminal** (on by default): for a terminal your look does not hit, it turns your body - never your camera - to it for one tick, clicks on the next and turns back, because a terminal clicked behind you without it draws GrimAC's Hitboxes flag. It skips any terminal whose stand is above your eyes, since terminals no longer open from below - the rule QUOI added on 2026-10-07. Range is a slider from 0.5 to 4.5 blocks and turns red past 3.0, which is the furthest measured against a live GrimAC without drawing a Reach flag — a terminal is an armor stand, and an entity gets a tighter reach limit than a block's 4.5. Opening only — Auto Terminals is what solves them — and it never rotates your camera.
- Terminal Triggerbot — **cheat build only.** Same shape as Secret Triggerbot: opens the P3 terminal your crosshair is already on, after a configurable delay, with a cooldown and a reach limit. Delay and Cooldown count whole game ticks (rounded up to 50 ms); a Delay of 0 clicks on the tick the crosshair lands on the terminal. Never moves your camera.
- Wither Boss Triggerbot (Goldor Triggerbot until 2026-11-01) — **cheat build only.** While your crosshair is on Goldor in F7/M7 P3 - or, with **All Wither Bosses** on, on any wither boss of the fight (Maxor, Storm, Goldor, Necron) - clicks him at a rate rolled inside a CPS band, from any range: there is no Range setting. In Attack mode a boss in melee reach is attacked; further away the click is the swing alone, which is what a vanilla left click sends there and what a Terminator or a mage beam fires on; with a block under your crosshair nothing is sent. Use mode is a right click (Terminator, Juju). Five class switches (all on by default) pick the classes it works on. **Aim Delay** (0-500 ms, 0 by default) is how long your crosshair must be on him before the first click, rounded up to whole 50 ms game ticks since clicks go out on the tick. Never moves your camera.
- Fast/Auto Leap — **cheat build only.** (its own tab, separate from Leap Menu) QUOI's Auto Leap: a leap to the right teammate for where you are (door opener, Crystal (P1), predev, pads, PY healer, Storm death, P3 sections, middle, P5, relic). The tab has three pages. The front page holds the Fast Leap switch, then **Targets** (Universal or Set Class), then two buttons, Universal Targets and Set Class Targets, each opening its own page (with "< Back" to return); the one the leaps use is lit orange and marked "(in use)". Under them come Click Behavior (Block Inputs, Fast Mode under it, Click Delay, Leap Timeout, Return To); each leap's rows are on the Universal and Class pages, grouped by boss phase: a row that cycles **Off** (the leap is unused), **Fast** (left-click a Spirit Leap or Infinileap there and it leaps) and **Auto** (it leaps by itself when its trigger happens), left click forward, right click back, with Door Opener's After Blood Off and P3's Gate Blown Only under their leap. A settings file from when Fast and Auto were two switches reads Auto where Auto was on, Fast where only Fast was, and Off where both were off. The **Universal Targets** page sets who each leap goes to for everyone: one row per leap ("Crystal: Healer"; P3 has S1-S4) that cycles None, the five classes and your party members by name (left click forward, right click back); a class goes to whoever has it, with the class picks you made in Leap Order applied. The **Set Class Targets** page is the same list for the class picked at its top (When You Play: Mage, Tank, Healer, Archer or Berserker), each row cycling Same as Universal, the five classes and your party members. With Targets on Set Class a leap goes to the pick for the class you are playing this run, and a class with nothing set uses the Universal target; with Targets on Universal the class picks are kept but not used. Your class is read off the dungeon tab list, with a Leap Order class pick on your own name applied only while the tab list does not show your class. A settings file from before the pages loads as Set Class when it had class picks set (they applied then) and Universal otherwise. a Mode button beside each leap's target (Per Leap or Posmsg; Posmsg goes to whoever posted that position in party chat, with the target from the page in use as the backup (each target's keywords are on the Universal page); block inputs / fast mode. Menu search finds every row on all three pages and opens the page a hit is on. An Auto leap takes one step a tick: if the leap is not held it swaps to it (hotbar only - with no leap in the hotbar it says so and does nothing), uses it, clicks the target's head, then swaps to Return To (your previous slot, Stay on Leap for no swap back, or a fixed slot 1-9); a step that fails (no leap, the menu did not open, the target is not in it) says which in chat. The leap menu never shows on screen during a fast/auto leap. **Leap Timeout** (0.5-5 s in 0.25 s steps, 2 s by default) is how long a leap waits for the Spirit Leap menu, then for the target's head, before it gives up. Whatever step a leap is on, it is dropped once it has run longer than both waits plus its click waits, and Block Inputs never blocks past that point; a queued leap that never got to run is dropped after 10 s. The custom leap menu never draws as nothing: with no one to leap to it shows a small notice (Escape closes it), where it used to leave an invisible open menu that held the mouse and movement keys. **Cooldown and no-menu (since 2026-11-03):** Hypixel's "This ability is on cooldown for Ns." answer to the leap item ends the leap at once and returns every input; the leap cooldown is also started by Hypixel's "You have teleported to X!" line, so a leap done by hand counts too, and a Fast Leap on a known cooldown never uses the item or blocks anything; a menu that has not opened within the server's normal reply (twice the measured ping plus 6 ticks, at least 8, never over Leap Timeout) is given up on and movement is released.
- No Rotate (was Leap Takes Target's Look; same saved switch) — **cheat build only, off by default** (its own card in the Dungeons cheat block; Fast Leap can be off). A leap normally puts you on the look of the player you leaped to. But Hypixel sends any teleport that comes within about a second of an earlier one with your own look kept, so a leap right after another teleport (an etherwarp, a Bonzo pad, a boss teleport) leaves you facing where you were. With this on, when such a teleport lands and Hypixel's "You have teleported to Name!" line comes with it, your view turns to that player's look as soon as they are loaded next to you (within 2 s), the short way round. Only your own leaps (by hand, Fast or Auto Leap); since 0.27.3 Hypixel also sends Ender Pearl and AOTE/AOTV teleports with your look kept, so using one of those after (or just before) the leap line cancels the pairing and never turns you; every other teleport, and a leap Hypixel already sent with the target's look, is left as the server sends it. It is a plain turn sent with your next movement; you are never moved. Testkit 1104-leaplook-*.
- Boss levers (Secret Aura tab, "In Boss"; this was the separate Lever Aura until 2026-10-15) — **cheat build only.** The Secret Aura tab holds two features side by side, Secret Aura and In Boss, each with its own switch (the card is on while either is). Under Levers, In Rooms (Secret Aura's lever clicking in rooms, only while Secret Aura is on) and In Boss. In Boss works with Secret Aura off; with Secret Aura on it also lets the aura click the other F7 P3 levers it knows (the old "F7 Boss Levers"). An old Lever Aura switch that was ON loads as In Boss ON; its Section Levers, Boss Lever Range, CPS, Swing Hand and Chat Feedback are unchanged. F7/M7 P3 Section 2: optional S2 section levers (once S2 opens, and optionally early), each clicked at most once, no camera turn (a lever behind you gets your body - never your camera - turned to it for one tick first, as Terminal Aura's Turn To Terminal does, because a click your look misses draws GrimAC's RotationPlace flag); paced by a CPS slider (1-20 flicks a second, each gap varying a little around the average). The Lights device is not part of it any more: see Lights Device
- Thorn (F4/M4) — Spirit Bear HUD (kills toward the bear out of 25/30, spawn countdown, overkill count), Thorn / Spirit Bear / spirit mob / Spirit Bow highlights (through walls is **cheat build only**; the cheat build also has two switches under its red header, **All Mobs Through Walls** - every mob of the fight except bats, the Spirit Bear included - and **Bats Through Walls**, each drawing its targets through walls with no line-of-sight test in the Spirit Mob color, Thorn and the bear keeping theirs), and stun-spot waypoints (three built in). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting. **Thorn Triggerbot** (cheat build only, off by default) sits at the bottom of the Thorn tab under its red Cheat Build header and counts toward the card's switch; it has its own Triggerbot Toggle Keybind row there (a bind made when it was its own card, before 2026-11-02, carries over): in the Thorn fight, with the Tribal Spear in your hand, it holds right click, so vanilla throws the spear at its own held-key rate. With Only When Aimed At Thorn (on by default) it holds only while your crosshair line meets Thorn with no block in front of him; a bat or another mob in between does not stop it. A right click you are holding yourself is never taken over or let go.
- Lights Device (F7/M7 P3 S2) — **cheat build only.** Its own tab in the Dungeons cheat block, out of In Boss since 2026-10-15, with two halves that each have their own switch and settings and work with Secret Aura and In Boss on or off (the card is on while either half is). **Lights Aura** flicks the six Lights levers for you when they are in range: Pre-Flick Before S2 (every unlit lever once, so the device is lit when S2 opens) and Finish When S2 Opens (after S1 is cleared, an unlit lever - the last one completes it - or, with all six already lit, exactly one lever so the device registers); Aura Range (2.1-4.5, to the lever's nearest point, checked again as each flick is sent), Aura CPS (1-20, each gap varying a little around the average), Aura Swing Hand and Chat Feedback; a lever behind you gets your body - never your camera - turned to it for one tick first. **Lights Triggerbot** flicks only the Lights lever your crosshair is on, by the same rules, after its Triggerbot Delay (0-500 ms, rounded up to whole ticks) and at least its Triggerbot Cooldown after its last flick, with its own Triggerbot Swing Hand; it never moves your camera. Every lever is flicked at most once a run between the two halves, nothing after the device is done or past S2. Secret Aura and Secret Triggerbot never click these six levers on an F7/M7 run, and each half has its own place in the mod's one-click-a-tick queue, ahead of Secret Aura. Your old In Boss Lights settings move here once: the Aura is on if In Boss was on with either Lights option on, with the range, CPS, swing and chat it used
- Dungeon Score Calculator — live estimated score and rank (S+/S/A...) from the tab list, with an optional Skill/Explore/Speed/Bonus breakdown, secrets still needed for S+, crypts and deaths (a switch each), colors for the labels, sub-labels, values and good/bad marks, the bonus kills (mimic, prince and the Murkbat bat: Hypixel's "A Bat has been slain. +1 Bonus Score" to a player with the Murkbat shard's Rekindle attribute; each player's bat is +1, up to 5 a run as the wiki and Odin count it, a teammate's known from their "Bat Killed!" party call), and optional 270/300 titles and party messages (party messages off by default)
- Score Warning (its own tab, works with Score Calculator off) — two once-a-run reminders read off the Score Calculator's own numbers. Pre-Boss Warning: the moment the Watcher says you may pass (the last blood mob dies, the second the Portal Entry split starts), a "Before the boss!" title and a chat line list what is still missing (crypts under 5, rooms not cleared - blood counts as cleared - and secrets short of S+); silent when nothing is. **Color Portal** (on by default) tints the portal into the boss from that moment until you enter: red under 270 score (do not enter), yellow from 270 (S), green from 300 (S+), by the Score Calculator's score. **Hide Portal Particles** (off by default, shown while Color Portal is on) drops the nether portal particles around that portal for the same window, nowhere else. **Mage Only** limits it to runs you play as Mage. **Permanent** keeps a title up from then until you enter the boss, following the score: "DO NOT ENTER PORTAL" while anything is missing (with the missing list under it, or the line alone with **List Missing Score** off) and a big green "ENTER THE PORTAL" once nothing is. Crypt Reminder: once Remind At (0:45 by default, 0:05 to 2:00 in 5 second steps; a saved 3:00, the old default, loads as 0:45) has passed since the run started (the first gate dropping, Mort's "found this map" line; never before it) with fewer than 5 crypts, outside the boss, a "Crypts: N/5" title and chat line, and with Tell Party the count in party chat. Warning Sound plays a sound with either. Both off by default
- Low Health Alert (its own tab) — a "Name is low" title with the health under it and a sound when a teammate's health on the dungeon sidebar turns red (once each time they go red), and also one tier earlier when it turns yellow ("Name is getting low", a higher sound; Alert At Yellow Health, on by default); the sidebar is read when Hypixel's team update arrives (event driven, plus once a second). Only While Healer (on by default) keeps it quiet unless you are playing Healer. Off by default
- Breaker Charges (its own tab) — your Dungeon Breaker's charges ("Breaker: 14/20", green, yellow under two thirds, red under a third) as a HUD line in dungeons, read from the first breaker in your hotbar. Move it in the HUD editor. Off by default
- M7 Wither Dragons — Phase 5 helper: per-dragon spawn timers in-world and on a HUD, spawn boxes, health, a tracer and a "which dragon is yours" title, and ice-spray / arrow counters. Your priority is the dragon that spawns first, as Odin and NoammAddons now do (the old power/class split is gone); Dragon Priority sends the Solo Priority class (Tank or Healer) to the one that spawns second instead, by default only for the first two dragons (First Dragon Only). Hypixel's P5 sidebar no longer names the floor, so the mod keeps F7/M7 through the boss until the world changes; before 2026-11-08 that ended everything at P5 (no boxes, tracer, title or end-of-run splits)
- M7 King Relics — relic spawn countdown, highlight of the cauldron matching the relic you're carrying, your placement time, and a party placement summary. **Block Wrong Relic** (off by default, 2026-10-25; NoammAddons' Block Wrong Relic): in P5, a left or right click on one of the five relic cauldrons that is not the color of the relic you carry is dropped before it is sent, and chat says once per relic which cauldron it was and which relic you have. Your own cauldron and every other block are untouched; sneaking lets the click through
- Relic Triggerbot and Relic Look (Relic Helpers tab) — **cheat build only, both off by default.** In M7 Phase 5. Relic Triggerbot clicks a relic your crosshair is already on (to pick it up, within 3 blocks of its box) or your relic's own cauldron (to place it, within 4.5 blocks); it never turns you to find one, never clicks a wrong cauldron, and only clicks once the look the server last heard meets the same target. One click per tick through the mod's action gate, at most one every Cooldown (default 200 ms). Swap To Relic (on) holds the relic one tick before the cauldron click. Relic Look turns you to the nearest relic within Look Range (default 8 blocks), then to its cauldron once you hold it, with Auto Routes' smooth human-like turn; moving your mouse gives you the camera back at once, until the next target. Look At Relic and Look At Cauldron switch each half. Stands aside while Auto Routes, Kill Mimic or AP3 are turning you.
- Chunk Cache — every chunk you've loaded stays readable in memory after the game would unload it, so the Interactive Map, its pathfinder, secret waypoints and the solvers still see rooms you've already visited; memory limit with oldest-first eviction, a Clear Cache button and a live cached-chunk readout. Per world, cleared on every world change; nothing extra is rendered, nothing in a cached chunk ticks, and collision/"is it loaded" stay exactly as they are without the feature
- Chocolate Factory Helper — legit, SkyHanni-style, and never clicks: inside the Chocolate Factory menu it outlines the best-value upgrade green and other affordable ones yellow, outlines stray rabbits gold with a ping and chat line when one appears, reminds you once per visit when the Time Tower has a charge and is not running, and shows chocolate per second, the best upgrade, its cost and the time until you can afford it above the menu.. Its info line above the menu is its own element in Edit HUD (Chocolate Factory Info Line), placed over the open factory menu. Outside the menu, the Hoppity Egg Timers HUD has **Hide Outside Hoppity's Hunt** (hidden while the hunt is not running) and **Hide While Busy** (hidden while you are busy; for now, in a dungeon), both off by default
- Collection Tracker — pick any Skyblock collections (added 2026-11-04; Misc, both builds, off by default) and a movable HUD element (Collection Tracker in Edit HUD) shows each one: its amount, its tier, the progress to the next tier ("Wheat IX: 512,345 (51.2% to X)", Maxed at the last tier) and, with **Show Rate** (on by default), how many you gained per hour this session. **Only Recent** (off by default) lists only the collections whose count went up within the **Recent Window** (a slider, 10-300 s, default 60 s, shown while Only Recent is on): one drops off when its window passes and comes back on its next gain (the gain times are kept in memory only; the Edit HUD preview still shows every pick). The picker lists every collection of Hypixel's list, grouped Farming, Mining, Combat, Foraging, Fishing and Rift, with a search box (a category name shows all of it); up to 30 picks, kept across restarts and in profiles. The list is Hypixel's public collections resource, with a copy in the mod for when it cannot be fetched. The amounts come from your own Skyblock profile through the Profile Viewer's source (your own API key if you set one, else the SkyBlockPV backend; no key ships with the mod), refreshed every 5 minutes, and need the Collections API setting on in Skyblock. Between refreshes they move live: from the hover of each [Sacks] message (still read when Chat Hider hides it), from items of that collection picked up into your inventory while no menu is open (items moved between a sack and the inventory are not counted twice), and from the "Total Collected" line when you open a Collection menu. A profile amount lower than what was counted live is ignored, since the API lags. Only reads; it sends nothing to the server
- Fairy Souls — missing-soul waypoints and a route to the souls you have not found (SkyHanni-style island graphs, downloaded at runtime), a per-profile found log that syncs from Hypixel's own Fairy Souls menu, and a HUD. The tab, top down: the switch (turning it off stops the guide - there is no Stop button), Show Waypoints, Show Path with its Nearest / Whole Island mode, Dim Path and Path Color, the Visible Path, Line Width, Re-Path After and Text Scale sliders, then HUD and Start On Island Join; Target Label, Target Color, Chat Feedback and the found-log buttons below. **Cheat build only: Auto Fairy Souls**, its own tab and one switch - it needs nothing else on, not even Fairy Souls (it loads the island graph and runs the route itself). Auto Walk and Auto Click are parts of it, both ON; with Auto Walk off you walk and it still clicks each soul you reach. Started with its Start/Stop key (no menu button); while it runs your camera is free (it turns only your body, like Auto Routes), moving the mouse looks around, and a movement key (forward, back, left, right, jump), Escape, opening chat or the **Emergency Cancel Key** (Home) stops it, by name; any other key or a click does not. Its own Start/Stop key also stops it, and opening any other menu ends it (walking with a menu open is not done). Its keys come from a model of your real movement - speed attribute (Skyblock speed, Speed, sprint), friction, momentum, air control and Jump Boost - so it brakes for corners and stops at the soul at any speed instead of running past; the last 8 blocks to a soul are walked, not sprinted. Its turning draws a fresh human movement for every turn (varied speed, sometimes a small overshoot and correction, a little jitter, now and then a pause); there is no Turn Speed setting. It clicks each soul once it is in reach (eye to hitbox, 3 blocks) and in sight, through the one-action-a-tick gate. A soul's stand is often a few blocks from the route's soul point (up to 3.6 on the Hub, often up on a ledge), so once it is near it walks the last blocks to a spot the stand itself is in reach and in sight from, instead of stopping at the point. Getting there it crouches under anything 1.5 blocks high (a top slab, a low overhang) and fits into small spaces - 1-wide holes and tunnels, a 1x1 drop - and on a narrow last spot beside a drop it sneaks so it never steps off. Jumps use your real Jump Boost (Hypixel's goes up to VI, about 6 blocks): a step it only just clears is jumped running, as before; one it clears with height to spare is jumped from a walk, never a sprint, and steered in the air onto the block (into a raised hole while your head still fits under its roof), so a soul up on a ledge only the boost reaches is collected instead of flown past; if the boost runs out or comes on mid-walk, the way there is planned again. Free Camera (on by default) keeps your camera where you point it while your character walks; off, you watch the walk turn, and the same keys still stop it. Warp When Stuck (on by default): when it cannot get out on foot or with a tool it is allowed, it warps to the island's own spawn and walks back to that soul once before skipping it; the warp's world change is not taken for a failsafe. Off the route - in water, lava, a pit, fallen off a ledge, knocked off, or stuck on a wall - it plans a way back over the loaded blocks (swimming up and out, ladders, jumps its Jump Boost clears, over slabs, carpets, snow and through open doors) and carries on; where there is none it escapes by etherwarp in the Etherwarp modes (with an etherwarp item in the hotbar), else by Ender Pearl in Walk With Pearls mode (pearls in the hotbar), else says No way out once and skips that soul. Modes: Walk Only (never warps or throws a pearl), Walk With Pearls (walks; throws an Ender Pearl only to escape), Etherwarp (warps that save walking), Fast Etherwarp (warp chains). Sprint can be turned off. Failsafes stop it with the alarm on a world change (lobby, limbo) or disconnect. Damage does not stop it (Shellwise); knocked back, it re-plans from where it landed; if you die, it waits for the respawn (whose teleport is not taken for a correction or a staff check) and re-plans from there. A server correction or a server-set rotation sounds the alarm and it carries on.
- F7 Spots (F7/M7) — your own walk-to waypoints per phase (box, beam, label, distance), a Storm crush timer HUD with purple-pad highlight, and Last Breath aim-spot markers filtered to your class (plus NoammAddons' arrow-stack points and Devonian's Last Breath waypoints for the five P5 dragons). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- P3 Nav (F7/M7) — highlights the Phase 3 section gate until it's actually destroyed, and boxes every unfinished terminal, lever and device in your section, each box vanishing the moment it's completed (through walls is **cheat build only**). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- P3 Status (F7/M7) — one switch, off by default, with every part under it on. **Status HUD** (movable in the HUD editor): during Phase 3 it shows your current section's progress in four short rows: "Terminals 2/4" (out of 5 in section 2), "Device 0/1", "Levers 1/2" and, in sections 1-3, "Gate" with a green ✔ or red ✘. A terminal's own hologram ("Inactive Terminal" or "Terminal Active") corrects the terminal count whenever it is loaded. **Short Progress Titles** replaces Hypixel's "Name activated a terminal! (3/7)" title or subtitle with one short subtitle, "Name Terminal 3/7": the name in that player's dungeon class color, "Terminal 3/7" in gold. **Device Done Title** and **Section Complete Title** show a title when a device in your section is completed and when a section's last objective is done, with a pling when **Title Sound** is on. **Hide Energy Laser Subtitle** drops the Phase 1 "The Energy Laser is charging up!" subtitle. Only lines whose name is a real username are touched, and only on F7/M7; Terminal QoL's Hide Completion still wins when it is on
- Rag Axe — Ragnarock Axe helper: cast/cancel alerts, separate channel (3s), buff (10s) and cooldown (20s) countdowns, the Strength gained message with an optional party announce, and built-in "rag now" prompts with per-prompt lead times for M7 P5 dragons (line + per-dragon spawn), Necron's drop-down, Storm, and F5/M5 Livid
- Blessings — tracks each dungeon blessing your run has (Power, Time, Wisdom, Stone, Life) from the tab list on a movable HUD, with optional Roman numerals and Boss Only (the HUD shows only in the floor's boss room). Levels only — no stat estimates
- Reparty — `/rp` or `/reparty` (off by default, featC-1108; Skyblocker and Skytils): as party leader, reads `/party list`, disbands the party, waits the **Invite Delay** (slider, 0 to 10 s in half seconds, default 1) and invites every other member back with one `/party invite`, spaced through the mod's chat queue (Hypixel's half second gap) and sent only while no movement key is held and you are not sprinting (experimental Grim's ChatC). Anyone who is not the leader gets one chat line and nothing is sent. **Auto Rejoin** (off, reparty2-1109) accepts by itself when another player disbands the party ("X has disbanded the party!") and invites you back ("X has invited you to join their party!") within the **Rejoin Window** (10 to 120 s, default 60): only a player who was in the party that was just disbanded counts, once per disband, sent as `/party accept X`. The old Auto Reparty (after a run) was removed. Legit.
- Puzzle HUD — a movable HUD (Puzzle HUD in the HUD editor, off by default, featC-1108; Odin PuzzleHud, Devonian PuzzlesDisplay and PuzzleTimers) listing the dungeon's puzzles under a "Puzzles done/total" header: a green check for done, a red cross for failed, a yellow star for open, then the puzzle's name, the player who solved or failed it and how long it took (a clock that starts when the tab list first names the puzzle and stops when it turns done or failed). State comes from the tab list's puzzle rows, the player from Hypixel's "PUZZLE SOLVED!" and "PUZZLE FAIL!" lines, polled twice a second only inside a dungeon. **Puzzle Times** and **Puzzle Solvers** (both on) hide those columns. Legit. With Funny Map on (cheat build only) it lists every puzzle the map has identified at once, each at 0:00, and a puzzle's timer starts only when someone opens it.
- Watcher Helper — grades the blood room's Watcher (off by default, featC-1108; Athen Watcher Helper): the time from the blood door's greeting to "Let's see how you can handle this." is how long he took to speak, and **Watcher Speed Title** (on) shows Vroom (under 22 s), Watcher (under 23 s), Yawn (under 25 s) or Zzz with the time; **Watcher Breakdown** (on) prints one chat line when "You have proven yourself" arrives: Speak, Move (the Watcher's first step, the same measure as Split Timers' Watcher Move), Kill and Total. **Send To Party Chat** (off) sends "Watcher spoke in 21.40s (Vroom)" to party chat with /pc once per run, only while teammates are in the dungeon. Works with Split Timers off. Legit.
- Carry Tracker — counts runs per carried player and tells you when to stop (off by default, featG-1109, carryhud-1109): add clients by the box and Add Player, by Add Party (every party member but you) or by `/carry add <name> [runs]` (`/carry remove <name>`, `/carry list`); up to 12. Each client has a runs target (the Default Runs slider, 1-50, sets it when adding; the - and + buttons change it per client). When a dungeon run ends with a client in the party, their count goes up by one and the run (name, floor, time, score) goes into a history. A movable HUD (Carry Tracker in the HUD editor) lists one row per client, "name  done/total runs", in the menu theme's color and green once done; it shows in dungeons and the Dungeon Hub and hides with no clients. When a client reaches the target you get one title, a sound and the chat line "Stop carrying <name>: <n>/<n> runs done"; Remove When Done (off by default) then removes them. A newly added client starts at 0. Counts and history are RECORDS in the shared store, kept per Minecraft account and added to by every instance; the client list and targets are settings. There is no webhook.
- Boss Health — a movable HUD line (or several) with the health of whichever Catacombs boss is up, F1 to M7, shown only in the boss room: Bonzo, Scarf, The Professor, Thorn (his lives, from the boss bar), Livid (the real one, told apart by the wool clue whether or not Livid Solver is on; with several Livids in sight and no clue yet it shows none), Sadan, and Maxor, Storm, Goldor and Necron (from the boss bar's fill times their full health, as NoammAddons and Skytils do). Show Minions (on) adds the four Guardians on F3, Scarf's Undead Warrior, Priest, Mage and Archer on F2, and Sadan's giants on F6. Show Max Health (on) adds the full health after the current one (the wiki's numbers; where none is known, the highest health seen this run), and Show Percent (off) adds the percent left. The health is green above half, yellow above a quarter and red below. The M7 dragons' health stays with Wither Dragons. Off by default
- Maxor's Crystals (F7/M7 P1) — Energy Crystal respawn countdown, how long you took to place yours (with a saved personal best, wiped only on a two-click confirm), a warning while you're still holding an unplaced crystal, an active-crystals counter ("Active: 1/2", from Hypixel's "X/2 Energy Crystals are now active!" line; once the laser fires it drops to 0/2 the moment the next set appears on its towers and can be picked up, instead of holding 2/2 until the next placement), and an optional highlight of the crystals. It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- Dungeon Run Summary — one saved record per finished run (floor, total time, splits, device/terminal times, secrets, crypts, deaths, failed puzzles, the mod's estimated score next to Hypixel's real Team Score, class and party size, the party with each player's class, rooms, secrets and deaths, which rooms each of them cleared and what killed them, and the dungeon map with every room's rotation), a history browser, per-floor personal bests (a run only counts, and is only announced, when it was actually finished with the boss defeated and Hypixel's Team Score reached the "PB Minimum Score" setting, S 270 by default or S+ 300; a wipe, a death-ended run, a warp out or a lower score never sets or announces one, and a stale PB mark on such a run is cleared when the game loads), and an optional compact chat summary (off by default so it doesn't repeat Split Timers)
- Posmsg — position-message waypoints for the F7/M7 boss fight: each one draws a ring on the ground and types its own message into party chat ("at hee2" — no coordinates, so teammates need no mod to read it) the moment you walk inside it. Completely inert outside the boss. Presets for the real spots (Simon Says/EE2/EE3/Outcore/Recore/Necron's Platform/P5) ship pre-positioned with real coordinates, plus custom ones you add; each has its own message, radius slider, ring toggle, color, label text scale and height, and an optional "only send once per run". Every waypoint starts off — turn on the ones you want. Leap onto a teammate standing in a ring and its message goes after your leap messages, half a second after the last one, never at the same moment (Hypixel would refuse it as too fast); walk in with nothing else being sent and it goes at once. Every party line the mod sends by itself (Posmsg, Leap Message, Cringe, Simon Says, Livid, Blood Camp, Melody and the rest) waits its turn the same way
- Advanced Position — your exact X/Y/Z on a movable HUD with 0-8 decimal places (F3 shows 3), plus optional facing (raw running yaw with its wrapped value, and pitch), the block you stand on, and velocity in blocks per tick. Standing on the ground the y velocity reads 0 (vanilla keeps -0.0784 there, gravity added after every move, which the HUD used to show as if you were falling); a jump or a fall shows its real y velocity. While something is holding your camera (an align, an auto puzzle repositioning), the facing lines follow where you are actually LOOKING rather than the body angle the automation is holding you at, since that is the one your mouse is moving; the body yaw is still shown, dimmed, after it, because that is the one the server receives and the one an AP3 node is made from
- Run HUDs — two separately placeable HUDs: a Secrets HUD (secrets found this run plus the current room's count, in a text color you pick with the Secrets Text Color setting, white by default; shown during the clear only - it hides from the moment you enter the boss room on any floor until the next run, and the HUD editor still shows it so it can be moved; the room count goes up the moment you take a secret and the action bar corrects it, and since 2026-11-01 a locked chest (Hypixel answers "That chest is locked!") is taken back off it, and a picked-up item counts only when it is a dungeon secret item that did not come out of a chest you opened) and the Score HUD from Score Calculator, which now also owns the 270/300 messages and mimic/prince/bat kill alerts (dungeons only). The run/no-lag timer used to be a third "Time HUD" here - removed, that belongs to Split Timers now
- Dungeon Alerts — Shadow Assassin alert, secret-collected sound, F6/M6 Terracotta timers, Spring Boots height HUD (with a height box in your color, drawn as an outline, filled, or both), dungeon class colors on nametags and tab, and room-entry alerts. The Dungeon Alerts tab (Shadow Assassin Alert and Room Alerts) has a switch for each alert and none over both: the alerts work independently, and the menu card is ON while either is on and OFF only when both are (an old file with the former both-at-once switch OFF loads with both alerts OFF). Room Cleared only alerts for the room you are standing in, and only when you saw it uncleared and then saw it clear (walking into a room a teammate already cleared is silent); the on-screen text says just "Room Cleared!" or "Secrets Done!" with no room name, nothing is sent to chat, and the On-screen Text switch is gone because the text is the alert. Cleared Color and Secrets Color set the two texts' colors, and the Shadow Assassin Alert has an Alert Color (purple by default). Key Spawn Alert (its own switch in the same tab, off by default) shows "Wither Key spawned!" or "Blood Key spawned!" with a sound when a key's armor stand appears in the clear, once per key (featB-1108, five more alerts in the same tab, each with its own switch, all off by default, all legit: Rare Room Alerts shows "Trinity found!" (or Tomioka, Duncan) with a sound the first time the live map identifies that room, once per room per run; Rare Drop Alerts shows "Ice Spray Wand dropped!" or "Skeleton Master Chestplate dropped!" when a dropped item's name tag (armor stand or item entity) carries that exact name, once per entity; Duplicate Class Alert shows "Duplicate Mage!" with the players sharing the class, once per duplicated class per run, read from the class map Class Colors builds from the tab list, with a separate "Party Message (Duplicate Class)" switch, off by default, that also sends it to party chat; Wither King Statue Box (M7 phase 5) draws a wire box in each dragon's color around every statue still standing, until that statue's block goes to air; Dimensional Slash Alert (M7 phase 5) shows "Dimensional Slash!" while you stand within 5 blocks of glowstone the server placed, and clears the zones when glowstone leaves)
- Party Interop — one place for what the whole party knows about the run. Works out what it can from what Hypixel already sends every client (the action bar's per-room secret count, terminal/device/lever lines, blood door and Watcher lines, the tab list's totals), reads the party-chat announcements other dungeon mods make (NoammAddons, Odin, Devonian, QUOI, Skytils markers) so you benefit without installing any of them, and leaves a seam for the mod's own relay. Every fact records where it came from and the most trustworthy source wins. Optional read-only bridge to NoammAddons/Odin if you happen to run them. On by default (turn it off to stop sharing and receiving); dungeons only
- Teammate Highlight — boxes your dungeon party in the world colored by their class, with optional name and distance labels, a self toggle and a dead-teammate skip; legit mode only shows a teammate you can actually see, the **cheat** build can box through walls
- Mob ESP — highlights star-tier dungeon mobs by name filter (real vanilla Glowing); legit mode only glows what you can already see via a real raycast, the **cheat** build can glow through walls too
- Mapping — a data-gathering tool for map research ("Dump Held Map Now"); Funny Map, the mimic room and the class-color heads it used to reserve are real settings of the Dungeon Map now. The Dungeon Map tab's **Extra Info Overlay** keeps its switch here, with **Extra Info Scale** (0.5x-2x, 1x by default) beside it: the text under the map is drawn that much larger or smaller, and a row too wide for the map at that size moves its last fields onto another row
- Auto Superboom — **cheat build only, off by default** (Dungeons cheat block, at the bottom). In a dungeon, your own left click on a cracked wall (cracked stone bricks) with something else in hand swaps to your Superboom TNT first and back a tick later. The click stays yours: the swap goes out at the start of the tick your press is queued in, ahead of the dig, which is the order a vanilla client sends after pressing the Superboom's hotbar key. The swap back waits for a later tick and for `HotbarSelect.swapWouldFlag()` (GrimAC PacketOrderE), and a second click on the wall keeps the Superboom out; a slot change of your own cancels the swap back. Runs through ActionGate. Tested on default and experimental GrimAC with your dig, use, scroll or teleport answer in the swap's tick (testkit 1108-same-superboom-*). **Untested**
- Etherwarp Waypoints — mark secret / etherwarp spots per room: /ew waypoint add [name] boxes the block you're standing on (centered), saved against the room so it shows in that room on every run. /ew delete (or /ew remove) deletes the waypoint you stand on, /ew delete <n> waypoint #n of this room, /ew clear every waypoint in the room you're in, and /ew undo and /ew redo step back and forward through the last 50 adds, deletes and clears. One toggle turns the whole section on or off
- Custom Leap Menu — replaces the Spirit Leap GUI (the real chest and your inventory are hidden) with 4 big name boxes in your Leap Order, colored by class; click a quarter of the screen or press 1-4 to leap (scale 50-400%); Show Player Heads puts each teammate's face in their box; **Show Map During Clear** (a switch; it was the Leap Style setting) shows the dungeon map during the clear with each teammate's face where they stand - click a face to leap - and the boxes again in boss; ready the moment Hypixel sends the menu, nothing shifts while a leap is in flight, and no head is drawn on the cursor after a click
- Auto Superboom targets (gates, crypts, princes) — **cheat build only.** The Auto Superboom swap above also happens on your left click on a Goldor P3 gate block (only while P3 runs: the gate's arch, door and portcullis blocks inside the gate boxes of `bosssim/BossGates`), and on a crypt's or prince's tomb blocks in the room you stand in (the blocks `CryptScanner` finds: lid slabs, a prince's gold, sea lantern, obsidian or smooth stone crown; the scan runs only when you click such a block and is kept 5 s). Same swap path, ActionGate and `HotbarSelect.swapWouldFlag()`. Also in this batch: Blood Camp's overlay no longer waits for you to stand in the Blood Room (it anchors to the Blood Room the map knows), Auto Blood Camp has **Work Just Outside Blood Room** (off; Trigger Bot and Aura also work up to 6 blocks outside the room's tile), Auto Terminals' Hover Terminals and Queue Terms run without the Terminal Solver or Auto Terminals switch, Auto Simon Says runs without Simon Says' own switch (solver, highlights, party tracker), Stream Safe's hand no longer pauses after the first click, and the real pointer returns when a Termism board is solved.
- Item Protection — protect items (hover + Protect Key, by Skyblock UUID or optionally item id, or by a typed name) and dungeon-starred items, so a Hyperion can't be dropped, thrown out of a menu, sold, salvaged, traded, auctioned or anvil-fed by accident; every block says so in chat. Each protected item shows a tiny five-point star in its slot's top-right corner, which stays while you hover it and sits under the tooltip (starred items protected by Auto-Protect Starred get no marker); starred items that are not locked (not on the Protect Item list) can still go into the anvil and trades. Show Star (on by default) turns that star off in every menu it is drawn in, your own inventory and its hotbar row included, while the items stay exactly as protected; Star Color sets its color while it shows. Lock In Place stops protected items being clicked at all, even to move them around your own inventory - this replaced Slot Lock, whose locked slots were carried over to their items' UUIDs. Prevent Hotbar Drops swallows the drop key while you hold a protected item; with Confirm To Force on, pressing drop 2-4 times (Drops To Force), each within 3 seconds of the last, drops it anyway
- Slot Binds — link two slots in your real inventory (one must be a hotbar slot); shift-click either one to swap it with its bound partner using the same real vanilla mechanic pressing a number key over a slot uses. To make a bind, hover a slot and press the bind key, then hover the partner slot and press the bind key again (pressing it over a bound slot deletes that bind). You can also hold the bind key over a slot and release it over a hotbar slot to swap the two; this does not make a bind. If Protect Item uses the same key, a quick tap over an item toggles its protection and a drag is only the swap (2026-11-08) The tab no longer lists the binds as "Slot a <-> Slot b" text. Pressing the bind key over a bound slot removes that bind; when Item Protect uses the same key, that first press only removes the bind (the item is not protected), and the next press protects as usual.
- Party Commands — Odin's party commands and "!" replies in one list: !warp, !wt, !allinvite, !ptme, !invite, !kick, !kickoffline, !reinv (kick the teammate who asked and invite them back 5 seconds later), !demote, !promote, !f1-!m7/!t1-!t5, !dt, !undt, !boop, !help, and the replies !coords, !ping, !fps, !time, !holding, !cf, !8ball, !dice, !tps, !location and !odin. Three sections: the main toggle (with a reply delay, 200 ms by default, so Hypixel does not answer "You are sending commands too fast"), which chats may use them (party, guild, all chat, Mod Chat, DMs, co-op; replies go back where they were asked), and one toggle per command. Anything that changes the party or the run only works for someone actually in your party or current dungeon run, whatever chat it came from; a DM'd !invite invites the sender only when they are on your friends list. Every other command, the replies included (!coords, !location and !time say where you are and your time zone), answers only a party or dungeon teammate or a friend, never a stranger. Only chat that really came from the server (or the relay) counts, it is rate limited per player and overall, and everything a party command does is written to the mod log. Your own party commands run too when you are the leader (your own "!pt Name" transfers to Name); only one that would act on yourself (a bare "!pt", "!pt" with your own name, warp+transfer, promote, demote, reinv, a DM'd invite) is skipped. A !reinv is never canceled by the party disbanding (kicking its last other member disbands it): the invite still goes out, and for 60 seconds after it a party invite from anyone who was in that party is accepted automatically. A floor request (!f1-!f7, !m1-!m7, !t1-!t5); inside Creation Cooldown it waits the cooldown out and then goes, and a repeat while one waits replaces it instead of counting against the rate limit. Every command starts ON once the feature is switched on (nothing is on until you do); of the chats, Guild (gc) and All Chat (ac) start OFF and the rest on.
- Slot Binds — link two slots in your real inventory (one must be a hotbar slot); shift-click either one to swap it with its bound partner using the same real vanilla mechanic pressing a number key over a slot uses. To make a bind, hover a slot and press the bind key, then hover the partner slot and press the bind key again (pressing it over a bound slot deletes that bind). You can also hold the bind key over a slot and release it over a hotbar slot to swap the two; this does not make a bind. If Protect Item uses the same key, a quick tap over an item toggles its protection and a drag is only the swap
- Party Commands — Odin's party commands and "!" replies in one list: !warp, !wt, !allinvite, !ptme, !invite, !kick, !kickoffline, !reinv (kick the teammate who asked and invite them back 5 seconds later), !demote, !promote, !f1-!m7/!t1-!t5, !dt, !undt, !boop, !help, and the replies !coords, !ping, !fps, !time, !holding, !cf, !8ball, !dice, !tps, !location and !odin. Three sections: the main toggle (with a reply delay, 200 ms by default, so Hypixel does not answer "You are sending commands too fast"), which chats may use them (party, guild, all chat, Mod Chat, DMs, co-op; replies go back where they were asked), and one toggle per command. Anything that changes the party or the run only works for someone actually in your party or current dungeon run, whatever chat it came from; a DM'd !invite invites the sender only when they are on your friends list. Every other command, the replies included (!coords, !location and !time say where you are and your time zone), answers only a party or dungeon teammate or a friend, never a stranger. Only chat that really came from the server (or the relay) counts, there is no cooldown on players (the mod's own replies never start with "!", so it cannot answer itself in a loop), and everything a party command does is written to the mod log. Your own party commands run too when you are the leader (your own "!pt Name" transfers to Name); only one that would act on yourself (a bare "!pt", "!pt" with your own name, warp+transfer, promote, demote, reinv, a DM'd invite) is skipped. A !reinv is never canceled by the party disbanding (kicking its last other member disbands it): the invite still goes out, and for 60 seconds after it a party invite from anyone who was in that party is accepted automatically. A floor request (!f1-!f7, !m1-!m7, !t1-!t5); inside Creation Cooldown it waits the cooldown out and then goes, and a repeat while one waits replaces it. Every command starts ON once the feature is switched on (nothing is on until you do); of the chats, Guild (gc) and All Chat (ac) start OFF and the rest on.
- Door Keys — marks a real dropped Wither/Blood Key the moment it appears with its pickup range (Hypixel SkyBlock 0.27.2 added 5 blocks to the key pickup range, so the range is the Key Base Range setting, 1 block by default, plus 5). **Pickup Range** picks how: Circle, a flat circle at the key's own height; Sphere, a see-through ball of that radius around the key, all one color with no line around it - step inside it and the key is yours. **Radius** sets the size of either, in blocks (far left follows the pickup range). **Circle Thickness** sets the circle's line width and **Circle Height** moves the circle up or down from the middle of the key's stand (it is drawn there, not at the stand's feet). **Circle Color** (**Sphere Color** on Sphere) and **Tracer Color** each take a color (Auto: full black for a Wither Key, red for a Blood Key, both at 50% alpha, and an Auto tracer matches the circle or sphere; right click a color to put it back to Auto). An optional tracer line (adjustable thickness) and, in the cheat build, an ESP option that shows them through walls
- Wither Doors — highlights the Wither and Blood doors the dungeon map in your hotbar shows, and nothing it does not: a door appears once a room it leads from is opened on the map, and nothing is drawn before Mort's countdown ends and the run starts (in the sim, before the run's countdown says GO), since there is no map to read until then. Each door is a box with a waypoint label over it ("Wither Door 34m", readable through walls; **Door Labels**, on by default, turns the labels off). Wither doors are red while the team has no Wither Key and, once it has one, the door you need to open turns green: the first locked door on the map's room path from your room toward Blood (what Auto Blood Rush opens next), else the nearest locked one by walking path, and only if the map has no answer the nearest in a straight line (it used to be the nearest in a straight line always, which could be a door behind a wall or off the way to Blood); the Blood door has its own **Blood Door Color** and **Blood Door (Key) Color** (it used to need Show All Doors). A wither door the world shows open is dropped, as the map does a moment later. Render Distance 32-256 blocks (96 by default). Off by default. **Style** draws the door as an Outline (the original look, and the default), a Fill, or a Filled Outline; **Fill Opacity** (5-100%, 35% by default) sets how strongly the fill shades the door. **Fill Color** is Same as Outline by default, so the fill takes the door's current color and a locked door still reads differently from one you have the key for; set it to Custom to fill every door with one **Custom Fill Color**. **Fairy Door** (both builds, on by default) highlights the door into the Fairy room in its own **Fairy Door Color** (pink, Fairy's map color) once you know it is one: the map draws that door like a wither door while its doorway is empty, so a map-wither door into an unopened room whose empty doorway you have actually looked at (a clear line of sight from your eyes) is the Fairy door. Until you have seen it, it is drawn as what the map says, a wither door. It clears when you or a teammate step into the room beyond, or the map shows it opened. The door on the far side of Fairy is not drawn while Fairy is unopened, and after that only once its block is loaded. In the cheat build, **Show All Doors** instead highlights every locked Wither door and the Blood door in the loaded world, before the map shows them and before the run starts, and draws the highlights - outline and fill alike - through walls; with it on, the Fairy Door is the door into Fairy on the path from the room you are in to Blood (the path Auto Blood Rush takes) from the start, taken from the map grid, and clears for the rest of the run as soon as you or any teammate steps into Fairy, the map shows Fairy opened, or the door itself opens; a Fairy door that is still a shut wither door (coal) is drawn by the wither highlight but in the Fairy Door color, and while the map cannot yet trace the path to Blood (right at the run start) the way to Fairy itself is used and a door already found is kept (2026-11-01)
- Trajectories — predicts where a real bow shot or Ender Pearl throw would land using the real vanilla drag/gravity physics for each. A normal bow shows its path only while you draw it, at the speed the draw has reached. Shortbows (on by default): a Skyblock shortbow (lore "Shortbow: Instantly shoots!", or a TERMINATOR / *_SHORTBOW item id) fires instantly at full power, so its path shows the whole time you hold it, with one path per arrow: three for the Terminator, the side arrows 5 degrees to each side. Never fires anything
- Loadout Keybinds — on the real "(N/M) Loadout" screen, use number-row keys and left/right arrows to click a loadout slot or page instead of the mouse. The tab's **Open Loadouts** button runs /loadouts (sent straight to Hypixel) to open the Loadout menu, whether or not the keybinds are on; **Open Loadouts Key** is a bind (key or mouse button, off until set) that does the same from anywhere with no menu open. Each slot key has its own mode: **Only In Menu** (the default) clicks only while the Loadout menu is open; **Open, Swap, Close** works anywhere outside a menu (never while typing in chat): it runs /loadouts, waits for the Loadout menu, clicks that loadout one action per tick, then closes the menu it opened, and says so in chat if the menu never opens within 5 seconds. **Highlight Selected Loadout** (on by default) puts a green background behind the loadout you have on in the Loadout menu: the one whose item says it is selected or equipped, or else the last one Hypixel's "You equipped Loadout N!" (or, for a renamed loadout, "You equipped <its name>!") named this session, found by number or by the slot's item name (no slot of that name on the page, no highlight); it only draws
- Ability Keybinds — bind any key or mouse button to your real dungeon class Ability or Ultimate (the same real vanilla drop-stack/drop-item action Hypixel already reads), instead of the fixed Ctrl+Q/Q The player list (Tab) always draws on top of every mod HUD and overlay. If an Ability or Ultimate bind is on the same key as the player list, a quick tap that casts the ability does not show the list; holding the key for 300 ms shows it, and so does a press where Hypixel answers that the ability is on cooldown (outside a dungeon nothing is cast, so it always shows).
- P4 Platform Highlight — highlights the real fixed 3x3 platform you need to mine after Goldor dies, before dropping into Necron's fight, and hides itself again once the platform has been mined out
- I Hate Diorite (cheat build only) — swaps Storm's real diorite pillars to see-through stained glass client-side for the whole F7/M7 boss (not only Storm's phase), and a block the server sets in a pillar (a pillar lowered or rebuilt) is glass the same tick it arrives instead of after a rescan (since 2026-11-03)
- Chat Keybinds — one tab, one switch (off by default), four sections. **Key Binds**: bind any key or mouse button (middle click included) to a command or chat message you type in yourself, each row with its own on/off; the old 8 menu binds are migrated automatically. **Catacombs / Master Mode** and **Kuudra**: /f0-/f7, /m1-/m7, /basic /hot /burning /fiery /infernal and /kuudra <tier> expand to Hypixel's own /joininstance command, one toggle per floor (F1 covers /f1 and /m1) and per Kuudra tier (a toggle applies the next time you join a world; hover one to see the exact command it sends). **Custom Shortcuts**: your own - a name you type (letters, digits and _, up to 32, without the slash) and the command it sends to the server, each with its own on/off, an optional key that sends the same command, and Delete. A name that is already a command (built-in, another mod's, Fabric's, vanilla's or the server's) is refused with the reason under the list, and custom shortcuts register as you edit them, no rejoin needed. Command Shortcuts used to be a tab of its own; its settings moved into Chat Keybinds on 2026-10-08 with nothing lost (a half that was switched off keeps its rows switched off)
- Reforge Helper — in the Blacksmith's "Reforge Item" menu, a panel to the left lists every reforge the item can roll at its type and rarity, each with the stats it gives at that rarity (Hypixel's stat icons; hover a row for the full names), the current one marked with an orange arrow. Click a row to add it to your Wanted Reforges, or click it again to take it off; wanted rows have a green check. The item gets an outline in a color of your choice once it has a wanted reforge, and its current reforge is written over it. Block When Wanted (on by default) cancels your own click on the Reforge button while the item has a wanted reforge, before anything is sent, so you never roll a good one away; a chat line says so. Wanted Reforges can also be typed in the tab (comma separated, like "Fabled, Withered"). The reforge list and stats come from the NotEnoughUpdates repo's reforge table (the source SkyHanni uses), bundled with the mod. The menu's layout (item in the middle of the second row, the button under it) is SkyHanni's, not checked in game. It never clicks for you. Off by default
- Revert Master Stars — shows Master Star items with the old all-red-stars look instead of Hypixel's numbered pip (cosmetic only)
- Inventory Search — Ctrl+F, or a click into the search bar, to search. The bar has one fixed spot and width on every screen: bottom middle, just above the hotbar, hearts and XP bar (in a window under about 280 GUI units tall, at the very bottom); everything else lays out around it - the Storage Overlay's grid and Inventory panel end above it, the Item Browser stops above it when it reaches over it, other panels over a menu move up, and a menu too tall to fit above it (a double chest at a big GUI scale) is moved up so no slot is under it. A long search shows its end. It searches by name/lore in any inventory-type screen; every matching slot is filled solid in the highlight color (red by default) behind the item. The bar is also a calculator like NEU's: type a sum such as 5k*3, 2s+10 or (1.5m-200k)/4 and it shows "= result" (+ - * x / ^, brackets, and the suffixes k, m, b, t, s = 64, e = 160 and %). A number or a closing bracket next to a bracket multiplies, so 2(1+1), (1+1)(2) and (1+1)2 are 4; two plain numbers side by side (2 3) are still a search. Adjustable bar scale, Ctrl+Backspace word delete, and correct highlighting inside the Storage Overlay's own grid Enter in the bar: if it holds a sum, the sum is replaced by its answer (as plain digits) and typing stops.
- Pet Wheel — a radial keybind menu of the pets you choose; picking one summons it through /pets (hold-and-release or press-then-click; every pet you add gets its own slice and the wheel lays itself out; Scale grows the wheel and its text). The summon reacts to the menu instead of waiting on timers: /pets goes out the moment you pick, and the pet is clicked the first tick its page has loaded, so it takes about one round trip to Hypixel plus a tick or two per page. Walking, jumping and sprinting are held still until it finishes. Hide Pets Menu keeps the /pets menu from ever appearing while the wheel works - it still opens and closes behind the scenes. Hypixel has no command that summons a named pet directly, so the menu is the only route. The wheel's tiles and labels are drawn in the mod menu's theme (labels on small plates, so they stay readable over the world on Light); its Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can pick any preset or your Custom colors for the wheel alone
- Best Friends — /bestfriends: time partied and dungeon runs done with each player, kept forever, in a searchable, sortable menu. The list shows each player's time compactly ("1h 32m", "10d 3h 10m"); clicking a player shows it to the second ("1h 32m 5s"), counting up live while you are partied. Time is measured with the system clock between seeing a player join and leave the party (checked a quarter of a second at a time), kept to the millisecond, written every 30 seconds, on leaving the server and when the game closes. Typing anywhere on the list goes into its search box. Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone
- Friends List — our own friends list with notes; /fl can open it instead of Hypixel's, and /flcustom always reaches ours. A sync (Refresh, or opening the list when it is over ten minutes old) walks every page of Hypixel's /fl by following its own next-page arrow, about one page a second, and the /fl blocks it asks for are hidden from chat. A sync that loses its reply part-way keeps what it read and says the list may be incomplete. Each row shows the friend's real skin head (from the tab list when they are in your lobby, otherwise their profile is fetched once and cached), what the last sync said they were doing ("SkyBlock - Garden", "Offline", aged when it is old), and an Invite button that sends /party invite (at most one every two seconds; also on a friend's detail page). The scrollbar is wide, its thumb can be dragged, and clicking the track jumps there. While a sync runs, the line under the list shows how far it has got ("Syncing... page 3/7", from Hypixel's "Friends (Page 3 of 7)" header); that line has its own strip below the list and never covers a row. One search bar, beside Add, narrows the list by name as you type (any part of the name, upper or lower case), and Add sends /f add with whatever is typed in it; just typing on the list fills it, and clearing it shows everyone again. A "Filter by" drop-down under the search bar narrows the list to one area: it lists All plus only the areas friends are in right now (the activity the last sync read for each online friend, such as "SkyBlock - Hub"), so an area nobody is in never appears, it refreshes as the friends data does, falls back to All when its area empties, and keeps the choice until the game closes. Each row also has a /pv button that opens the mod's own profile viewer for that friend (nothing is sent to the server; the name is checked to be a valid username first). Rows appear one by one while a sync is still reading pages, not only when it ends. Friends who are in your party are listed first under a themed "In Party" header, with a "Friends" divider before the rest, which keeps its order (switch "Party Friends On Top", default ON; non-friend party members are not added; the search and area filter apply inside both groups). Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone
- Auction House Browser — one switch for the browser and for Hypixel's own Auction House drawn in its look (below). The keybind, the Open Auction House button, or /ah with the /ah override on: every live auction from Hypixel's public API (scanned in the background every five minutes, page by page off the render thread, only new auctions decoded on a rescan, skipped entirely while Hypixel's snapshot has not changed) in one screen of the mod's own design. Hypixel's six categories (Weapons, Armor, Accessories, Consumables, Blocks, Tools & Misc) are tabs along the top, with a search field (type anywhere to search; names, item ids and catalog names all match) and Sort, Rarity, BIN only / Auctions only and Price range filters beside it; recent searches and recently viewed items sit on the left. Listings are cards with the item's real icon, its name in Hypixel's colors, the price (BIN, top bid or starting bid), a BIN or bid-count badge, time left and pet level, and the cheapest BIN of an item is outlined and marked Lowest BIN; hovering shows the item's full lore plus the lowest BIN, how many are listed and recent sale prices. Clicking a card sends Hypixel's own /viewauction for it and its real auction page opens in the same frame (see Reskin Real Auction House). Create Auction, Manage Auctions and View Bids are one button away through Hypixel's own /ah menu. Lowest BIN per item, an hourly lowest-BIN history (24 points, drawn as a sparkline) and recent sales from Hypixel's ended-auctions feed (polled once a minute only while the AH is in use) are kept across restarts. The screen follows the mod menu's theme (or its own Theme setting; Inventory Theme there gives the older look, the Inventory Theme's Amber, Dark or Light and its accent), and its backdrop is opaque so no HUD shows through. /hypixelah always reaches Hypixel's own menu
  - *Hypixel's own Auction House, reskinned* (the same switch: turning the Auction House Browser on or off turns this on or off too, since 2026-10-11) — Hypixel's real Auction House menus (the Auction Master, /ah, or a listing opened from the browser) open as pages of the same screen instead of the chest: the Auction House menu as cards, the Auctions Browser with its own category tabs, search sign, sort, rarity and BIN filters and page arrows in the header and toolbar and its listings as cards (price, BIN or bids, seller, time left, lowest-BIN marker), an auction's page with the item large, its price, seller and time left, a market panel (lowest BIN, listings, recent sales, 24-hour sparkline) and Hypixel's own Buy Item Right Now / Submit Bid / Bid Amount / Bid History buttons as cards, Confirm Purchase and Confirm Bid as a green Confirm and a red Cancel, Manage Auctions and Your Bids as listing cards with Sold and Expired badges, and Create Auction with your inventory drawn so you can pick the item. It is still Hypixel's real menu: each click you make is sent as the same single click on the same slot, only when you click and only if that slot still holds what was drawn; bid and price signs are Hypixel's own. Pages change without a flash: the previous page stays drawn until the next one has loaded. Menus it does not recognize (Auction Stats, Bid History) stay Hypixel's own; hold the Hypixel Menu key (Left Alt by default) or press Hypixel menu in the header to see Hypixel's. On by default; part of the Auction House Browser toggle
- Bazaar Browser — one switch, one Bazaar screen for browsing and for Hypixel's own Bazaar. Categories (Farming, Mining, Combat, Woods & Fishes, Oddities) are tabs across the top beside a search box; down the left is Recent, the products you opened or searched for most recently on this SkyBlock profile, newest first, one click from opening again; under it is your inventory, so you can list what you carry: with Hypixel's Bazaar open a click on an item there is the same click on that item in Hypixel's menu (which opens its product page to sell it or make a sell offer), and with no menu open it opens that product like a click in the list; anything the Bazaar does not sell is dimmed and does nothing; along the bottom are only Sell Inventory Now, Sell Sacks Now and Manage Orders. Browsing is drawn from the live Bazaar data and needs no menu open: a category lists its products under Hypixel's own groups (Wheat & Seeds, Gemstones...), each with its real icon, instant buy and sell price, margin and seven-day volume, and the Buy, Sell, Margin and 7d vol headings sort by that column; typing searches every category at once. Clicking a product opens it on Hypixel. With a Booster Cookie the screen sends Hypixel's own /bz with the product's name and shows the product (prices, the top buy orders and sell offers, and its four trade buttons filling in) until Hypixel's page arrives in the same place; with Follow-up Click on (the default) and exactly one search result being that product, it clicks that result once for you as the follow-up to your click, so you land on the product page - with none or several matches, or Follow-up Click off, the results show with the product highlighted and you click it. At the Bazaar NPC without a Cookie, each click makes the one menu click that leads toward the product (its category, then its group, then the product). The keybind opens the screen with no menu behind it, and like Hypixel's remote Bazaar only while your Booster Cookie is active (read from the tab list's Cookie Buff); without one, chat says so. /bz is always Hypixel's own command. The bottom buttons press Hypixel's real button when it is on the open page, and otherwise (remote, with a Cookie) send /bz and press it as the follow-up to your click. Hide HUDs in Bazaar (on by default) hides every HUD - bars, map, timers, overlays - while the Bazaar is open. The scoreboard (the Custom Scoreboard, or the vanilla sidebar with it off) stays up over the Bazaar backdrop whatever Hide In Menus says, and the panel narrows to sit beside it (panels under 400 wide are not squeezed: then the panel wins and the board is drawn under it). The colors follow the mod menu's theme (or the Bazaar's own Screen Theme; Inventory Theme there gives the older look, the Inventory Theme's Amber, Dark or Light). Track My Orders reads your orders whenever Manage Orders is open (read only).
- Order Helper (was Bazaar Order Helper; sections Bazaar and Auction House) — (killer560, 2026-11-02; SkyHanni's Bazaar Order Helper; off by default) in Hypixel's Manage Orders menu (Your Bazaar Orders, or Co-op Bazaar Orders) each order's slot is colored: green when it is 100% filled or has items or coins to claim, red when it has expired, yellow when another player's order beats it (undercut or outbid) - a buy order below the best buy order, or a sell offer above the cheapest sell offer, by the mod's Bazaar prices (the same data the Bazaar Browser uses, fetched as soon as the menu opens even with the browser off). An order at the top of its side is not colored. Hovering an order adds a line saying which (and the better price), plus how old the Bazaar prices are; with no prices, or prices older than 15 minutes, only the green filled state shows. Highlight Outbid and Highlight Filled turn each color off, Outbid Color and Filled Color set them (the alpha is how strong the fill is; the outline is solid). In the Bazaar Browser's Manage Orders page the same colors tint the order rows. Draw only: nothing is clicked or sent. In Items Highlight Auction House (default ON, under the helper's switch; killer560, 2026-11-08): in Hypixel's Manage Auctions and Your Bids menus a listing whose lore says "Status: Sold!" or "Status: Ended!" (something to claim) gets the Filled Color, and one that says "Status: Expired!" gets the Expired Color. There is no underbid check there. Draw only.
  - *Hypixel's own Bazaar, reskinned* (the same switch: turning the Bazaar Browser on or off turns this on or off too, since 2026-10-11) — Hypixel's real Bazaar (the Bazaar NPC, or /bz) opens as the Bazaar screen above instead of the chest: the main page is the browsing view, group pages and search results are product rows with buy and sell prices (and margin and weekly volume from the live data), the product page is the product with live prices over big Buy Instantly, Sell Instantly, Create Buy Order and Create Sell Offer cards, the amount, price and confirm steps of instant buys, buy orders and sell offers are cards showing Hypixel's own text, Manage Orders is your live order list (filled, price, total, claimable, and whether each is still the top order), and Go Back is the Back button in the header. Hovering anything shows the real item's full tooltip. It is still Hypixel's real menu: each click you make is sent as the same click on the same slot, only when you click and only if that slot still holds what was drawn, so buying, selling, orders, claiming, canceling and the custom amount signs all work exactly as before. Changing page does not flash: until Hypixel's next page has arrived, the page you were on stays exactly as it was (and cannot be clicked), and nothing in the header, tabs, recents or bottom bar moves. Screens it does not recognize (Bazaar Settings, History, Confirm Instant Buy) stay Hypixel's own. Hold the Hypixel Menu key (Left Alt by default) or press Hypixel menu in the header to see Hypixel's own menu. On by default.
- Listing Helper — on Hypixel's Create BIN Auction menu, shows the lowest BIN among similar listings with a similarity percentage and copies lowest-minus-one-coin to your clipboard. Never clicks or types into Hypixel's menu
- Party Dungeon Data — teammates running this mod share room secrets, rooms and doors found, mimic/prince/bat kills and M7 dragon spawns over the mod's own relay, feeding the map, score calculator and HUDs. The format is an open spec (relay repo PROTOCOL.md)
- Cross-Mod Bridge — connects to Devonian's and Odin's party sockets so teammates running those mods share data with you too, and your finds reach them. Skips any mod you already have installed, only ever sends your own name, logs in through Mojang's normal handshake. On by default, like every sharing setting; each mod can be switched off. It never connects to NoammAddons' socket (its owner asked us not to, November 2026): if you run NoammAddons yourself, Party Interop reads what NoammAddons' own connection learned straight from it, and NoammAddons teammates' rooms, doors and score flags still reach you through the dungeon map and their party-chat announcements
- Team Melody HUD — live Melody terminal progress for every teammate in P3, from teammates on this mod, Odin users (via the bridge) and your own read-only tracker, which also shares your progress (Share My Progress stays on the tab with the HUD off; the Scale slider shows only with the HUD on). Since 2026-11-01: a teammate on this mod or Odin is drawn as their own Melody board (the target column, the moving pane in the active row, the rows already done and each row's button), and one who only posts progress in party chat ("Melody 1/3 - 33%", "Melody 67%") as a bar. A finished Melody reads 100% (your mod tells teammates when you finish; for anyone else their "activated a terminal!" line while their Melody is showing does it), and a player is dropped when the P3 section changes or after 15 s with no update. Share My Progress is off by default and shares whatever Party Dungeon Data and the relay data switch are set to.
- Teammate rooms on the map — rooms and doors your teammates found show on the dungeon map before you reach them, dimmed until you see them yourself (display only, never used by pathfinding)
- Inventory Theme — re-skins chest and inventory menus, and your hotbar, as vanilla's own GUI recolored: the beveled panel (light top-left, dark bottom-right edges), each slot's square (vanilla's sunken bevel in Vanilla; in every other theme a one-unit outline in the theme's light shade on all four sides, so every slot is outlined top and left too), the player-model window, the crafting arrow and the hotbar, all drawn by the mod. The drawer has no Inventory Theme row (the card's switch is that setting).
