Every overlay color is customizable (per-terminal highlights, Numbers' next/after-next/3rd tiers, Rubix left- vs right-click, Melody's board palette, panel background/border and label text) with a one-click Reset Colors. With Custom GUI on, the terminal is redrawn as a compact panel of only the squares that matter, and the real chest slots behind it are fully gone: hovering where one sits shows no highlight or tooltip, and clicks and keys there (number keys, drop, pick block, shift-click) do nothing - only the panel's own squares click. Terminal titles are read with color codes and resource-pack glyphs removed, and a terminal the solver cannot read is written to the log once (its title and board), for reporting. The tab's **Terminal QoL** section (both builds, every part off by default) holds Terminal Protection, **Hold To Click**, the **Terminal Drop Key** (the vanilla Drop key is moved to this key or mouse button while a terminal is open, so Q cannot throw your weapon; bound to a mouse button, pressing it in a terminal clicks the slot under the mouse the way the Drop key does, since 2026-11-01), Melody Keys, the terminal and Melody GUI scales and Hide Completion. The solver tracks every terminal with Inventory Theme on too (until 2026-11-01 Inventory Theme's chest background stopped it from ever seeing a terminal, so the solver, Custom GUI and Auto Terminals never showed), and Inventory Theme stands down on a terminal under Custom GUI. **Hold To Click** (both builds, off by default; 2026-10-26): hold left click in a terminal and the slot under the mouse is clicked again and again, like holding the drop key - your own press is the first click, then nothing for Hold First Delay (250 ms by default, 0-1000), then at most one click every Hold Repeat (75 ms by default, 50-500). It never picks a slot for you and only clicks the one you are pointing at, and only when the solver marks it correct right now: Numbers only the next number, Rubix in the direction its label says, never a wrong slot (a terminal resets on a wrong click), and a slot whose last click the server has not answered yet is not clicked again. On Melody it clicks the row button under the mouse once, when the moving pane reaches the target column. It sends at most one click a tick, stands down while Auto Terminals is clicking that terminal, and needs the solver on. In the cheat build, Auto Terminals has: **Melody Lookahead** (0-2, shown as "Melody Lookahead: 2 (clicks 3)"): after a proper Melody click on an edge row, that many more rows are clicked right behind it, so 0 clicks only the proper row and 2 clicks all three (a saved value from before 2026-10-26, when the count included the proper click, keeps its old behavior); **First Click Delay** (0-300 ms, 300 by default, since 2026-11-03; both modes): how long after a terminal's contents settle Auto Terminals waits before its first click; it replaced the old fixed 500 ms opening settle, and later clicks are unchanged (Stream Safe's hand still sets off during the wait); **Stream Safe** (off by default; replaced the 2026-10-26 Fake Cursor switch): with it on, auto clicks come from a drawn mouse pointer - the Windows arrow at the real pointer's size, the real pointer hidden - that starts at the center when the terminal opens and works the board like a hand: it picks the next slot the way a person does (the nearest one, sometimes the one after, or a row or column sweep that changes direction, a habit rolled per terminal; Numbers keeps its order and reads ahead to the next number), waits a reaction time (varied round **Reaction Time**, 60-400 ms, 130 by default, now and then a longer hesitation), then moves on a curve whose bend varies for a Fitts-law time that varies move to move, so a near slot is reached quickly and a far one takes longer (**Cursor Speed** 50-300%), sometimes overshooting past the slot and coming back (**Overshoot Chance**, 18%), sometimes landing on the slot's edge or the next slot and correcting (**Miss Chance**, 6%), on a long move sometimes stopping part way for a second look. Since 2026-11-01 it moves like a hand that does not stop to click: it arrives still gliding, the click goes out a moment after the pointer is on the slot for good (not once it has stopped), the pointer runs on a little and settles inside the slot, and the next move bends out of that motion in an arc rather than starting from rest; the reaction pause is taken only from rest, and the hand sets off during the terminal's opening settle, so the first click is no longer late. In Melody it goes straight to the row buttons (no reaction, miss or overshoot) as soon as the board opens and follows the active row, so it is already on the button when the row comes due. A wrong click is never sent. A slot needing more than one click (a Rubix pane at 2) is clicked again after a double-click-like gap without the pointer leaving it. The Min/Max delays do not apply with it on, so solves are slower (see the testkit report for times). With it off: the Min/Max delays, no pointer is drawn, and **Hide Mouse Cursor** hides the real pointer while clicks land. In both modes a Rubix pane needing several clicks is finished before the next pane; **Hover Terminals**: a correct slot under the mouse is clicked for you; **Hover Delay** (0-300 ms, since 2026-11-01) is the least time between two hover clicks, not a wait on the slot, so the first hover clicks at once. **Hover Melody** (on with Hover Terminals): rest the mouse on a Melody row button and it is clicked once, when the moving pane reaches the target column, with no mouse movement needed. **Terminal Sounds** (Terminal QoL, off by default; Odin's): a chosen sound (the Click Sounds presets or your imported files, Default plays nothing) for each correct click you make and another when you finish a terminal, with a volume slider; the click sound needs the Terminal Solver on. **Terminal Click Path** (Terminal QoL, off by default; Athen's Terminal Click): after a terminal closes your clicks show as numbered dots joined by lines for 4 seconds, green for a right slot and red for a wrong one; needs the Terminal Solver on. **Click Order** (cheat build, Auto Terminals, off Stream Safe): First, Random, Closest or Furthest decides which correct slot Auto Terminals clicks next (Closest and Furthest measure from the slot clicked last); Numbers keeps its fixed order. **Queue Terms** (cheat build, own switch in the Auto Terminals tab, off by default; Nebulune's): in Panes, Rubix, Numbers, Starts With and Select a click of yours on a correct slot that comes while an earlier click still waits for the server's answer is held and sent when the answer lands, one per update (a Numbers click made one ahead waits until its number is next); it paces through the same one-click-per-tick gate as Auto Terminals and stands down while Auto Terminals is clicking.
- Termism — a practice mode that generates fake terminal puzzles (every real type, including a real timed Melody puzzle) so you can drill them without a real dungeon run, with Auto Terminals also usable here on the cheat build to test it risk-free (with Custom GUI on, as before; with it off the board is yours to read). With Auto Terminals' Stream Safe on, a practice board is played exactly as a real terminal is: the human cursor is drawn and glides, arcs, misses and overshoots to each slot, clicks only once it has arrived, waits the same opening settle, picks slots in the same human order and stays on a Rubix pane that needs two clicks, and Melody rests on the row's button and bursts ahead by Melody Skip Mode and Melody Lookahead (the burst dropped once the pane is off the target). Melody Skip Mode and Lookahead apply in Termism with Stream Safe off too. The menu has no explanatory line, and Random and Done have a little more room around the terminal buttons. Resizing the window keeps the current board and its timer.
- Full Block — **cheat build only.** Expands the clickable area of levers, buttons, chests, and Wither Essence blocks so real secrets are easier to click, with a master on/off toggle; each block type has a Shape setting (buttons: Flat / Full / Custom, the rest: Full / Custom), and Custom adds Width, Height and Length sliders from normal size to a full block - see [Which jar do I download?](#installing) below.
- Simon Says — highlights the correct button(s) on the F7/M7 boss-fight Simon Says device with numbered, colored boxes (next = green, then orange, then red), tracks party members' progress from their chat announcements (compatible with Odin/QUOI's own progress messages too), and can block a real click on the wrong button (Prevent Misclicks - hold Shift to override). Manual skips (2026-11-01): after a skip attempt (two or more start-button presses), a press on the second light is let through until your first click lands, and the highlight then follows the light you pressed; a light that lights again before any click marks the skip as landed and drops the first light; and a press the device takes on a light the solver did not have (a Shift override) ends the first reveal's corrections, so the next round is read fresh, light by light. The **cheat** build additionally has a Trigger Bot, Auto Start for the real "skip" trick (configurable clicks, exact tick spacing), and Auto Solve with Rotate / No Rotate modes (No Rotate never moves your camera, but turns your body to each button for a tick before clicking it, because a click your look misses is dropped by GrimAC as RotationPlace) and a Timer Target (11-13s from the first start-button click, same as Hypixel's own device timer, with up to ±250ms variance; it plans the device's real rounds from the first click - 4 rounds, 10 clicks on Hypixel since SkyBlock 0.27.2, 5 on a server that still runs the old device such as p3sim, which each server's own devices confirm). Rotate mode adds Stim (between rounds the aim quickly retraces the whole round it just did, first button to last, and comes back to the first button; look only, it never clicks; the Stim Buttons count was removed on 2026-11-01) and a Momentum slider (Off to 100%, default Off: the click goes out once the crosshair is on the button face instead of fully settled, the camera carries part of its speed into the next turn and heads for the next button as soon as a click is sent; off, the aim is exactly as before), plus Auto Restart SS (restarts the device when it breaks), a Restart keybind (it does nothing while Auto Simon Says as a whole is off), and a Trigger Bot delay slider (ms from aiming at the right button to the click, rounded up to whole 50 ms game ticks). All of that lives in its own red Auto Simon Says section directly under Simon Says - see [Which jar do I download?](#installing) below.
- Tick Timers — real countdowns for Maxor's start, Necron's drop, Goldor's start and damage tick, and Storm, driven off the real boss chat lines and counted in server ticks (timings follow SkyBlock 0.27.2's faster boss pacing). **Storm Line** is ONE line in P2: a countdown to Storm's lightning from his opening line, then, once the lightning goes off, the purple pad timer (Storm's 1-second pad cycle), then after the first crush the same timer for yellow. **Goldor**'s "Tick:" reads 0 on the tick Goldor hits: after Start runs out it stands at 3.0 until Goldor's opening line, then reads 0 60 ticks after that line and every 60 ticks after (Goldor's real phase, read off killer560's mask procs, 2026-11-05); on Hypixel your own health dropping never moves it, only two mask or Phoenix procs a whole number of hits apart, and the count is held to the server's own game time, so a lag spike cannot leave it early or late; Tick Timers and every other HUD element stay drawn inside Hypixel's terminal menus (other containers still hide them). Storm Pads (P2) outlines the purple pad, or all three, in a color you pick Storm's one line shows **Purple** as ONE countdown of 62 server ticks that starts at his lightning line and ends at 0 (NoammAddons' PY timer), then goes away until the first crush starts the Yellow pad cycle (since 2026-11-03). The HUD box is anchored on its center: when its text gets wider or narrower it grows or shrinks equally on both sides, and the HUD editor places it by its center (an old saved position is read as a center once).
- Split Timers — per-segment run splits for every floor, announced in chat and shown on a HUD list in one fixed layout: **Blood Open** (run start until the blood door opens), **Watcher** (blood door until the last blood mob dies - the Watcher's "You have proven yourself" line), **Portal Entry** (from there until the boss's first line) and **Boss Entry** (the sum of those three); a line; on F7/M7 **Maxor**, **Storm**, **Terminals**, **Goldor**, **Necron**, **Dragons** (M7 only - Phase 5 starts on Necron's death line or the Wither King's first line, whichever comes first) and **Boss** (the whole boss fight); a line; then **Total** and **Lag** (how much of the run was server lag, in seconds). On other floors the boss is one row named after the boss (Bonzo, Scarf, The Professor, Thorn, Livid, Sadan), which is the boss time itself. Every time reads the same way, on the HUD and in chat: the real time, then the time without server lag in parentheses - "Blood Open: 22.39s (19.59s)" - while **Lagless Times** is on (default). If Mort's opening line is missed, the clock starts one second after "Starting in 1 second." The end-of-run chat summary lists the same rows, and every segment name in chat (the summary and each "Watcher took 47.50s!" line as a split ends) is written in that row's HUD color. **Counting** picks Every 0.01s (default) or Ticks, which rounds every split time to the nearest 0.05s. Watcher Move, Core Entry and the M7 Phase 5 dragon/relic lines are optional extras around the layout. **Blood Rush Splits** (off by default; idea from the reference mod adm) times the blood rush room by room on its own HUD (Blood Rush Splits in the HUD editor): a rush room runs from the wither door into it opening (the first from Mort's line) to the door out of it opening, until "The BLOOD DOOR has been opened!". **Rush Style** Compact shows one row a room - the room's time, its key time (the door into it fully down to the key picked up; dark red when the key lay on the ground over 0.35 s) and, when key-in-hand to door-open took over 0.25 s, that door time; Detailed lists each room's Door Down, Last Mob (its key appearing), Pickup, Opened and Room Total. An Average row follows once the Blood door opens. Rooms are named from the map: where the room's key dropped, else the far side of the wither door into it; the room that led into Fairy is pink. **Rush To Chat** (off) sends the room times as one chat line when the Blood door opens. Times follow Lagless Times. **Core Entry Times** (F7/M7) times each player from "The Core entrance is opening!" to the first moment they stand inside the core (the closed room in the middle of the Goldor arena, x 42-68, y 106-155, z 54-119); anyone already inside when the line arrives reads 0.00s, a dead player (a ghost that can fly through the core) is never timed, and once the Necron fight starts anyone who never got in reads "Did not enter" and is never the slowest, with the slowest optionally sent to your chat, party chat or the bottom of the HUD; **Show With Splits Off** keeps Core Entry and its HUD rows going with Split Timers itself off On the HUD every row's name sits in one left column and its time in one right-aligned column (the Core Entry and P5 lists align the same way).
- Terminal Timers — Odin-style F7/M7 P3 timing: "Panes solved in 3.21s!" after each terminal you complete, (section | phase) times added to every terminal/device/lever completion line with a per-section summary when the core opens, and the Simon Says whole-device time **Terminal Breakdown** (a switch in the Terminal Timers tab, on once Terminal Timers is on; Devonian's): when the core opens at the end of P3, one chat line per player with how many terminals, devices and levers each did, most first.
- Mask Invincibility Timers — automatic active/cooldown timers for Spirit Mask, Bonzo's Mask, and Phoenix Pet, detected off the real "saved your life" chat lines. The **cheat** build additionally has Auto Mask, its own red card beside Mask Invincibility (which it needs on, for the procs and cooldowns). Mode picks Keybind or Full Auto (the default, and what an older Auto Swap setting loads as): in Keybind the Swap Key (unbound until you set it; a key or mouse button) puts on a mask when you wear none or swaps to your other mask when you do; Full Auto swaps once the mask you wear, or Phoenix, procs, and never without a proc. Swap Order picks what comes next: Mask First (the default) tries your other mask (Spirit before Bonzo when none is worn), then Phoenix; Phoenix First summons Phoenix first, then the masks. Nothing on cooldown, already worn, not owned (the Spirit / Bonzo / Phoenix switches) or already summoned (Hypixel's "You summoned your Phoenix!" and Autopet lines) is picked, and with nothing left it says so in chat once. Dungeons Only (on by default) keeps it out of everything but the Catacombs; Boss Only (off) lets it act only in the floor's boss fight, never the clear. A mask goes on through the /stats equipment menu, Phoenix by your rod's Autopet rule or the /pets menu (Phoenix Route), one interaction a tick, never under a screen except the menu the swap opened; a proc that lands while a screen is open swaps as soon as it closes (within 5 seconds). Swap Step Delay (3 to 20 ticks, default 7) is the pause between each step; an older millisecond value loads as ms / 50, rounded **Mask Keybinds** (legit, its own card): while you have the /stats menu (Your Equipment and Stats) open, the Equip Mask Key equips a mask with one click in that menu per press, so pressing again swaps back - Spirit First or Bonzo First, from the masks in your inventory right now (the worn one is never a choice); the only thing that stops a swap is the mask's proc cooldown, said once in chat ("Spirit Mask on cooldown: 12s"). Dungeons Only (on by default) and Boss Only. It never sends a command or opens a menu.
- Sharp Shooter (i4) — a Solver that marks every hit target green and dots the between-column spots to aim at for predictions. The **cheat** build adds Auto i4: shoots each lit target (Rotate / No Rotate, adjustable rotation speed; No Rotate keeps your camera still and turns only your body to each shot for a tick), Terminator (with predictions) or Machine Gun Shortbow (aims each block, fires Rapid Fire at the start), auto swap to your bow, and Auto Mask with a Bonzo / Spirit / Phoenix order (in development)
- i4 Leap Out — **cheat build only**, in the Sharp Shooter (i4) tab. Leaps when your i4 device finishes (or on left-click at pre4) to a chosen class, player, or the Melody player with a backup target; Prevent Inputs blocks input during i4 and the leap
- 0 Ping Dungeon Breaker — **cheat build only.** While holding a real Dungeon Breaker item with charges left, insta-mines the exact block you're looking at the instant you start mining it, instead of waiting on your real connection's ping; obsidian is left to the server, which digs it over time, so clicking it never leaves a hole on your screen. **Don't break secrets** (off by default, on the Dungeon Breaker tab): the breaker - Zero Ping, Breaker Aura and Auto Routes breaker nodes - never breaks a secret block (chest, trapped chest, lever, Wither Essence or Redstone Key skull). Whatever the setting, the breaker never breaks anything inside a Fairy room; puzzle rooms are not affected. **Dungeons Only** (off by default): Zero Ping acts only inside a Catacombs run
- Dungeon Map — a HUD copy of the real held dungeon map, on both builds showing only what the dungeon map in your hotbar shows: the rooms you have opened, and the gray "?" rooms beside them, unnamed - it never draws a room the map has not shown, even when the world has it loaded. A door the map shows gray (into a room nobody has opened) is drawn gray, as on the real map. The map item is read at the corner Hypixel draws each floor at (Entrance, Floors 1-3 with 18-pixel rooms; Floor 4 and 5-7 with 16), so a stray green run on the map cannot shift the whole reading. Rooms are colored by type (brown normal, magenta puzzle, orange trap, yellow miniboss, pink fairy, red blood, green entrance, gray unexplored), corridors flush against the rooms, multi-tile rooms merged, and the room marks (check, cross, "?") are Hypixel's own, copied pixel for pixel off the hotbar map where Hypixel drew them (with no map item to copy from - the sim, p3sim - a built-in copy of the same shapes). **Custom Checkmark Sprite** (off by default) swaps them for the mod's own outlined marks, or (Sprite: Your Own) for PNGs you draw - green.png, cleared.png, failed.png and unopened.png in the folder Open Sprite Folder opens, any size up to 64x64; a missing one uses the mod's mark. Rooms you have not opened use NoammAddons' colors: a gray question-mark room (and the door into one) is the Unopened gray, 65,65,65, and the Blood Room behind its blood door is drawn in the Blood color darkened the way NoammAddons darkens it (darker twice, about half). On Funny Map a room you have not opened keeps its own color, the same before the run starts and after the doors drop (it used to turn about half as dark the moment Hypixel handed the map over). **Darken Unexplored** (0 to 0.9, 0 by default) darkens all of them further; a saved 0.05, the old default, moves to 0 once. Room names and checkmarks sit inside the room's own box: an L-shaped room (Chambers, Altar...) is labeled on its row of two, never on the empty corner of its square. The room you are standing in is drawn opened, all of it, the moment you are inside its walls, without waiting for the hotbar map to redraw. The mimic's room turns a faint red once you have killed the mimic in it and stays red for the rest of the run. You are a green arrow and each teammate a white one (**Show Teammates**, on by default); a teammate out of render distance is placed from the map item's markers, matched to names in the order the tab list shows the party, and glides to each new marker instead of jumping; **Recolor by Class** colors teammates' arrows by class and puts a faint ring of their class color around their head with Player Heads; **Player Heads** (shown under Show Teammates while it is on, off by default) draws you and your teammates as your skins' faces instead, each head turned so its top points where that player faces, on this map and the Interactive Map (anyone whose skin is not known yet keeps the arrow); **Player Marker Size** (0.5x-3x) sizes arrows and heads on both maps; every arrow is inside a solid outline that contrasts with its own color so it stands out on any room. **Names Holding Leap** (off by default) writes every teammate's name under their marker while you hold a Spirit Leap or Infinileap. **Mark Reported Rooms** and **Color Rooms by Type** are on by default. Wither doors are plain black and the door into the Fairy room is drawn in the Fairy room's pink; **Wither Door Outline** (off by default) puts an outline round each locked wither door, with **Wither Door Outline Color** (amber by default) and **Wither Door Outline Thickness** (1-4 px, 2 by default) shown under it while it is on. On Funny Map a door is the same color as the rooms it joins (it was darkened a second time and read darker than both). The map is sized by scrolling over it in the HUD editor (there is no Room Size slider; a Room Size from an older config is folded into the map's HUD scale once). Its outline is the same square for the whole run and on every floor; inside it the grid is scaled from the floor you are on - Entrance 4x4, Floor 1 4x5, Floors 2-3 5x5, Floor 4 6x5, Floors 5-7 6x6, Master Mode the same as its floor number - so nothing re-fits or shrinks as rooms are found, and a small floor's boss arena is never drawn as rooms. In the dungeon sim, which has no hotbar map, the map shows the floor that was built (a sim holding a dungeon map in slot 9 is read like Hypixel's). **Map Theme**: Real Map (Hypixel's colors, the default), Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean and Viridian (worked out from the mod menu's presets, each room type keeping its own hue), Match Menu Theme (follows whatever theme the mod menu is in) or Custom. Room colors exist only under Custom: **New Custom Scheme** makes a scheme from the colors on the map now and opens the scheme editor - a sample floor drawn in the scheme's colors with every room type, an unexplored room, a wither door, the blood door and checkmarks, beside its name and its ten colors (each a picker), Reset to Real Map and Delete (click twice). Every scheme is saved; under Custom the Scheme setting picks one and Edit Scheme reopens it. Map Background and Border Color are pickers of their own. The untouched Border Color (white) and Wither Door Outline Color (amber) follow the menu theme's accent; a color you pick stays. **Extra Info Overlay** (Map Extras) adds two centered rows under the map, laid out like NoammAddons': Secrets found / needed for S+ / the floor's total and Crypts, then Score, Deaths and the bonus kills as M (Mimic, Floor 6 and up), P (Prince) and B (the Murkbat bat), each green when done and red when not, B with a count when more than one player got theirs - at half the font size, so one screen pixel per font pixel at GUI scale 2, never wider than the map; it works with Score Calculator off. In game the Split Timers never draw on top of the map or its Extra Info. **Cheat build:** **Funny Map** (off by default, at the bottom of the tab) draws every room the mod has scanned, not just what the hotbar map shows, with names and real colors - the ones you have not been in darkened only by Darken Unexplored. A room you have never entered carries Hypixel's gray "?" (Room Labels: Checkmarks; with names on, its name is a dimmer gray), a room you have entered but not cleared carries nothing, and a cleared room its white check (green when all secrets), so the three read apart without any room changing color; while it is on the tab hides Predictive Rooms and Special Room Guess, which draw nothing on it (their saved values are kept for when it is off); with it on, **Show Mimic Room** (on) tints the mimic's room from the start, from its trapped chest, in **Mimic Room Color** (20% red by default; its alpha is how strongly it blends). Without Funny Map the cheat map is the normal map. The Interactive Map always draws the whole scanned floor. **Map deductions** (no setting, on every map): a room whose name is known but whose tiles are not all drawn is completed in its own color, like the rest of the room, when exactly one placement of its shape fits what the map shows (Pit, 4 long with 3 tiles drawn and a wall above, gets its 4th tile below; never a guess while two placements fit, over a tile a room is drawn on, or across a seen wall or door); a slot still blank stays blank (no gray "?" rooms); and a fully known room with no door seen and only one side it could open to gets a door drawn there, in the door color. What the map then really shows replaces it. The map item map reasons only from what the map item shows. Worked out only when the map data changes.
- Secret Waypoints — real, preloaded per-room secret positions (chests, items, wither essence, bats, redstone keys) from the same public room database NoammAddons uses, shown for the room you're in only. NoammAddons' colors by default (chest magenta, item blue, bat green, essence black, key red), each box sized to its object (or a full block), drawn through walls (toggleable), with an optional name label above each one. A waypoint disappears as soon as you take that secret - click the chest/essence/key, pick up the item (or a teammate does; you can be well away from it, even out of the room, by the time the pickup arrives) or kill the bat. Levers are waypoints too (found live in the room, in their own color), and a lever's goes as soon as you click that lever and stays gone for the run; clicking any other lever hides nothing else. Levers that are no secret are never shown: Red Blue's two in the cage nook, the sixteen on the cauldrons at the top of Pressure Plates, the wall lever on red wool under a crypt in Mines (the floor lever below it is the real one), and none at all in Water Board, whose levers are the puzzle's. Walking into another room redraws the waypoints at once, so the room you left stops showing its own. Two extra toggles, Show Crypts and Show Princes (both off by default, each with its own color), box the crypt tombs and the prince's golden crypt in the room you're in, with the same style, names and through-walls as the secrets. A box covers the tomb as it stands: the stairs or blocks it stands on as well as its lid (a prince's box takes in the whole slab lid its gold or lantern sits in, not just the gold), up to the top of its tallest block (half a block over a slab lid, a whole block where the lid has a raised row). Hide Crypts After 5 and Hide Princes After 1 (both on by default) stop drawing crypts once the tab list counts 5 crypts, and princes once "A Prince falls" has been said in the run. The room database only knows how MANY crypts a room has, and a crypt's undead and the prince do not exist until the tomb is blown, so these are found from the room's own blocks: a crypt is the lid of smooth stone slabs, or Prison Cell's single row of slabs on polished andesite (with the prince rules it gives the database's crypt count exactly in 126 of the 134 captured rooms, so expect the odd decorative slab panel to be boxed and the odd tomb to be missed; a slab staircase, and slabs laid flat on the floor like Teleport Maze's, are never a crypt), a prince is gold set beside a smooth stone slab or a sea lantern walled in polished andesite, with nothing on top of it (so Pirate's carpeted lantern tables are not princes), and a crypt with a gold block against it is the prince. Once a tomb is blown its waypoint goes the same tick, whether or not you kill what came out. A part of the room your client has not loaded (or has let go of) never counts as empty: a crypt there is not judged opened, and a lever there keeps the waypoint it had when it was last seen
- Predictive Rooms (a setting on the Dungeon Map tab, with Special Room Guess under it, not a card of its own) — names a room on the Dungeon Map before you enter it, from what you have seen of it. Every other tick (every tick, with two rounds of extra rays round the doorway, while you can see into an unopened room) the mod casts rays from your eyes across your view (the game's own line-of-sight test) and records the first block each one hits inside an unopened room; it never reads any other block, so nothing behind a wall, round a corner or under a floor is ever used. Those blocks are compared with every room in the room library that ships with the mod (the sim's captured rooms), at all four turns and every placement that fits the floor and does not overlap an opened room; a room/turn/placement that disagrees with more than a few percent of what you saw is ruled out. Once a tile has 20 seen blocks, every placement still standing is the same room and each has matched at least 15 of them (a tenth of a second's look through a doorway, and named from up to 27 blocks back from it; the parts of a puzzle that change between runs, such as Water Board's board, never count toward those matches), the map shows that room's name and color on the tiles every remaining placement covers and leaves the tiles only some of them cover blank (no gray "?"; a room whose turn you cannot tell yet shows only the part you can be sure of, and fills in as you see more). Two different rooms still possible: nothing. A predicted room keeps its type's color but its name (and secrets line) is drawn gray, grayer than a room you have been in, until you enter it. Before the run starts Hypixel has given no map yet: the room you stand in is the start room, never looked into and never covered by a predicted room, so a room you see early by digging the green room's gate is named on its own tile, and the moment the map arrives every prediction is decided again against it, so nothing predicted stays over a room the map shows opened. Doors are never predicted; the map's doors stay the held map's. A room the map already names is left alone, so on the Interactive Map it only adds rooms the world scan has not identified; with Funny Map on, the Dungeon Map draws no predictions and the tab hides both rows. **Special Room Guess** (same tab) splits an unopened room the held map shows is one tile across - every side the floor's edge or an opened room it is not joined to - into darkened stripes of the special rooms it can still be: Miniboss while no miniboss room is opened, Trap while no trap room is opened, Puzzle while fewer puzzle rooms are opened than the tab list's "Puzzles: (N)" (rooms Predictive Rooms has named count as found); when only one is left it is filled solid in that room's color, so the miniboss room turns yellow once the trap and every puzzle are accounted for. An unopened room whose only door is the blood door is filled as the Blood Room. On the legit map only (the cheat map already knows). Both on by default, both builds
- Blood Camp — tracks the Watcher and his blood mobs in the Blood Room on every floor by their real skull skins, predicting where each mob is about to land and counting down until it's vulnerable. It draws what NoammAddons' Blood Camp helper draws, using the same prediction: the trip starts at the skull's spot in the wall and the landing spot is 16.1 blocks along the skull's direction of travel for the Watcher's first wave, 11.9 after it. Over each landing spot there's a 1x1x1 outline box from 1.5 to 2.5 blocks above it (2.5 px, magenta, turning green once your ping is longer than the time left). A cyan **Spawn Line** (2 px by default, width 1-10) runs from the skull's position 2 blocks above the stand, drawn where it is that frame, to the landing spot. The seconds left show with a shadow at **Timer Text Scale** 2 by default, one decimal and no unit, in **Timer Text Color** (white by default, a color picker with a reset). All of it shows through walls from the skull's first move until it's removed. **Kill Sound** (on) plays once per mob, on the tick the Trigger Bot is to hit it - its kill timer moved by Trigger Offset (one tick after the box turns green by default), never before the Kill Popup's KILL once the Watcher has greeted the room - and only for a skull that really flew out (one shuffling on the wall makes no sound); mobs due together share one sound. Its sound is Arrow Hit by default (click to cycle) at **Sound Volume** (0.5). The old Countdown Start Sound, which played as each mob started to spawn, is gone. Mobs that reach the same moment together make one sound, not a burst. The cheat build's Trigger Bot and Aura live in their own **Auto Blood Camp** tab. A lag spike that stops the skull's movement for a moment no longer loses the line: the trip, its path and its countdown are kept and pick up again when the movement resumes; only a skull that already arrived, or one gone quiet for 5 s, starts a new trip. A server stall no longer runs the countdowns ahead of the mobs: every countdown, the Kill Popup and the kill timer count server ticks (the time the server stood still is left out, measured from its per-tick pings), and a skull moved by an absolute position packet (what a server sends for a big catch-up step) is tracked like any other move, so the line and box appear under lag too. The Kill Popup's fast-Watcher kill time is per floor, NoammAddons' table: 480 server ticks after the Watcher's first line in the Entrance, 600 on F1, 500 on F2-F4, 470 on F5-F6, 420 on F7 (Master Mode the same). Silverfish, the Watcher himself, his two Watchful Eyes (by their name or as the mobs spawned with him), Primal Fears (the Great Spook's Primal Fear and its six forms) and what you summon yourself (Spirit Sceptre bats, the Guided Sheep, Witherborn's wither) are never blood mobs, and a mob name tag counts only inside the Blood Room. A skull is tracked from anywhere in the Watcher's room, so a mob sent from the far wall while he is at the other one is no longer missed (it used to need the skull within 20 blocks of him, 2026-11-02); the room stays where the Watcher was last seen while he is out of your client's range, and a skull with a skin missing from the list is tracked too when it leaves one of the room's display cases. A blood mob is recognized the tick it appears: the room is checked every tick instead of every 5, and a trip's landing spot is kept after the server removes its skull, so the mob that appears there a tick later is still that trip's (2026-11-02). **Blood Camp Stats** (all off): **Mobs Left HUD** (every blood mob the camp still needs, alive or not spawned yet, read off The Watcher's boss bar since 2026-11-02: the bar is the share of the camp left, times the camp's size - 9 + the floor's number per the wiki, F7 16, corrected from the bar's own steps when a camp turns out a different size - rounded up, so it reads 1 only when one mob is left in the whole camp; with no Watcher bar in sight it shows ? until the Watcher says no more are coming, then the blood mobs it counts), **Mob Ticks HUD** (server ticks lost to slow kills: every tick from the kill timer to the first kill, then every tick at least one blood mob is alive on the floor - a mob on its way does not count - until "You have proven yourself"; a blood mob counts as dead from the server's death event, so a killed player-model mob stops counting at once instead of until Hypixel removes it, 2026-11-02), and **Camp Lag HUD** (the server's lag since the Watcher's greeting), one movable "Blood Camp Stats" HUD element, shown from the Watcher's greeting until the boss is entered (since 2026-11-02; the stats and Party Message are unaffected). **Party Message** sends "Blood Camp: N ticks lost to slow mob kills (Xs)" to party chat the moment the camp clears (the Watcher's "You have proven yourself", no longer at the end-of-run summary) if you camped the Blood Room; **Send For Any Class** sends it whoever camped (it is counted from any room the blood mobs are loaded in); **Include Camp Lag** adds the lag.
- Auto Blood Camp — **cheat build only**, its own tab next to Auto Trap (split out of the Blood Camp tab on 2026-10-07; same settings, same saved values). Both its automations only ever act on blood mobs - the mobs Blood Camp saw land at the end of a trip, or whose name tag names a Watcher mob, never any other mob, however close or in view - and a click only goes when nothing but blood mobs inside the Blood Room is anywhere along its line (a beam or an arrow carries on past its target), so a shot cannot hit a mob outside the room. Both only act while you are inside the Blood Room (the room tile the Watcher is in, or a room the map calls the Blood Room - a map that took the Blood Room for some other room no longer stops them); outside it Blood Camp still draws, but nothing clicks or turns. Neither ever acts through a wall: the Aura only goes for a mob a line from your eye reaches without meeting a block, and a click only goes when your look reaches the mob before any block (Blood Camp still draws the ones behind walls). Neither clicks faster than your weapon allows a full-damage hit: on Hypixel round(10 / (1 + Attack Speed / 100)) ticks apart (10 at 0 Attack Speed, 5 at 100; a shortbow rounds up), with Attack Speed read from the tab list's Attack Speed line (the Stats widget, or the dungeon's Skills and Stats widget), the last reading kept while the tab does not show it, and taken as the cap (100, 5 ticks) when it was never shown, since most players are at the cap and a click too early on Hypixel only wastes that click on the mob just hit (capped at 100); elsewhere the vanilla attack cooldown. **Trigger Bot** left-clicks a blood mob at its kill timer - the moment its box turns green and the Kill Sound plays - while your aim is roughly on its landing spot (the spot up through the drawn box) or on the mob - your look only has to pass within half a block of the box, or within 5 degrees of its center, on both the look the server last saw and this tick's - from any distance you can see it: an attack when a mob (player-model ones included) is in melee reach under your aim, otherwise the swing alone, which is what a real left click sends there. **Trigger Offset** (-10 to 10 ticks, +1 by default) moves that click: slid left (below 0) it clicks that many ticks before the kill timer, right (above 0) that many after it (flipped on 2026-11-01; a saved value keeps its click moment); the old Hit Lead does not carry over. With **Auto Detect Lag** (on) the kill timer allows for your measured ping, as the box does - the ping with any server stall taken out, so a lag spike no longer makes it click early at an empty spot; off, the offset counts from the countdown's own end. If a mob stands on the spot and the hit did no damage (no hurt flash and still alive one round trip plus 4 ticks later), or the click was a swing because the mob was not in reach yet and now is, it clicks again straight away, up to 4 more times, 2-4 ticks apart; a mob that was hit and is still alive is clicked again once its hurt flash is over, at your weapon's pace, until it dies (since 2026-11-02; it used to be clicked once). Nothing is attacked before the Kill Popup's kill timer either: once the Watcher has greeted the room, neither automation clicks until "KILL" shows (the Watcher's predicted move less Kill Popup Lead), moved by the same Trigger Offset (+1: one tick after it); a mob whose own countdown ended earlier is clicked then. Without a greeting (joined mid-blood) there is no kill timer to wait for. After that the Trigger Bot keeps going: any blood mob in the Blood Room that is not on its way to a landing spot - landed while you looked elsewhere, walked off its spot toward you, or was held back by the kill timer - is clicked the first tick your look meets it (plus the same retries on a miss, and again while it survives a hit), at your weapon's pace, so the next mob is taken as soon as it is under your aim. In the tab, Auto Detect Lag, Trigger Offset and Never Miss sit under a small red **Attack Speed** header below the Trigger Bot and Aura switches (Freecam Mode moved up under the switches), shown whenever either is on: Auto Detect Lag and Trigger Offset time the Aura's first click on a mob the same way as the Trigger Bot's. **Never Miss** (off) uses no mob timer (since 2026-11-03): no mob countdown and no Trigger Offset, but it never attacks before the Kill Popup's KILL (since 2026-11-04). From then it attacks a blood mob the tick after it spawns - a trip's mob on or near its landing spot, or any other blood mob in the room - as soon as it is under your look and in sight, and a mob it hit that is still alive is hit again at your weapon's pace for as long as your look is on it. While it is on, Trigger Offset is hidden in the tab and does nothing (its value is kept for when Never Miss goes off again; the menu search still finds it). **Mage Only** (off) lets both act only while the dungeon tab list says you are the Mage. **Aura** turns you toward the mob due next - only once its click moment is a reaction time plus the turn to it away (about 10-20 ticks; it no longer follows a mob across the room from the wall), staying on the mob it is turning to rather than jumping to a second one landing beside it, and following a target point that moves (the mob appearing on its spot) smoothly instead of snapping to it - its upper chest once it stands there, 1.5 blocks above the landing spot before - with a human-looking mouse movement: a reaction time first, then a turn that speeds up and slows down (longer for a bigger turn, never more than 26 degrees of yaw or 16 of pitch a tick), usually stopping a little short or a little past and correcting, a slight curve, a faint drift while resting, every step a whole number of mouse counts at your sensitivity, moved every frame rather than every tick. It also kills: once the mob it aims at is killable (the Trigger Bot's moment, or with Never Miss the tick it is there) it clicks it at your weapon's pace until it dies, at any range as long as nothing is in the way - with whatever you hold, the way your own click would: a Hypixel shortbow (its lore says "Shortbow") is a right click, anything else a left click (an attack when the mob is in melee reach under your look, otherwise the swing a mage weapon's beam fires on). The Trigger Bot clicks the same way. Between the hits that can land it also clicks the way someone spam-clicking would, at an uneven 7-10 clicks a second with the odd pause, but only well inside your weapon's cooldown (never within 2 ticks of the next hit, which still goes on the first tick it can), and only on Hypixel, where a hit inside the cooldown does nothing - and mobs already standing in the Blood Room when you come in (landed before you got there, or hit but still alive) are targets too, nearest to your aim first. Moving your mouse takes over at once: the Aura stops and waits until your mouse has been still for half a second. **Freecam Mode** (off, shown under the Aura switch) turns only your body while your camera stays where you point it; moving your mouse hands the aim straight back (body under the camera), and when nothing is left to aim at the body turns back under the camera the same way and the camera is released. Both act on Blood Camp's predictions, so Blood Camp itself must be on; the tab says so when it isn't.
- Auto Close Chest — closes a dungeon secret chest ("Chest"/"Large Chest"/"Trapped Chest"). **Close On Any Key** (every build, on by default, since featE-1108; own row since 2026-11-09): the chest opens normally and the first key or mouse press closes it, so you can read the loot first; the press is eaten. **Auto** (**cheat build only**, off by default, in a red cheat section at the bottom; it was the Auto value of the Close Mode row until 2026-11-09): closes the chest the moment it opens, before it is shown; Secret Aura counts a chest closed this way as opened, so it does not click it again, and Auto Secret leaves the room the tick it opens; the auto puzzles' reward chests, Auto Boulder and Auto Tic Tac Toe's secret still see the chest as opened. Old files migrate: mode AUTO becomes Auto ON (cheat build) plus Close On Any Key ON, mode CLICK becomes Auto OFF plus Close On Any Key ON. Off by default.
- Item Quality — one more tooltip line on a dungeon drop, "Item Quality: 47/50 (Floor 7)", read from the item's own baseStatBoostPercentage and item_tier. Off by default; the tooltip only, nothing about the item changes.
- Block Ender Pearls In Boss — in the F7/M7 boss phase, your own right click with an ender pearl in hand does nothing (no use packet, no swing) and an action-bar note says so, at most once a second. The mod's own pearls (Walk With Pearls, Auto Gfs, AP3) are never blocked, and neither are Auto Routes or Auto Clear. Both builds, default ON; boss phase is only detected on F7 and M7.
- Iced Mob Boxes — after you right-click an Ice Spray Wand, boxes exactly the mobs that show the freeze's ice blocks on their head (an ice block in the head slot, or an armor stand holding one by the head), for as long as the ice is there (through walls, box color setting). If no ice shows within 4 ticks of the click, it falls back to the living mobs within 6 blocks in front of you for 5 seconds. Each click logs what stands around the nearest mob's head. Off by default.
- Current Room Name — a movable HUD line "Room: Water Board" with the dungeon room you stand in, from the map's room identification (the sidebar's Room line if the map has none), re-read twice a second. Off by default.
- Cheat Utilities — **cheat build only.** Wither ESP (F7 boss), Secret Aura (auto-clicks chests/levers/essence/redstone keys in reach, and puts a picked-up redstone key on its redstone block - the key is not an item since SkyBlock 0.18.4, so this is one plain right click on a side of the block open to air, with nothing in your hand that would place a block, once a key has gone within 10 blocks of you (yours or a teammate's) and the block is within the skull range; that placing is the room's key secret, and Auto Routes' awaits and Auto Secret count it, while the pickup counts for an await only; one your look misses gets your body, never your camera, turned to it for a tick first; essence and redstone key skulls within 4.7 of their center, chests and levers 4.5; nothing for 20 ticks after a world change; nothing while Auto Clear is killing with no Auto Routes route running; skips puzzle rooms where a click could fail the puzzle, and the Water Board and blaze chests until that puzzle is done; never clicks a dummy lever - Red Blue's two wall levers beside the chest in the cage nook (only the floor lever outside the cage), Pressure Plates' sixteen cauldron levers and the lever on red wool under the Mines crypt - at any rotation, and Secret Waypoints, Secret Triggerbot, Secret Sound and Auto Routes' secret counts ignore them too; while an Auto Routes route is running it clicks nothing - no chest, trapped chest, lever, skull or key placing - unless the node you are on is waiting for secrets (an await that is not met yet; not a delay await, a crypt or bat node, a warp in flight or the walk between nodes), and with no route running it works as before; items are still picked up by walking), Auto GFS (sack refills; Hypixel Skyblock only, never p3sim; one /gfs at a time, waiting up to 5 s for the items before the next, at most 3 unanswered tries per item per world, and on a server that answers "Unknown or incomplete command" it stops until you join another server and says so once), Auto Ult (Healer/Tank at the right boss moments), and Auto Chocolate Factory (clicks the chocolate, claims strays, activates the Time Tower and buys the best-value upgrade, on the current menu layout; never clicks a menu-navigation slot) - Cheat Utilities — **cheat build only.** Wither ESP (F7 boss), Secret Aura (auto-clicks chests/levers/essence/redstone keys in reach, and puts a picked-up redstone key on its redstone block - the key is not an item since SkyBlock 0.18.4, so this is one plain right click on a side of the block open to air, with nothing in your hand that would place a block, once a key has gone within 10 blocks of you (yours or a teammate's) and the block is within the skull range; that placing is the room's key secret, and Auto Routes' awaits and Auto Secret count it, while the pickup counts for an await only; one your look misses gets your body, never your camera, turned to it for a tick first; essence and redstone key skulls within 4.7 of their center, chests and levers 4.5; nothing for 20 ticks after a world change; nothing while Auto Clear is killing with no Auto Routes route running; skips puzzle rooms where a click could fail the puzzle, and the Water Board and blaze chests until that puzzle is done; never clicks a dummy lever - Red Blue's two wall levers beside the chest in the cage nook, only the floor lever outside the cage - at any rotation, and Secret Waypoints, Secret Triggerbot, Secret Sound and Auto Routes' secret counts ignore them too; while an Auto Routes route is running it clicks nothing - no chest, trapped chest, lever, skull or key placing - unless the node you are on is waiting for secrets (an await that is not met yet; not a delay await, a crypt or bat node, a warp in flight or the walk between nodes), and with no route running it works as before; items are still picked up by walking), Auto GFS (sack refills; Hypixel Skyblock only, never p3sim; one /gfs at a time, waiting up to 5 s for the items before the next, at most 3 unanswered tries per item per world, and on a server that answers "Unknown or incomplete command" it stops until you join another server and says so once), Auto Ult (at the right boss moments, for the classes switched on under Ult For - Healer and Tank by default; Wish On Clear, for a Healer, uses Wish during the clear when a teammate's sidebar health turns red, tries once, then waits for Hypixel to say Wish is ready again or for the cooldown Hypixel names, so it never keeps trying while Wish is on cooldown), and Auto Chocolate Factory (clicks the chocolate, claims strays, activates the Time Tower and buys the best-value upgrade, on the current menu layout; never clicks a menu-navigation slot) During a Shadow Assassin's leap (the world border moved off you, which makes the game refuse block clicks) Secret Aura holds its click, without spending a try, and clicks the tick the border comes back; after 5 seconds it clicks as before.
- Secret Clicked (the Secret tab, Secrets folder) — a red box, with an anvil sound (Locked Chest Sound), on a chest you clicked when Hypixel answers with "That chest is locked!". Box On Every Secret (off by default) adds a gold box for 7 seconds on every secret you collect - the chests, levers, essence, redstone keys, bats and items Secret Sound detects; with it off, a secret you take shows nothing once taken (its Secret Waypoint goes on the tick you take it). In the same tab, Mute Chest Sounds and Mute Lever Sounds silence the server's chest open/close and lever click sounds in dungeons (only a sound that comes from a chest or lever, so the start countdown's clicks still play). Three independent switches, all off by default. Since 2026-11-01 Secret Sound, Secret Clicked and the Secrets HUD share one **Secret** tab, each under its own header with its own switch. A chest's secret sound waits until the chest is known not to be locked: it plays when the chest's menu opens, or after half a second with no "That chest is locked!" line, and not at all when that line comes. Every chest click plays its own sound, even two chests clicked within that half second, and a click that only reaches the server (another mod's click hook answered first) still plays.
- Solver highlights — each puzzle and boss solver (Boulder, Quiz, Ice Fill, Ice Path, Weirdos, Water Board, Creeper Beams, Blaze, Tic Tac Toe, Teleport Maze, Livid and the P4 Platform Highlight) has its own **Solver Highlight** switch, **Solver Highlight Through Walls** and, for those that draw waypoints, **Solver Highlight Style** (Outline or Fill) at the bottom of its tab; the shared Solver Highlights tab was removed on 2026-10-18 and a file from before starts every solver on the old shared values. Highlights are drawn after water, so water behind them no longer hides them. Solver Highlight off: the solver still solves and its chat and HUD lines still show, only the world drawing stops.
- Boulder Solver — reads the real Boulder puzzle room's floor pattern and highlights the real stone button to press next, ported from a known 8-pattern solution database. Never clicks for you
- Quiz Solver — reads the real Oruo the Omniscient trivia question and lettered answer options in chat, looks up the correct answer in a bundled real question database, and highlights that option's answer pillar. Never answers for you. **Block Wrong Answers** (off by default, 2026-10-25; NoammAddons' Quiz solver): once the solver knows the right answer, your click on one of the other two answers' buttons is dropped before it is sent, and chat says once per question which answer was wrong and which is right; before the answer is known nothing is blocked, and sneaking lets a click through while **Sneak To Override** is on (on by default; 2026-11-01). When Oruo says someone chose the wrong answer, the highlight is taken away (the puzzle has failed; 2026-11-01). **Quiz Timer** (off by default, 2026-11-01; a movable HUD, NoammAddons has one): while the Quiz is running it shows which question is next and how long until it can be answered ("Quiz 2/3: 3.4s", green "answer now" once it opens), from the same 11 s / 5 s server-tick timings Auto Quiz uses, so you can leave the room and know when to be back. (Fixed 2026-10-01: the box was drawn one block low. Decoding the room settles what those three coordinates name - each is a smooth-stone block with air above it, ringed by four of the room's twelve wall buttons, which is all twelve - so they are the pillars Oruo's buttons hang off, not floor to stand on.) A question asked while you are out of the Quiz room is kept for when you walk back in (2026-10-17). The Quiz's progress (started, question N of 3, time until it can be answered) is one shared state every feature reads, driven only by Oruo's exact lines. **Quiz logger** (always on, log only, 2026-10-18): every line starts with `[QuizLog]` and carries the server tick and wall time - when you walked into the Quiz room and where, each of Oruo's lines, each question with the tick it opens by the shared state's estimate, each answer press (yours or Auto Quiz's, early or on time) with Oruo's reply or the lack of one, and the wrong-answer line. Written in dev and cheat builds, dropped by a release.
- Ice Fill Solver — identifies each of the real Ice Fill puzzle's 3 floor layouts and draws the real known-safe walking path across all of them. Never walks for you. The line's color is picked in a color box (2026-11-01; it was a cycle of five presets, and an old setting loads as its color). **Etherwarp Protection** (off by default, 2026-11-01) drops your own etherwarp right click in the Ice Fill room unless it is safe: standing on ice that has not turned to packed ice yet, every warp is refused; standing on packed (or blue) ice, only a warp that lands on the next spot of the drawn path (or the next tile still to fill, so the step onto the next floor is allowed) goes through; off the ice (the stone around it, the stairs) you warp freely. The landing is judged where the server will put you, not where the crosshair points (2026-11-02): Hypixel warps along the look and sneak it last heard, so a click made while the camera is still turning would land a tile off the one you aim at (that is what gave "Don't move diagonally!"); such a click is held for one tick until the server has your new look. A refused warp sends nothing, and chat says why. Auto Ice Fill's own warps are not affected.
- Weirdos Solver — reads the real Three Weirdos NPC dialogue lines and highlights the real correct chest (and, optionally, ruled-out ones) the moment a line gives it away
- Water Board Solver — identifies the real Water Board layout and shows a real live countdown above every remaining lever click, highlighting the soonest one. Never clicks anything for you The next lever is highlighted on the lever's own hitbox only
- Creeper Beams Solver — highlights the currently-connected Sea Lantern pairs in matching colors, live as panes are rotated. A pair turns red only when you've connected one of its lanterns to the wrong partner. Never touches anything. Every pair in the room has its own clearly different color (yellow, green, cyan, magenta, then orange, blue, white, brown), kept until that pair is done (2026-11-01: two pairs could both be yellow). **Show** picks what is drawn: All, One At A Time (one pair until you finish it, then the next) or Next (that pair and the one after it); the first set always includes the pair from the creeper's feet to about straight above it (when there is one; a pair picked before the creeper has loaded does not count, so the creeper's pair still comes first once it appears, and after about 40 scans without a creeper the nearest pair opens for good), each later pick is the lit pair nearest you, and a wrong (red) pair is always shown.
- Blaze Solver — ranks real blazes in the Lower/Higher Blaze puzzle by HP and highlights the correct next few kill targets in order. Never attacks anything
- Tic Tac Toe Solver — reads the Tic Tac Toe board and highlights just the button / item frame of the best move on your turn (minimax), as an outline or a fill. The optional prediction shows, on the computer's turn, the square YOU click next: the computer's best reply is worked out first, and the square is your best move after it, so you can spam it. A square the computer has not taken in any of its best replies comes first; when there is none, a square the computer might take is still shown if it is your best click after every other reply it could make, and if the computer does take it the solver shows your best move as usual (2026-11-01: with the computer to move on X . O / O O X / X . . both bottom squares are safe and nothing was shown). Every position is computed, none is special-cased. **Prevent Misclicks** blocks clicks on every other square while one is highlighted; with **Sneak To Override** on (the default, 2026-11-01) sneaking lets a click through. Never clicks for you
- Teleport Maze Solver — tracks the pads you've used and narrows down the real exit pad from where each teleport makes you face (green = the one, gold = candidates), with a tracer from your crosshair to the best next pad. Never moves you. 2026-11-01: **Tracer Color** (a color box, cyan by default), **Current Cell Only** (only the four pads of the cell you are in; at the start or end pad every pad shows) and **Best Pad Only** (only the pad you should take next: the tracer's pad, or the exit once it is the only candidate).
- Ice Path Solver — reads the silverfish Ice Path board and draws the shortest push path to the exit, outlining the silverfish's next stop. Never hits the silverfish
- Livid Solver (its own tab) — identifies the real correct Livid on Floor 5 from the wool color clue, re-checking it twice a second, and highlights it in that Livid's own color with an optional line to it, plus a countdown for its opening invulnerability window, in ticks or seconds (Timer Unit). Never attacks anything. Three extras, each off by default: Ice Spray Alert (an "Ice Spray Livid!" title and a sound the moment the invulnerability ends), Hide Wrong Livids (the clones and their name tags are not drawn once the real one is known; render only, they stay in the world and hittable), and Announce Color (sends "Livid is Red (Hockey Livid)" to party chat once the answer has held for 2 seconds, at most twice a run)
- Architect's First Draft — when a puzzle is failed (its own fail line, whatever that puzzle's wording is - e.g. "PUZZLE FAIL! ...", or Quiz's Oruo announcing a wrong answer), posts a line in your chat you can click to get an Architect's First Draft from your sack. **Cheat build:** optionally gets it automatically, only when the fail line names you (Hypixel Skyblock only, and not on a server that answered /gfs "unknown command"; the click line is offered instead). Works in the dungeon sim too: a local world has no "PUZZLE FAIL!" broadcast and no sack, so the sim's puzzles report their own fails and the draft is handed over directly rather than fetched — the same two settings, and in the sim the cheat-build restriction does not apply because there is no server to automate against

- Auto Sell — runs by itself on any sell screen while it is on (there is no start or stop command). Sells items from a list you build, with a never-sell list that always wins; there is no "sell everything else" fallback, so an empty list sells nothing. It uses a real shift-click, so if it ever misidentifies the screen it moves the item rather than selling it, and it only ever touches your own inventory slots. Add an item to the list with the Toggle Item key (set in the tab) while hovering it in any container - pressing it again removes it. `/autosell list` or the tab's Open Sell List button opens the list as a menu where you add by identity, Add Held, or click a row to remove it. Every stack is clicked once, so three swords of the same kind are three sells in one visit. An item added to the list while the sell screen is open is sold in that screen without reopening it. After each visit the log has a "[AutoSell] visit result" line and one line for every stack left unsold with the reason: not on the sell list, on the never-sell list, no identity, refused by the server, or timed out with no answer. Between two sells it waits a random 0-10 ticks, with a min and a max slider. **Cheat build only**, off by default A stack that a click sold but that comes back (a ghost sell: it goes into the menu and straight back), or that the server answered and never moved, is clicked again - at most twice per slot, also after the visit has ended, while the same menu is open. **Any NPC Sell Menu** (on by default) also sells in any Hypixel NPC shop you can sell into, told by the shop's sell/buyback slot, not only menus titled like Trades. The sell list menu has a Remove button to the right of each item and its list no longer runs under the buttons. **Add Defaults** (in the tab and the list menu) adds his dungeon-junk list: Oak Sign, Lever, Earthen Blade, Silent Death, the Zombie Lord, Super Heavy, Zombie Knight, Zombie Soldier, Bouncy, Skeletor, Skeleton Grunt, Heavy, Rotten, Skeleton Lord, Zombie Commander and Skeleton Soldier sets (all four armor pieces), the Skeleton Master set without its chestplate, Vitamin Death, Machine Gun Shortbow, Soulstealer Bow, Premium Flesh, Candycomb, Training Weights, Defuse Kit, Tripwire Hook, Stone Button and the Healing VIII Splash Potion - each once. A potion is keyed by its name (HEALING_VIII_SPLASH_POTION), since every Hypixel potion shares one id.
- Class Selection Overlay — on the class-selection screen, a bar across the top showing every party member and the class they're currently on, and a faint class-name watermark in each of the five boxes with whoever is assigned to it written over the top. Click a name then a box to assign. The screen is found by its contents rather than by slot positions, because no layout for those five icons is documented anywhere. Assignments write through the same class picks the Leap Order editor sets (`/class`), so every class-colored display in the mod agrees with it; an Edit Classes chip at the end of the bar opens that editor. Its switch is in the Leap Menu tab, next to an Edit Classes button
- Auto Anvil — in Hypixel's Anvil, puts two identical enchanted books in and combines them into the next level, one click at a time, waiting for the server to show each step before the next. It only ever pairs the same book at the same tier: same single enchantment, same level, and every other SkyBlock attribute equal, read from the item's data, never its name. Books with more than one enchantment, recombobulated books, stacks and protected items (Item Protect) are left alone. It only combines where the anvil can make the next level for that enchantment (two Sharpness IV make V, two Sharpness V make nothing, Looting stops at III, Infinite Quiver goes to X), and an enchantment it has no rule for is never combined. Lowest levels go first, so with Combine Results Again on four III books become a V. It stops with the reason in chat on anything unexpected (the anvil closed, a book did not arrive, a preview or result that is not the expected book, something left in the anvil). Settings: Combine Results Again, Close When Done, Min/Max Delay. **Cheat build only**, off by default

- Bazaar Advisor — legit and read-only: when a Bazaar menu opens it prints one recommendation in chat (what to buy, how many, the expected profit). Turn it on in Items > Bazaar Advisor. Set the answers with `/bzflip`: `npc` or `bazaar` (where it sells), `total` or `percent` (what it optimizes), `insta` or `order` (how it buys), `instasell` or `offer` (how it sells on the Bazaar). Insta Buy is capped by your inventory space; Buy Order by the purse only. Expected profit counts only what sells within a day, and for a Buy Order only what fills in a day: a buy order fills when someone Insta Sells into it, so its day is Hypixel's 7-day instant-sell volume over seven, times the share a top order gets against the other buy orders (halved at 25 of them). A big margin on an item that instant-sells a few times a day therefore ranks low, and an item with no instant-sell volume in the API counts as filling nothing. The recommendation states the fills a day. With `percent`, a Buy Order is ranked by that day's profit as a share of the coins the order ties up, not by its margin alone. Off by default.
- Bazaar Flipper — the Bazaar Advisor's picks, traded for you: it runs the autopilot on your `/bzflip` answers (NPC or Bazaar, total profit or best percentage, Insta Buy or Buy Order, Insta Sell or Sell Offer) - reading Manage Orders, buying, selling, relisting outbid orders, collecting, and selling to the `/trades` NPC in NPC mode. **Cheat build only**, off by default and unbound by default. Your keybind starts it and **Escape, the Emergency Cancel Key (Home) or your keybind again stops it** - in the world, in Hypixel's Bazaar menus, on the reskin and in the amount sign alike; no other key or click does (the stop message names the key). While it runs, other keys and clicks in a Bazaar menu or the amount sign are kept off the menu (a stray number key there would be a hotbar swap); Escape still closes a Bazaar menu, and in the sign it is swallowed so nothing gets sent. Its purchases are sized by what really fits: Hypixel's own stack sizes (enchanted books, reforge stones such as Kaleidoscopic Mineral, and every item Hypixel marks unstackable hold one per slot; the Instant Buy page's "Fill my inventory!" amount is never exceeded), one order's limit (71,680, or 256 for an item that does not stack), and the purse above the Reserve at the price the order book will really charge (the book walked to that many, plus Hypixel's 4% quote), checked again against the live page and the Confirm Instant Buy price before it clicks; a buy that comes out too dear is tried again smaller instead of skipped. It stops on a target purse, on nothing being profitable, on the daily NPC sell limit, and on any menu it did not expect. The tab has the switch, the Start Key, Configure /bzflip, the Reserve slider, Min Profit Per Cycle (a buy-and-sell cycle expected to make less is passed over; Off takes any flip that pays after fees), Max New Orders Per Session with its cap, Stop At Max Orders (on: the session ends at the cap, and stops when Hypixel says every order slot is taken; off: a new round of orders starts once the round's orders are done, and it waits for a free slot), NPC Sales Today (the coins sold to NPCs today on this account against Hypixel's 500,000,000-a-day cap, with the time to its 00:00 UTC reset), Target Purse, Menu Speed and Sell Speed.
- AH Flipper — watches the Auction House for BINs listed under what the same item sells for: the full AH scan gives each item's cheapest BINs, and the newest listings are checked every 15 seconds (Hypixel's public API, no key). "The same item" means the same SkyBlock id, dungeon stars and Recombobulator (pets are left out). A flip's sale price is the cheapest other BIN less the Undercut, and its profit is that sale after the BIN listing fee (1% under 10M, 2% to 100M, 2.5% above) and the 1% collection tax, less the price. Off by default. Settings: Min Profit %, Undercut, Min Profit, Min Sell Price, Max Buy Price (your purse by default) and Min Listings (how many other BINs must exist before their cheapest is trusted). Run Mode Background (the legit build's only mode) puts each flip in chat with a clickable [Open] that runs `/viewauction` so you buy it yourself. **Cheat build:** Run Mode Main also buys each flip (Buy Item Right Now, then Confirm), one at a time, only while no menu is open and outside dungeons, and stops after Max Buys Per Session or three failed buys in a row (Escape or the Emergency Cancel Key stops it too). After a buy the relist price is remembered, so the AH Price Buttons offer it as Copy My Last Price. **Cheat build, Auto Relist** (off by default, untested on Hypixel): opens `/ah` by itself while no menu is open, outside dungeons and with the buying macro idle, claims won bids (View Bids) and sold and expired auctions (Manage Auctions: Claim All, or each one's Collect Auction), then lists each flip the macro bought and each expired item it claimed back through Create BIN Auction at the flip's sale price, or the current lowest BIN less the AH Price Buttons' Undercut for an expired item (Relist at My Last Price, on by default, prefers the price you listed the same item at in the last 6 hours; never under Min Sell Price), checking the price the menu shows before Create BIN Auction and Confirm. It runs after a buy or after "[Auction] NAME bought ..." at most once a minute, or at once with Relist Now; every step goes through the one-action-per-tick gate 250-500 ms apart; Escape or the Emergency Cancel Key stops it, and three failed runs in a row halt it. Its menu names come from public mods.
- AH Price Buttons — four buttons on Hypixel's Create BIN Auction menu that copy a price for the item you are listing to your clipboard, as plain digits for the price sign. In Hypixel's own menu they sit in a panel left of the chest (right of it when there is no room, always clear of the search bar); in the reskinned Auction House they sit in the toolbar. **Copy LBIN**: the lowest BIN of that exact item (same SkyBlock id, dungeon stars and Recombobulator; a pet's type, rarity and level) from the full AH scan, times the stack, less the Undercut (1 coin by default). **Copy Average**: the item's average lowest BIN over 1 or 3 days (the Price Tooltip's Average Days, 3 by default) from Skyblocker's public price list (hysky.de), times the stack; it is an average of lowest BINs, not of sale prices. **Copy Estimated Value**: the Price Tooltip's Est. Item Value of the stack with its modifiers (its Value Mode). **Copy My Last Price**: the price you listed the same item at in the last 6 hours. A button with no price is grayed out and its tooltip says why; a click says "Copied" on the button and in chat. Off by default; replaces the AH Price Copier, which copied the lowest BIN by itself when an item went in. Clipboard only: never clicks, types or sends anything to Hypixel.
  - *How it prices a flip.* Two different Hypixel endpoints: the Bazaar order books, and `npc_sell_price` from the item resources list. For each candidate it walks the real instant-buy order book cheapest level first, so the rising price as a purchase eats through the book is paid for rather than ignored, and stops at the first of three limits — a level priced at or above the NPC price, a full inventory (36 slots at the item's real stack size), or your purse running out. `quick_status.buyPrice` is deliberately not used: it is a weighted average and matched the exact top of the book on only 628 of 1833 products when this was measured, so it overstates what a purchase realizes.
  - *Ranking.* Either **Percentage Margin** or **Total Profit Per Run**, and it always takes the best one by that ranking whose profit after fees is above 0 (the Min Profit Per Run slider was removed on 2026-10-16). A third option, profit per coin invested, was dropped because it orders identically to percentage margin.
  - *Fees.* Checked on the wiki: the Bazaar's tax (1.25%, 1% with the Bazaar Flipper Community Shop upgrade) is taken on sales TO the Bazaar, not on instant buys, and NPCs and `/trades` pay their price with no fee; a Booster Cookie changes no rate. So a flip's cost is exactly what the book walk pays and its revenue exactly what the NPC pays. The fees are written as constants (both 0) so a future one is one line. What Hypixel's Instant Buy page shows is not the cost, though: it quotes the book price plus 4% and refunds what the buy did not use (wiki, Bazaar), so the flipper takes a page price as book = quote / 1.04 before comparing it with the NPC price, and makes sure your purse covers the full quote, which Hypixel requires before it runs the buy.
  - *Live price check.* Each buy is checked against the live Instant Buy page. If the page shows the flip no longer pays, it buys nothing, leaves the Bazaar, rescans and takes the next best flip; that product is not tried again this session unless a fresh scan prices it lower than before.
  - *Daily NPC sell limit.* Hypixel pays at most 500,000,000 coins a day for NPC sales, resetting at 00:00 UTC (wiki, Shops and Coins). The flipper counts every "You sold ... Coins!" line for your account today (saved, so a restart keeps it) and never sizes a buy past what the NPCs will still pay for; at the limit it refuses to start and says when it resets. A limit line from Hypixel, or a sale the NPC refuses near the limit, stops it at once with the same message, and what could not be sold is not counted as revenue. Sales made in another client or before this build ran are not seen; then the limit line or the refused sale is what stops it.
  - *Target Purse* stops it (between cycles) once your purse reaches that number. You type it: plain digits with or without commas (5,000,000) or a number with k, m or b (750k, 2.5m, 1b). Empty or 0 means no target, text that is not a number is ignored (the label says "not a number"), and anything past a trillion is clamped. The box shows the saved value with commas.
  - *Failsafe for not having the money.* Your purse is passed into the sizing, so an item you cannot fully afford is simply costed at what you can afford, and the next best item can win on its own merits. If nothing is profitable it stops and says so.
  - *Profit tracker.* Each finished cycle goes into `/profit bazaarflip` (cheat build only - a legit jar has no such card or command): coins spent from Hypixel's "[Bazaar] Bought Nx X for C coins!" lines, coins received from its "You sold" lines, the tax (0), and the net, which is exactly what your purse moved by. Units bought and not sold earn nothing there. Like the other trackers it can show this account, all accounts, or a mod profile.
  - *Scanning.* Once on start, then once a minute **while running only**. Nothing scans in the background while it is idle. Every scan is a fresh download of the whole Bazaar, asking caches not to answer, and a snapshot whose own timestamp is more than 60 seconds old is fetched again and then refused; nothing from an earlier session or a test is ever used. `/bazaarflip scan [n]` prices the market once and prints the ranking without touching a menu.
  - *Menu Speed* slider (1-10) sets the gap between two consecutive automated actions in the menus — 700-1300 ms at 1, down to 160-310 ms at 10, drawn randomly inside that band each time. It does not change how much is bought and does not shorten any safety timeout.
  - *Sell Speed* slider (1-10, same scale, much faster) sets the gap between two sell shift-clicks: 150-220 ms at 1, down to 51-58 ms at 10.
  - *Topping up.* When the planned units are in, it buys more of the same item while the purse and the free room still cover it (at the scan's price plus the quote margin). When the Bazaar does not reopen after a Custom Amount buy, it sends `/bz` again if there is still room and coins.
  - *The cycle.* `/bz <item>`, the item in Hypixel's search results, **Buy Instantly**, then on the Instant Buy page it buys the exact amount the scan found profitable, capped by your free space, the NPC sell limit and Custom Amount's own "Buy up to N x." line. When **Buy only one!**, **Buy a stack!** or **Fill my inventory!** is exactly that amount it clicks that; otherwise it clicks **Custom Amount**, types the number on the first line of Hypixel's sign ("Enter amount / to order") and presses Done. Before that it bounds the price from the preset buttons (a bigger preset's average price is never below the custom amount's), and if that bound is not under the NPC price, or your purse cannot cover it, it shrinks the amount or falls back to the largest preset that is safe.
