And while one of those is moving you with sprint off, no sprint can start on you whoever asks - another mod's auto sprint (Odin's answers the sprint key in the game's movement step; others call the sprint switch every tick) is refused then, so a route walks at the speed it planned. A sprint already under way is left alone, as the game would.
- Smooth Teleport — when an Etherwarp, Instant Transmission (AOTE/AOTV), Wither Impact (Hyperion, Astraea, Scylla, Valkyrie) or Sinseeker/Leech teleport lands you, the camera glides from where it was to where you landed over a set time (50-500 ms, default 100) instead of snapping, in first person. Purely visual: you are already at the landing, and your crosshair, clicks and every automation aim from there from the first tick. A server position change that does not answer one of your own teleport uses (a lag-back, a correction, a warp) is never smoothed and cancels a glide in progress. Each teleport type has its own toggle. Off by default; in General, under Etherwarp Overlay
- Etherwarp (tab) — **cheat build only (since 2026-11-02; legit 2026-10-26 to 2026-11-02).** One card in the Dungeons cheat block holds Zero Ping Etherwarp and Left Click Etherwarp under a red header at its bottom; the card's switch is on while either is on. No explanation lines in the tab: every row explains itself in its hover tooltip. Searching "zpew" finds it in the cheat build; a legit build has no Etherwarp tab at all. Order: the Zero Ping Etherwarp switch, Dungeons Only, Chain From Prediction, Overlay From Prediction, Give Up After, then Works For as the last legit rows; the cheat build's rows follow at the bottom under red headers (the cheat Works For senders, Multi Warps, Left Click Etherwarp). **Untested**
- Zero Ping Etherwarp — **cheat build only, off by default** (it was in both builds from 2026-10-26 to 2026-11-02). **Dungeons Only** (off by default) keeps it to dungeons (the sim counts). The moment you use an etherwarp, your view is drawn where it will land, instead of a round trip later. Camera only: your character moves when the server teleports you, nothing extra is sent, and your position is never faked. Chained warps (another etherwarp before the first has landed) are predicted from the predicted landing, and the Etherwarp Overlay highlights from there too (Overlay From Prediction), so you can aim the next warp at once. A landing the server does not confirm, or no answer within Give Up After, puts the view back on you. Smooth Teleport does not glide a teleport it already showed. **Works For** lists everything that sends an etherwarp, each with its own switch, all on by default (they were all predicted before the list existed): Manual Etherwarps (your own right click) in both builds, and in the cheat build, under the red header, Left Click Etherwarp, Auto Routes (etherwarp nodes, path hops, Zero Ping and Charged Chain warps - the camera only; Auto Routes' own Zero Ping Chain still decides how a route aims), Interactive Map (map presses, Go + Secret, Auto Blood Rush, Auto Routes' Go To and start-node trips), Auto Secret / Autopilot, Auto Clear, Auto Puzzles (Ice Path's Etherwarp Reposition and the other puzzle warps), AP3 (a use node holding an etherwarp item) and Pathfinding Hopper (Auto Fairy Souls). A warp is predicted only when Zero Ping Etherwarp is on and its sender's switch is on. The Interactive Map needs no Zero Ping Chain of its own: its hops have always gone one a tick, each aimed from the last one's planned landing, without waiting for the server. **Multi Warps** (cheat build, red header, off by default; a settings file from before the switch turns it on when Warps Per Tick was above 1 or Aimed Warps was on) is the switch over the next two, which show only while it is on. **Warps Per Tick** (1 = off, up to 10): each of your own etherwarps (right click or Left Click Etherwarp) is followed in the SAME tick by that many minus one more, at the same look, each from where the one before lands - on flat ground looking at the floor 8 blocks ahead, Warps Per Tick 5 moves you 40 blocks in one tick. This is the one part of Zero Ping Etherwarp that sends packets. A warp that would land nowhere is not sent and ends the burst; each warp costs its mana. Only the same look works: a second use in a tick with a different look is what an anticheat flags (GrimAC BadPacketsJ), so Auto Routes' differently aimed nodes still go one a tick. **Aimed Warps** (cheat build, under Multi Warps, off by default): several clicks inside one tick at different looks each warp their own way, all in that tick - each extra look spends one banked Blink Charge tick (so it needs Blink with Charge on and a bank; without one the clicks go as vanilla sends them, all at your current look). Right after a teleport the burst waits for Blink's bank to be covered (at most 3 s), as Blink's own shot does. The Emergency Cancel Key (Home) holds both: while its hold is on, Warps Per Tick sends no extras and Aimed Warps leaves the clicks to vanilla (a burst waiting for cover is dropped); your own etherwarp still goes. Streamer Mode's Disable cheats stops them the same way. Auto Routes does the same for whole routes with Charged Chain.
- Left Click Etherwarp — **cheat build only, off by default.** With an etherwarp item in hand (an Aspect of the Void or End with Etherwarp Merger, or an Etherwarp Conduit), a left click etherwarps where you look. If you are not sneaking, sneak goes down for that tick and the warp goes the next tick - the shortest a sneak then a click can be, with no random delay - and sneak comes back up afterwards; if you already sneak, the warp goes on the click itself. The left click never attacks, swings or digs with the item. The warp is sent as an ordinary right click (the block half first when you look at a block in reach), so Zero Ping Etherwarp predicts it like any other. Stands down while Auto Routes or the Interactive Map is moving you. At the bottom of the Etherwarp tab (its own card in the Dungeons cheat block until 2026-11-01).
- Sound Effects — plays a sound you pick (a preset or a file you imported) instead of the game's for 23 game sounds: arrow hits and shots, attack hits, block hits and breaks, mob deaths, XP orbs, item pickups, level up, anvils, the enchant table, chests, doors, explosions, teleports (ender pearl, Enderman), note blocks, villagers and NPCs, the Wither, eating and drinking, splash potions, totems and fishing. Each sound is ONE row: its name with its ON/OFF switch and the replacement-sound selector beside it (left click steps forward, right click back; the drawer never stacks these rows). Every row is off by default and Default keeps the game's own sound; the game's sound is not played at all while a row replaces it, and a sound that is missing falls back to the game's with one chat line.
- Sound Disabler — mutes the game sounds you pick, on your client only (added 2026-11-02; Misc, both builds). Nothing is done to any packet: the server, the anticheat and every other feature still get the sound, it is just never played. Off by default; its switch is the first row, then one switch per sound. Bonzo's Staff (off by default, like every row) mutes the staff's shot and its balloon's blast and nothing else: the shot is a ghast sound, muted only when a player holding a Bonzo's Staff or Starred Bonzo's Staff stands where it plays, and the blast a firework sound, muted only within 4 seconds and 48 blocks of such a shot, so real ghasts, a Blaze Slayer's fireballs and other fireworks still play. The other rows (all off by default) mute every use of their sounds, since a game sound has no owner: Explosions (Wither Impact, TNT, creepers), Bow Shots (every bow, the Terminator spam), Endermen, Blazes, Wolves, Spiders, Phantoms, Withers (the F7 Withers and their skulls, not wither skeletons) and Rain And Thunder. Skyblock Weather (Skyblock only) mutes the island weather Hypixel added in 0.27.2 (Thunderstorm, Acid Rain, Hellstorm, Blizzard and the rest): every sound in the Weather category plus rain and lightning; Hypixel publishes no list of the sounds the newer weathers play, so one that still plays is found with Show Sound IDs and muted with Custom Sound IDs. Custom Sound IDs mutes any other sounds by id, separated by commas or spaces (`entity.villager.ambient`; `minecraft:` is assumed, and an id ending in `*` mutes every id starting with it), and Show Sound IDs says the id and category of every sound played in chat (each at most once every 2 seconds, marked "(muted)" when it was; click an id to copy it) so you can find one. Ticking sounds (minecarts, bees, the elytra) are not covered. Kept across restarts and in profiles
- Session Recorder — while it is on, writes what the dungeon features do to one file per session in `config/killer560/diagnostics/` (`killer560smod-sessionlog-<date>_<time>.log`), so the file can be sent back for analysis: Auto Routes, Auto Puzzles, Breaker and Breaker Aura, Secret Aura and the other auras, Arrow Align, Blood Camp, the map and Auto Clear, server corrections and the automations' progress lines, plus world joins and leaves. One line per event: time, client tick, ticks since that feature's last line, feature, event and the details the feature logs (positions, blocks, items, the server's answer or the timeout). It only writes a file: it never plays, clicks, moves or logs in anything. A file stops at 20 MB with a last line saying it was cut. If the session file cannot be opened or written, the tab says so. A "Recording" tag shows in the top right while it runs. It will not start in an instance that has a `killer560smod-session-recorder-disabled` file in `config/killer560/` (the deploy script puts one in the Dungeons instance). Off by default; in Misc

**Display**
- Borderless Fullscreen — F11 toggles between windowed and borderless fullscreen, never true exclusive fullscreen
- Confine Mouse In Menus — in borderless fullscreen on Windows, keeps the mouse pointer inside the game window while a menu or container (a chest, the mod menu, the pause menu) is open, so it cannot drift onto another monitor. Opening chat, alt-tabbing away or closing the menu lets it go. It only acts in game (never on the title screen, the server list or any other menu outside a world), and **Only In Dungeons** (off by default) limits it to dungeon runs, and it does nothing in a normal window or exclusive fullscreen. Only the pointer's range changes, never its look or what the game sends. Off by default; in Display Options
- Fullbright — see clearly in dark areas without touching your real brightness setting
- Hide Pause Menu Icons — leaves the row of four small icon buttons (Report Bugs, Give Feedback, Friends, Player Reporting) out of the pause menu, and the rows close up with the usual spacing, so Statistics is followed directly by Options (or a server's own buttons such as Hypixel's Server Links, which always stay). On by default, in Display Options; turn it off to get the icons back (with the menu theme on they then sit on a lighter face so their outlines show). Takes effect the next time the pause menu opens
- Mods Screen — the mod's own mod list, Mod Menu built in: a Mods button on the Themed Main Menu (under Mod Settings) and on the pause menu (a full-width row where the icon row was, or under it) opens a screen listing every mod you have loaded, by name, with its icon and version. A mod that carries others (Fabric API's modules, anything inside another mod's jar, or a mod naming a parent the way Mod Menu reads it) lists them under it: the arrow and count at the right of its row open and close them. The search box filters by name, id, author or description and opens a parent to show the children that match; Libraries (ON by default) hides library mods and the built-ins (Java, Minecraft, Fabric Loader) when turned off. Clicking a mod shows its big icon, name, version, id, authors and full description (the mouse wheel scrolls a long one), with Configure, which opens its settings screen (this mod's own menu; another mod's needs Mod Menu installed, since that is where those screens are registered), and Website, Source and Issues for the links it gives. Escape or Back returns to the menu you came from. In its card (Misc): Open Mods Screen (the Mods buttons themselves always exist, with no switch). With Mod Menu installed the pause menu keeps Mod Menu's own Mods button and adds none of its own, unless Hide Pause Menu Icons took Mod Menu's icon away
- Pack Disabler — every SkyBlock item drawn the way it looked before Hypixel's 2026 resource pack: its old item or player-head skin, drawn through whatever resource packs you use, so a pack's seeds stay that pack's seeds. Items that never had an old look (90 of them, mostly Galatea, Safari and Garden items, plus the Great Spook's Ghostfly Soul) get the mod's own original textures, which your resource packs can replace too. The table covers about 3,040 items, 1,391 of them on Hypixel's own models, built from Hypixel's item list, Hypixel's resource pack and the NotEnoughUpdates item repository (MIT, data only) as it was before each of its texture updates, last refreshed 2026-11-03 for pack 97 (the Reforge Stone, Power Stone and equipment textures of 0.27.2, the Great Spook items and the Bingo tools). Items a menu shows without their SkyBlock id (Bazaar and Auction House pages, recipes) are recognized by Hypixel's model and get the same old look. Paper Menu Buttons (on by default): Minecraft 26.3 redrew maps as folded maps, so Hypixel's map buttons such as Create Sell Offer and Create Buy Order are drawn as paper again; 26.2 already draws the old flat sheet and is left alone. Hypixel's pack itself still loads, because SkyBlock requires it, and only item looks change. Off by default, in Display Options. If Noamm's PackDisabler mod is installed this one stays idle and says so once
- Custom Crosshair — replaces the vanilla crosshair with one you design in its **Custom Crosshair** section of the General tab, CS2/Valorant style, with a live preview beside the settings (click it to change the background; the strip under it previews movement spread, recoil and target colors). Styles: Cross, T-Shape, X, Dot, Circle and Cross + Circle, a center dot for any of them, and each arm switchable. Every size is a slider - length, thickness, gap (negative overlaps through the middle), dot size, circle radius and thickness, rotation and overall scale - and is rounded to whole screen pixels, so thin lines stay sharp at any GUI Scale. Size Mode is GUI Scale (follows your GUI Scale, and your monitor while Auto Scale is on) or Screen Pixels (the same pixel size everywhere). Color with transparency from the color picker, Chroma (the same flowing rainbow as Enchant Colors, at the screen center), Invert Blend (vanilla's look), its own dot color, and an outline with its own thickness and color. Optional dynamic behavior: spread when moving, sprinting or jumping, a recoil kick when you swing, and a different color while aiming at an entity or a block in reach. Vanilla's attack indicator can stay or go, and it can hide in menus (chat does not count) and show in third person; F1 hides it as usual. Built-in presets, named presets saved in the config folder, and Copy Code / Paste Code to share a crosshair as a short code. Off by default; in both builds

**Accounts**
- Account Switcher — swap between your saved Microsoft accounts from the main menu's Swap Accounts page. It lists the accounts of the launcher you started the game from: Prism Launcher's, or the Modrinth App's when you play from the Modrinth App (read only, never changed; a Modrinth account signs in with the token the Modrinth App last fetched, so if one says its sign-in has expired, restart the Modrinth App and try again). Every account is a full-width card with its skin head, its name, the account you are playing as standing out in the theme's accent (an accent-tinted card with a bold frame and side bar, its name in the accent and a solid ACTIVE pill), the proxy it connects through (No proxy, its own, or the Universal one) and its Hypixel ban status; click a card to switch to it. Copy ID (copies the account's public UUID; Shift-click copies its session token for Direct Session Login, which gives full access to the account for about a day, so never share it) and Set Proxy / Edit Proxy sit at the right end of each card. Each account can have its own SOCKS proxy, applied automatically every time you swap to it. With six or more accounts a search box narrows the list (or just start typing); the list scrolls smoothly and the page fits any window size and GUI scale. The footer holds Back, Direct Session Login, Set Universal Proxy and the Universal switch
- Proxy Client — optionally route your connection through a SOCKS proxy; set it with Set Universal Proxy on the main menu's Swap Accounts page (this instance uses it), then flip the Universal switch to make every instance use it no matter what. Only the Minecraft server connection goes through the proxy: signing in (Microsoft, Xbox, Mojang, including the account you swap to), the Hypixel API, the mod's relay and every other web request still use your real IP, and the server and proxy names are looked up through your normal DNS, so Microsoft, Mojang and those services still see every account come from your real IP. Swap Accounts and its proxy and session login pages are drawn in the mod menu's theme; Swap Accounts Theme in Display picks Theme Default (follows the menu) or any preset or your Custom colors for them

**Home**
- A HUD editor to drag and resize the mod's on-screen elements wherever you want them. You can move exactly what you could just see: an element is draggable if its own setting is on **and** it actually drew on screen within the last ten seconds, so the dungeon timers are editable in a dungeon and the RNG Meter ranking is editable for ten seconds after you close that menu. An element that is switched on but has not been on screen recently is not shown - turn on Show Unseen to move those too. Anything switched off in settings is not listed at all. Every element starts fully on screen at any GUI scale or window size, and opening chat never hides the HUD (real menus still do). Each element's box is the size of what it draws, with the same small margin all round, and its preview sits exactly where it draws in game. Resizing works like a desktop window: drag a box's corner to resize it with its shape kept (the opposite corner stays put), and a stat bar's edges and corners change its length and thickness separately; the cursor turns into the resize arrow over a handle, and scrolling over a box still scales it. While dragging or resizing, a box snaps to other elements' edges and centers, the screen's edges and center, and the gap two neighbors in a row already have, with an orange guide line while it is snapped; holding Alt places a box freely for that drag. The editor's own options are not on the editor screen (which shows only the elements and one short hint line, gone while you drag; Escape closes it) but in Home's **HUD Editor** section: Snapping on/off, what it snaps to (Element Edges, Element Centers, Screen Edges, Screen Center, Equal Spacing) and Show Unseen. The Open Menu Key (Home > Mod & HUD, backslash by default; click it and press a key to change it, Backspace unbinds) opens the mod menu in game when no screen is open and you are not typing. The **Emergency Cancel Key** (Home > Mod & HUD, unset by default; a key or a mouse button, Backspace unbinds) stops everything automatic at once: every macro below, Auto Routes, AP3, Mimic Killer, the Interactive Map's warps and the etherwarp / Ender Pearl hoppers (each keeps its own stop rules too). It also starts a hold: until you press it again, change worlds or start a macro yourself, no aura, triggerbot, Auto Terminals click, auto puzzle or Auto I4 shot goes out and no Auto Routes or AP3 node starts (your Slot Binds, Pet Wheel and Loadout Keybinds still work); chat says when it holds and when it lets go. An auto puzzle stopped this way does not pick its room up again. Since 2026-11-01 every macro follows one rule: a click macro (Auto E-Table, Bazaar Flip, Auto Croesus, Auto Sell, Auto Anvil, Inventory Sorter, Storage Scan All) stops only on Escape or the Emergency Cancel Key; a movement macro (Auto Fairy Souls, Auto Secret, Auto Clear, Blood Rush) also stops on a movement key (forward, back, left, right, jump) or the chat key; no other key, click or scroll stops either, and keys typed into chat or a menu never count. Auto Routes and AP3 keep their own stop rules beside the Emergency Cancel Key. A macro's own Start/Stop key still stops it. The Edit HUD keybind works over any screen - an inventory, a chest, a Croesus or Chocolate Factory menu, this menu - except while you are typing: chat, an anvil rename, a sign or book, a command block, any search box (the creative and recipe book searches, this menu's, the Bazaar's, the Auction House's, the inventory search bar) or any other focused text box, and while a keybind button is waiting for a key. Opened over a container, Edit HUD draws that container behind the boxes so the overlays drawn on it (Start Croesus, Start ETable, the Croesus Chest Profit panel, the Chocolate Factory info line) are placed where they really sit; Escape goes back to the container. Those container overlays are listed only when they were drawn in the last ten seconds - over their menu, or just after you closed it - and Show Unseen does not bring them back anywhere else. It does not open with an item held on the cursor. Health and Mana Bars' bars and texts are not in Edit HUD: **Edit Bars** (Home's quick buttons, beside Edit HUD, and on the Health and Mana Bars page) opens an editor with only them, every one that is switched on, where they move, resize and scale as described there, Predefined areas included
- HUD Scale — one slider, SkyHanni-style, that resizes everything the mod draws at once, from 5% to 300% in 5% steps (100% by default). Everything follows the slider live while you drag it, the menu it sits in included. On HUD elements it multiplies each element's own size rather than replacing it, so an element you set to 200% and then a HUD Scale of 50% draws at its original size, and goes back to 200% when the slider returns to 100%. It also sizes the mod's own menus and screens (this menu, its color pickers, the editors, browsers and the rest), the Bazaar and Auction House reskins, the center-screen popups and the DVD logos. Menus follow it between 50% and 300%, never grow past what fits the window (the whole menu stays on screen, centered, on a small window), and land on a whole number of screen pixels per GUI pixel when the setting is close to one, so the text stays crisp; clicks always land on what is drawn. The menu re-sizes when you let go of the slider. Not scaled by it: the HUD editors (they show elements at exactly the size they draw), the Custom Crosshair (its own size setting), and vanilla and Hypixel screens the mod does not reskin. Saved with profiles. Auto Scale (monitor) - the button under it, ON by default - also sizes everything the mod draws (every HUD element, the center-screen popups, and all of the mod's own menus: this menu, the AP3 and Auto Routes editors, the friends list, the AH and Bazaar browsers and the rest) to your window, so it all stays the same size relative to the screen on a 1080p, 1440p or 4K monitor and at any Minecraft GUI Scale. The reference is a 2560x1440 window at GUI Scale 3, where nothing changes at all; a 1920x1080 window at GUI Scale 3 draws at 75%, a 4K one at 150%, and the button shows the factor in use. The HUD Scale slider multiplies on top. HUD positions move with it, so a layout keeps its shape on every monitor and nothing is pushed off screen. The HUD editor itself is not scaled, so it always shows elements at exactly the size and place they draw in game. Saved with profiles
- The Home page (2026-10-15): the version line reads only "Version <mod version> · <cheat|legit>" (no Minecraft version, no build); eight quick buttons in a 4x2 grid (tools on top: Edit HUD, Edit Bars, Check Updates, Sweat Mode; links below: YouTube, Discord, GitHub, Features Doc; Reboot was removed, `/reboot` stays); the Menu Theme row ends with a Main Menu: Fancy / Simple chip that flips the main menu's scene; the Mod Settings cards are in a fixed order, not alphabetical: Mod & HUD, Click Sounds, Descriptions, Discord Rich Presence, Auto Update, Ban Reports (then the red Cheat block). Sound Effects was a Home card from 2026-10-18 to 2026-10-20 and is now in Misc.
- Discord Rich Presence's options sit directly on the Home tab under the Mod & HUD settings, with no dropdown to open
- The mod menu remembers your scroll position, selected tab, and search text between opens

**Profiles**
- The Profiles page shows the active profile at the top (with Import beside it), a New Profile card with the name box, Save Current As New and New Blank Profile, then a card per saved profile with an Active badge on the loaded one and Rename / Load / Export / Delete (Rename turns the name into a box right on the card: type, then Save or Enter; a name another profile has is refused, and renaming the active profile keeps it active; Delete asks for a second click). Loading a profile shows nothing on screen, only the log. Save your current settings as a named profile, switch between profiles, and export one to a single `.zip` file to share with a friend: Export puts the zip in the profiles' share folder and opens that folder, so it is ready to drag into Discord. New Blank Profile makes a profile with every setting off (named from the name box, or Blank), without changing your current settings until you Load it; it keeps your recorded routes, etherwarp waypoints, class overrides and HUD layout, and leaves Skyblock Only and the HUD editor's options at their defaults. To receive one, click Import Profile: it opens the import folder, and any `.zip` dropped in is imported within a couple of seconds as a new profile named after the file. A profile carries everything you set up, not only the settings: your GIFs, crosshair presets, the scoreboard background image, AP3 routes, Breaker Aura configs, inventory layouts, the cringe lines and the sim's speed and keys all travel with it, and loading a profile replaces them as a set (a GIF the profile does not have goes away; a GIF on the HUD switches straight away). A blank profile starts with none of them except your AP3 routes and Breaker Aura configs. Left out on purpose: credentials and keys (session login token, per-account proxies, the proxy password, the Last.fm login, the Profile Viewer API key, and the old built-in Hypixel key early versions wrote into the RNG Meter file - your own stays when you load a profile), downloads and caches (the room database and room library, island graphs, the speech model, Auction House and item caches, the video browser's browser profile, Auto Update downloads, the /reboot work folder, the egg spots seen, Storage Search's island chests and wardrobe pages, the Party Finder's stats), logs and bug reports, history (run history, Croesus and Experimentation logs, tracked players, Fairy Souls found, the Design a Map list, the sim's recent rooms and measured floor sizes) and your Bazaar orders as Hypixel last showed them. Loading a profile changes every setting at once, with no restart, Streamer Mode, Legit GFS, the cape and the sim's loadout and room filters included. An imported profile never changes your Auto Update channel or your sharing settings (sharing your build with other mod users, party dungeon data, Melody progress, the supporter name and trail): those keep the values you had when you imported it. Profiles move between versions: a profile made on 26.1.2 or 26.2, on the cheat, legit or testing build, or on an older version of the mod, imports and loads on any other - settings a build does not use (cheat settings on the legit build) are kept and come back when it returns to a build that uses them, settings it does not know take their defaults, and a damaged or newer-version file is reported instead of breaking the import. A profile from before profiles carried files only changes settings when loaded. While a profile is loaded, every setting and file you change is saved into that profile too, so switching away and back keeps your changes

## Requirements

- Minecraft `26.1.2`
- [Fabric Loader](https://fabricmc.net/use/) `>=0.19.3`
- [Fabric API](https://modrinth.com/mod/fabric-api)
- Java `25`+
